### [1] [Generalizable AI-Generated Image Detection with Embedding-Space Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04111)
*Nikos Giakoumoglou,Paschalis Giakoumoglou,Tania Stathaki,Symeon Papadopoulos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FOX通过先锁定专家级DINOv3检测器、再用其表征引导轻量VLM生成取证解释的两阶段设计，实现"解释训练不改变判定"，在14个未见生成器上达97.4平均AUC，XPlainVerse 69.8 BERTScore、LOKI 56.1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令驱动的AI图像编辑只做局部重合成，不留下拼接边界，难以定位；多数检测器是黑盒、结论不可验证；在检测分布上微调骨干会记忆快速过时的生成器指纹，泛化差。基于纯视觉表征（尤其DINOv3）的专家检测器跨生成器泛化好，但可解释方法放弃了这一优势——联合训练单一多模态模型做分类与语言生成会使视觉表征受语言目标牵制。因此作者提出硬性要求：解释训练不得改变判定。

Method: 两阶段解耦：①构建并冻结（锁定）专家级DINOv3检测器；②用其表征引导轻量VLM产出取证解释。具体以联合嵌入预测器将锁定的视觉证据映射到冻结文本编码器的语义空间，生成引导信号，VLM按需将其语言化。

Result: 14个未见生成器上平均AUC 97.4；XPlainVerse上BERTScore 69.8；LOKI上56.1。

Conclusion: 以"检测器锁定+表征引导解释"的架构可同时保持强跨生成器泛化与可验证的取证解释，且解释训练不影响判决，为可解释AI图像取证提供可行范式。

Abstract: AI-generated images have reached unprecedented realism, while instruction-driven AI editors re-synthesize images for local changes, leaving no splice boundary to localize. Most detectors are black boxes with unverifiable verdicts and generalize poorly because adapting a backbone on the detection distribution memorizes generator-specific fingerprints that quickly become obsolete. Specialist detectors built on pure-vision representations, particularly DINOv3, generalize well across generators, but explainable methods forgo this advantage: jointly training one multimodal model for classification and language generation subjects its visual representation to the language objective. We therefore require that explanation training cannot alter the verdict. We introduce FOX, which first builds and locks a specialist DINOv3 detector, then uses its representation to guide a lightweight VLM toward forensic explanation. A joint-embedding predictor maps locked visual evidence into a frozen text encoder's semantic space, producing guidance that the VLM verbalizes on demand. FOX achieves 97.4 mean AUC across 14 unseen generators, 69.8 BERTScore on XPlainVerse, and 56.1 on LOKI.

</details>


### [2] [SparseGraph-I2P: Consistency-Guided Hypothesis Selection for Efficient Image-to-Point-Cloud Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04591)
*Ziyang Yu,Xiang Li,Qiong Chang,Jun Miyazaki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SparseGraph-I2P通过结合外观与深度兼容性构建粗假设图，并采用稀疏精细匹配与深度感知过滤，提高位姿求解器的内点率，从而提升I2P配准召回率并降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粗到细的I2P配准存在两难：粗阶段可能丢弃正确但歧义的patch对，而保留更多候选又会在重叠patch上重复精细匹配，导致内点率低、召回受限与延迟增加。

Method: 提出SparseGraph-I2P。构建Coarse Hypothesis Graph，结合外观与深度导出的一阶、二阶兼容性，选取固定预算的patch对；采用Sparse Fine Matching，每个查询像素仅解码一次，并跳过被祖先patch与同一点云区域配对所覆盖的像素—点比较；传感器深度时以SC2谱支撑度对对应关系排序做单次EPnP求解；单目深度时先由对应几何标定深度或逆深度再过滤。

Result: 在RGB-D Scenes V2与7-Scenes（传感器深度）上取得所比较方法中最高的配准召回率和最低延迟；使用预测深度时，在RGB-D Scenes V2上保持最高召回，但需额外标定开销。

Conclusion: 通过在图假设选择与稀疏精细匹配层面提升内点率，SparseGraph-I2P在保证高召回的同时实现低延迟，并可扩展至单目深度输入。

Abstract: Coarse-to-fine image-to-point-cloud (I2P) registration risks discarding correct but ambiguous patch pairs, while retaining more candidates repeats fine matching over overlapping patches. SparseGraph-I2P aims to improve registration recall by increasing the inlier ratio at the pose solver, using sensor or monocular depth. A Coarse Hypothesis Graph combines appearance with depth-derived first- and second-order compatibility to select a fixed budget of patch pairs. Sparse Fine Matching decodes each queried pixel once and skips pixel--point comparisons covered by an ancestor patch paired with the same point-cloud region. With sensor depth, depth-aware filtering ranks correspondences by SC2 spectral support for a single EPnP solve. With monocular depth, depth or inverse depth is calibrated from correspondence geometry before filtering. SparseGraph-I2P achieves the highest registration recall and lowest latency among the compared methods on RGB-D Scenes V2 and 7-Scenes with sensor depth. With predicted depth, it retains the highest recall on RGB-D Scenes V2 at an additional calibration cost. Code will be released.

</details>


### [3] [VISTA: Vision-Language Integrated Semantic Triage Architecture for Online Face Clustering in Long Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04932)
*Jiashu Liao,Joanne Garde-Hansen,Sanjay Sharma,Tanaya Guha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VISTA 是首个面向长视频在线人脸聚类的视觉-语言框架，通过受控、考虑不确定性的语义推理，同时保持高聚类纯度与长程身份连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人脸聚类模型大多为离线方法，需要一次性获得全部数据；而实际场景中数据往往不可得或整体处理代价过高。少量已有的在线方法依赖几何视觉特征与简单阈值，在长时、无约束视频中面对显著外观变化时失效。

Method: 提出 VISTA 框架，利用视觉-语言模型进行语义推理，并以受控的、不确定性感知的方式维护聚类状态，从而在光照、姿态与服饰变化下保持鲁棒性。实验采用相同的检测器、嵌入与评测协议进行公平对比。

Result: 在 VPCD 基准数据集（15 集完整剧集，来自 The Big Bang Theory、Buffy the Vampire Slayer、Sherlock）上，VISTA 实现了稳健的精确率-召回率平衡，在同等设置下持续优于最强的在线无监督基线 COFC。

Conclusion: 视觉-语言语义推理结合不确定性感知机制，可有效解决长视频在线人脸聚类的身份连续性问题，优于仅依赖几何特征的在线方法。

Abstract: Clustering faces by identity in long, continuous video streams is a challenging task that serves as a building block for human-centric video analysis. The majority of existing face clustering models are offline, i.e., they assume the entire data to be available altogether. In many practical scenarios, however, the entire data is either unavailable or poses computational challenges to be processed in its entirety. A handful of online methods that exist rely on geometric visual features and simple thresholding techniques, which fail when the faces exhibit significant appearance variations in long, unconstrained videos. To this end, we present VISTA, the first vision language framework for online face clustering in long videos. VISTA leverages semantic reasoning in a controlled, uncertainty aware fashion to maintain both high cluster purity and long range identity continuity. This makes it robust to the variations in illumination, pose, and costume changes that the faces and characters exhibit in long real world videos. Extensive experiments on the VPCD benchmark dataset (with 15 full episodes from The Big Bang Theory [2007-2019], Buffy the Vampire Slayer [1997-2003], and Sherlock [2010-2017]) demonstrate that VISTA achieves a robust precision and recall balance, consistently improving over the strongest matched online unsupervised baseline (COFC) under identical detector, embeddings, and evaluation protocol. Code and supplementary material: https://github.com/SkyJiashu/VISTA.

</details>


### [4] [Physics-Informed Functional Tucker Method with RKHS Factors for Sparse Spatiotemporal Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03717)
*Zhengyang Liu,Jianguo Huang,Junxi Hu,Yucheng Mi,Haojie Cai,Wenxuan Shen,Zhikun Zhang,Renfeng Peng,Guangtao Zhang,Zhiqiang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出物理信息函数型Tucker模型PhysFTM，用RKHS参数化Tucker模态因子并施加PDE有限差分残差约束，实现稀疏固定传感器与缺失帧下的连续时空场重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅凭固定稀疏传感器与缺失帧重建物理场时，未观测区域缺乏约束，反问题病态：模型可拟合传感器却给出错误的离传感器结构或不可靠的帧间预测。

Method: 将Tucker模态因子在再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）中函数化参数化；在离传感器配点上以有限差分残差形式引入PDE约束，为无观测监督区域提供物理信息；支撑帧上用交替最小化分别优化Tucker核心张量与RKHS因子重构连续空间场；再在Tucker核心空间构建连续轨迹，由学习到的空间RKHS-FTM表示解码，恢复完整时空场以补全缺失帧。

Result: 在Allen–Cahn、Navier–Stokes、三维热方程及三维Copernicus海洋再分析网格数据上，于两种缺失帧协议下均实现准确的2D/3D重构。

Conclusion: 将RKHS函数化张量分解与PDE残差约束结合，可有效缓解稀疏观测下反问题的不适定性，并借核心空间轨迹插值实现缺失帧时空场重建；代码已开源。

Abstract: Reconstructing physical dynamics from fixed sparse sensors and missing frames is a fundamental challenge in scientific research. Sparsity leaves large regions of the field unconstrained, making the inverse problem ill-posed: a model may fit the observed sensors while producing inaccurate off-sensor structures or unreliable predictions between support frames. To this end, we propose PhysFTM---a physics-informed functional Tucker model that parameterizes the Tucker mode factors in reproducing kernel Hilbert spaces (RKHSs) and incorporates PDE constraints through finite-difference residuals evaluated at off-sensor collocation points. These residuals provide additional physical information in regions without direct observational supervision. PhysFTM first reconstructs continuous spatial fields at support frames via an alternating minimization scheme, optimizing the Tucker core tensor and the RKHS-based factors separately. To enable missing-frame reconstruction, PhysFTM constructs a continuous trajectory in Tucker-core space, which is decoded by the learned spatial RKHS-FTM representation to recover the full spatiotemporal field. Experiments on Allen--Cahn, Navier--Stokes, three-dimensional Heat equations, and a three-dimensional gridded Copernicus ocean reanalysis demonstrate accurate 2D/3D reconstruction under two missing-frame protocols. Code is available at https://github.com/liusirzzz/PhysFTM.

</details>


### [5] [Beyond Proposals: Region-Consistent Open-Set Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03741)
*Jiehui Tang,Yunlong Yu,Zhao Wang,Jungong Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将开集目标检测（OSOD）重构为以对象为中心的语义推理问题，提出OCSI-Net，通过聚合相关候选框进行区域级语义一致性决策，并引入几何感知不确定性机制稳定开集边界优化，在多个基准上平均提升6.83%的调和均值指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在密集候选机制下，同一目标对应的高度重叠候选框常被赋予相互矛盾的已知/未知预测。现有方法对每个候选框独立进行开集推理，忽略相关候选框之间的共享语义，导致检测碎片化和开集决策不稳定。

Method: 提出OCSI-Net统一框架：(1) 以对象为中心的语义一致性——将相关候选框聚合为结构化区域簇，从聚合的语义证据推断区域级语义，从而在同一对象区域内强制一致的开集预测；(2) 几何感知不确定性机制——用于稳定开集过渡边界附近的优化过程。

Result: 在多个基准上的大量实验表明，OCSI-Net持续取得最先进性能，已知与未知检测调和均值相较次优方法平均提升6.83%。

Conclusion: 将OSOD建模为区域级、对象中心的联合语义推理并结合几何不确定性稳定，可有效缓解候选框级预测矛盾，提升开集检测的一致性与整体性能。

Abstract: Open-set object detection (OSOD) suffers from a fundamental limitation under dense proposal mechanisms: highly overlapping proposals associated with the same object are often assigned contradictory known/unknown predictions. Existing methods perform open-set reasoning independently for each proposal, while ignoring the shared semantics among correlated proposals, resulting in fragmented detections and unstable open-set decisions. In this paper, we reformulate OSOD as an object-centric semantic inference problem, where correlated proposals jointly support a coherent region-level semantic decision. Based on this perspective, we propose OCSI-Net, a unified framework that combines object-centric semantic consistency with geometry-aware uncertainty stabilization. Specifically, OCSI-Net aggregates correlated proposals into structured region clusters and infers region-level semantics from aggregated semantic evidence, thereby enforcing coherent open-set predictions within the same object region. Furthermore, a geometry-aware uncertainty mechanism is introduced to stabilize optimization near open-set transition boundaries. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that OCSI-Net consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance, improving the harmonic mean of known and unknown detection by an average of 6.83\% over the second-best method.

</details>


### [6] [FlowRig: Unified Skeleton Generation for 3D and 4D Meshes via Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03775)
*Fengzhi Guo,Nate Morrill,Shiqi Li,Cheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoTWorld 是一个基于 flow matching 的统一 3D/4D 骨骼生成框架，单次前馈即可为整段序列生成时间一致的骨架，并支持骨骼数控制、不确定性估计与掩码式插值/预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有静态 3D rigging 方法质量高但骨骼数可控性弱，逐帧用于动态网格时不具备时间稳定性；4D 方法多依赖昂贵的逐序列优化。二者缺乏统一的前馈方案，且配对 4D rigging 数据稀缺。

Method: 提出 MoTWorld：模板无关、数据高效，基于 flow matching 对整个序列一次性生成时间对齐骨架，同时自然兼容单网格 3D rigging；支持显式骨骼数控制、不确定性估计用于候选选择，以及基于 masking 的插值、forecast 与 backcasting；训练采用 curriculum，从大规模 3D rigging 数据初始化再适配中等规模 4D 网格-骨架数据。

Result: 实验显示 MoTWorld 在静态 3D rigging 上取得强性能且骨骼复杂度可控，并在 4D 重建与预测上获得高质量、时间一致的结果。

Conclusion: MoTWorld 以统一前馈框架同时覆盖 3D 与 4D 骨架生成，兼顾时间一致性与可控性，并通过 3D→4D 课程式训练缓解 4D 配对数据不足，为统一 rigging 提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Automatic rigging is a fundamental task for both static 3D and dynamic 4D mesh animation, yet existing methods do not provide a unified feedforward solution for these two settings. While static 3D riggers achieve strong quality, they often provide limited control over bone count and become temporally unstable when applied frame by frame to dynamic meshes. Existing 4D methods, in contrast, typically rely on expensive per-sequence optimization. We propose MoTWorld, a template-free, data-efficient framework for unified 3D and 4D skeleton generation. Built on flow matching, MoTWorld generates temporally aligned skeletons for an entire sequence in a single feedforward pass, while naturally handling single-mesh 3D rigging. The model further supports explicit skeleton-count control, uncertainty estimation for candidate selection, and masking-based interpolation, forecasting, and backcasting. To address limited paired 4D rigging data, we adopt a curriculum that initializes from large-scale 3D rigging data and adapts to mid-scale 4D mesh-skeleton data. Experiments show that MoTWorld achieves strong static 3D rigging performance with controllable bone complexity, as well as high-quality and temporally consistent 4D reconstruction and prediction results.

</details>


### [7] [Geometric Refinement and Distributional Alignment for Sim2Real Roadside LiDAR Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03936)
*Aman Upganlawar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文报告 UrbanTwin Sim2Real LiDAR 挑战赛 LUMPI 赛道的参赛方案：仅用合成数据训练 3D 检测器并评估于未见真实路侧 LiDAR，最终综合得分 0.4793（检测 mAP 0.1333、真实性 0.9126），贡献不在新架构而在于诊断驱动的工程改进与实验方法论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 Sim2Real 路侧 LiDAR 场景下，缺乏真实标注时只能依赖合成数据训练；同时需要兼顾检测性能与合成点云的分布真实性。作者希望厘清性能瓶颈的真实来源，并提出一套可迁移、可预测的竞赛实验方法。

Method: 基于 PointPillars 基线，未改架构。三项主要改进：(1) 诊断出小目标失败属于定位而非检测问题，引入无参数几何精化；(2) 审计自身后处理，发现一条已发布规则在破坏有效预测并予以修复；(3) 解析化分析真实性指标，证明四分量之一为九个汇总统计量的闭式函数。方法论上利用 mAP 按类别可分解性，在单次提交中嵌套多个受控实验，并由已测量组件预先推断总分。

Result: 组合得分 0.4793，检测 mAP 0.1333，真实性 0.9126。几何精化带来行人 AP 相对提升 86%；后处理修复带来 Van 类相对提升 335%，为最大单项检测增益；提交前对最终得分的预测精确到四位小数。同时等篇幅报告负结果，并在第 8 节完整交代数据来源、合规性及所有影响得分的机制。

Conclusion: 在 Sim2Real 路侧 LiDAR 检测中，细致的失败诊断与对既有后处理的审计往往比架构创新更具性价比；将指标解析化并利用 mAP 的类别可分解性，可实现组件化、可控且可事前预测的提交策略，这一实验方法论被认为是更具可迁移价值的成果。

Abstract: We describe our approach to the LUMPI track of the UrbanTwin Sim2Real LiDAR Challenge, in which a 3D object detector must be trained exclusively on synthetic data and evaluated on unseen real roadside LiDAR, while the synthetic point clouds are simultaneously scored for distributional realism. Our final entry scores 0.4793 combined, from a detection mAP of 0.1333 and a realism score of 0.9126. Our contribution is not a new architecture. Working from a PointPillars baseline, we obtained most of our gains from three sources. First, a diagnosis that small object failure in roadside LiDAR is a localisation problem rather than a detection problem, which led to a parameter free geometric refinement worth 86 percent relative on pedestrian AP. Second, an audit of our own post processing which revealed that a shipped rule was destroying valid predictions, the repair of which produced our single largest detection gain at 335 percent relative on the Van class. Third, treating the realism metrics analytically rather than as a black box, which showed that one of the four components is a closed form function of nine summary statistics. We also report an experimental methodology that we believe is the more transferable result. Because mean AP decomposes into independent per class terms, a single submission can carry several mutually controlled experiments, and a submission assembled entirely from previously measured components has a score that is known before it is sent. We predicted our final score to four decimal places prior to submission. We report negative results at equal length, and give a full account of data provenance and compliance in Section 8, including a full account of every mechanism contributing to the submitted score.

</details>


### [8] [FlowBench: Counterfactual Imagination Training for Spatial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03937)
*Yifan Liu,Baihan Zhang,Khai Loong Aw,Klemen Kotar,Daniel L. K. Yamins*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出"反事实空间想象"（CSI）任务并构建FlowBench资源（428K训练样本），用于训练与评估VLM与世界模型对假设运动下场景变化结果的推断能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类具备在假设运动下想象场景变化的能力，这对空间推理与机器人规划至关重要，但VLM与世界模型在该能力上研究不足，且缺乏统一的训练与评测资源。

Method: 将能力形式化为CSI；构建FlowBench，覆盖仿真与真实场景、家庭环境与桌面操作、多样题型；采用可扩展的全自动流水线，将每个假设运动与空间问题配对，并在仿真中给出精确的结果帧与光流作为监督。

Result: 闭源VLM存在"想象差距"（推断运动结果时劣于直接观察），开源模型接近随机；Qwen3-VL-8B在FlowBench-Sim-Train上微调后仿真提升29.5个百分点、真实图像提升16.9，sim-to-real迁移良好；四类世界模型的text-to-flow预测均有提升，Psi-0.6由52.1%升至61.7%。

Conclusion: FlowBench可作为CSI能力的统一训练与评测基准，代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: Humans imagine scene changes under hypothetical motions, an ability central to spatial reasoning and robotic planning. However, this capability remains underexplored in vision-language models and world models. We formalize this capability as Counterfactual Spatial Imagination (CSI) and introduce FlowBench, a resource for training and evaluating both, with 428K training examples spanning simulated and real-world scenes, household environments and tabletop manipulation, and diverse question types. A scalable, fully automatic pipeline pairs each hypothetical motion with spatial questions and, in simulation, exact outcome frames and optical flow. For VLMs, proprietary models exhibit an imagination gap, performing worse when motion outcomes must be inferred than when observed, and open-source models perform near chance; fine-tuning Qwen3-VL-8B on FlowBench-Sim-Train raises its accuracy by 29.5 percentage points in simulation and 16.9 on real images, a strong sim-to-real transfer. For world models, training on FlowBench-Sim improves text-to-flow prediction for all four model families we test, raising the accuracy of Psi-0.6 from 52.1% to 61.7%. FlowBench thus serves as a training and evaluation resource for both. Code and datasets are released at https://github.com/liuyifan22/FlowBench.

</details>


### [9] [Coordinate Frame Recovery, Per Class NMS and Null Return Modelling for Sim2Real V2X LiDAR Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03940)
*Aman Upganlawar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文报告 UrbanTwin Sim2Real LiDAR Challenge 中 V2X-Real 赛道的参赛方案：仅用合成数据训练 3D 检测器，在真实路侧 Ouster OS1-64 点云上评估，同时考察合成点云的分布真实性；最终综合得分 0.7457（检测 mAP 0.4382，真实性 0.9251），提升主要来自三项诊断性发现而非架构改动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成到真实（Sim2Real）路侧 LiDAR 检测存在显著的域差异与评测细节陷阱，作者希望厘清哪些因素真正决定最终得分，并如实报告诊断过程与负结果。

Method: 仅使用合成点云训练 3D 目标检测器；通过诊断性排查修正三类问题：（1）预测与评测真值之间的坐标系不匹配；（2）全局统一 NMS 阈值（IoU 0.3）抑制了相邻行人，改为按类别设置阈值；（3）在合成点云中建模真实数据中约 31% 以字面点 (0,0,0) 编码的空回波。

Result: 修正坐标系后得分由 0.3520 升至 0.5147；按类别 NMS 使 mAP 由 0.0751 升至 0.4295；建模空回波后 EMD 摆脱接近下限的状态。最终检测 mAP 0.4382、真实性 0.9251、综合 0.7457。

Conclusion: Sim2Real 竞赛成绩的主要瓶颈来自坐标系、NMS 与数据格式等评测/预处理层面的细节，而非模型架构；论文同时以同等篇幅报告负结果，并在第 9 节完整说明数据来源与合规性，逐项归因得分来源。

Abstract: We describe our approach to the V2X-Real track of the UrbanTwin Sim2Real LiDAR Challenge, in which a 3D object detector must be trained exclusively on synthetic data and evaluated on unseen real roadside LiDAR from an Ouster OS1-64 unit, while the synthetic point clouds are simultaneously scored for distributional realism. Our final entry scores 0.7457 combined, from a detection mAP of 0.4382 and a realism score of 0.9251. Three findings account for most of our result, and all three were diagnostic rather than architectural. First, a coordinate frame mismatch between our predictions and the evaluation ground truth was suppressing detection AP entirely; recovering it took our score from 0.3520 to 0.5147 in a single submission. Second, a single global non maximum suppression threshold was destroying genuine pedestrian detections, because pedestrians occur in groups and a 0.3 IoU threshold applied to a 0.7 m box suppresses distinct neighbouring people; correcting this per class raised overall mAP from 0.0751 to 0.4295. Third, approximately 31 percent of the real reference point cloud consists of null returns encoded as the literal point (0, 0, 0), a format feature entirely absent from our synthetic clouds, which pinned Earth Mover's Distance near its floor until we modelled it. Negative results are reported at equal length, and Section 9 gives a full account of data provenance and compliance. That section also accounts for every mechanism contributing to the submitted score.

</details>


### [10] [Camera Radar LiDAR Fusion via Point-Cloud Projection for Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03942)
*Kavin Chandrasekaran,Sorin Grigorescu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种早期融合架构，将相机图像与HD雷达、LiDAR的透视视图点云结合，同时完成车辆检测与可行驶空间分割，在RADIal数据集上以更低计算成本超越现有单模态与融合模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶需在多样环境下安全运行：相机分辨率高但低光照与恶劣天气下性能显著退化；HD雷达可靠且角分辨率高但缺乏丰富视觉特征；LiDAR昂贵但测距精确、点云稠密。各传感器存在独特权衡，因此需设计先进融合算法以发挥互补优势，同时控制计算复杂度并保持精度。

Method: 采用早期融合（early fusion）策略，将相机图像与来自HD雷达和LiDAR的透视视图（perspective-view）点云数据在输入层直接结合；单一模型以多任务方式联合训练，同时执行车辆检测与自由可行驶空间分割。

Result: 在RADIal数据集上的实验表明，将点云数据与相机图像在前视图（front-view）中融合，其性能超过了最新的独立模型与融合模型。

Conclusion: 在前视图进行点云—图像早期融合是一种有效且相对轻量的多传感器融合方案，可在保持精度的同时发挥相机与雷达/LiDAR的互补性，适用于自动驾驶感知中的检测与分割联合任务。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles require robust perception systems to operate safely under diverse environmental conditions. While cameras provide high-resolution visual data, their performance significantly degrades in low-light and adverse weather. High Definition (HD) radar offers superior reliability and angular resolution under such conditions but lacks rich visual feature recognition. LiDAR, though expensive, delivers highly precise distance measurements and dense point clouds. Each sensor exhibits unique trade-offs, necessitating the development of advanced sensor fusion algorithms to harness complementary strengths while minimizing computational complexity and maintaining accuracy. In this study, the proposed fusion architecture performs early fusion by combining camera images with perspective-view point cloud data from HD radar and LiDAR sensors. The proposed model is trained to detect vehicles and segment free drivable space simultaneously. Experiments are performed on the RADIal dataset where we demonstrate that fusing point cloud data with camera images in front-view surpasses the performances of the most recent standalone and fusion models.

</details>


### [11] [Do We Still Need Rotation-Invariant Architectures? A Statistical Robustness Benchmark for Point Cloud Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03952)
*Abdulla Nasir Chowdhury,Gulpi Qorik Oktagalu Pratamasunu,Kazuhiro Fukui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对10种点云架构在5个域上跨姿态、噪声、遮挡的鲁棒性进行基准测试，并用确认性假设检验分析；发现旋转不变性带来的噪声鲁棒性代价取决于获取不变性的方式，可通过简单的不变性损失训练与更宽的旋转增强加以缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量工作将旋转不变性直接内置于点云架构中，但缺乏对这些架构在多种扰动下鲁棒性代价的系统、统计严谨的对比，尤其不清楚"不变性导致噪声鲁棒性下降"是本质属性还是实现方式所致。

Method: 基准测试10种架构（PointNet、PointNet++、DGCNN、RIConv、RIConv++、RISurConv、PointMLP、PointTransformer，以及等变网络VN-PointNet、VN-DGCNN），覆盖5个域的姿态、噪声、遮挡扰动，采用确认性假设检验而非点估计进行分析；随后在嵌入上加入简单的不变性损失、用三个训练种子重新训练全部10种架构，并在独立设计的ModelNet40-C上评估；另考察放宽旋转增强后的表现。

Result: 三种旋转不变（RI）架构姿态鲁棒性最佳，但在5个域中有4个域噪声鲁棒性排名最低。不变性训练迁移范围窄但可靠：在其扰动目标对应的损坏族上均有提升（遮挡平均+10.5、lidar +13.4个百分点）。在相同训练预算下，最佳标准骨干与最佳RI架构在未见损坏上表现相当（每个种子差异在2.5个百分点内），且经不变性训练的RIConv++是种子最稳定的顶级模型。放宽旋转增强后，标准骨干在全球面旋转下仅承受一个平坦的、与角度无关的精度损失。

Conclusion: 旋转不变性的噪声鲁棒性代价并非本质固有，而与其获取方式相关；不变性损失训练和更宽的旋转增强可有效缩小差距。作者发布基准及用于分析点云鲁棒性、复现结果的软件包。

Abstract: A substantial line of work builds rotation invariance directly into point cloud architectures. We benchmark 10 architectures (PointNet, PointNet++, DGCNN, RIConv, RIConv++, RISurConv, PointMLP, PointTransformer, and the equivariant VN-PointNet and VN-DGCNN) across 5 domains under pose, noise, and occlusion, and analyze the results with confirmatory hypothesis tests rather than point estimates. The three rotation-invariant (RI) architectures achieve the best pose robustness but rank among the lowest on noise robustness in 4 of 5 domains. We ask whether this cost is intrinsic to invariance or specific to how invariance is obtained. We retrain all 10 architectures unchanged, with a simple invariance loss on the embedding and three training seeds each, and evaluate all models on the independently designed ModelNet40-C. Invariance training transfers narrowly but reliably. It improves exactly the corruption families its perturbations target wherever the baseline has headroom (mean occlusion +10.5, lidar +13.4 percentage points of accuracy). At matched training budget the best standard backbone and the best RI architecture end on par on unseen corruptions (within 2.5 percentage points in every seed), and invariance-trained RIConv++ is the most seed-stable top model. Once rotation augmentation is widened, standard backbones concede only a flat, angle-independent accuracy offset under full-sphere rotation. We release the benchmark along with a package for analyzing point cloud robustness and reproducing our results.

</details>


### [12] [SurgEye: Instruction Tuning-Free Perception-Reasoning Framework for Surgical Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04012)
*Qing Xu,Xiechen Shen,Xianjian He,Guqiao Liang,Zixiao Chai,Jiale Qin,Yifan Chen,Siyi Li,Tian Yuan,Yiwei Li,Ziheng Meng,Zhen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SurgEye：不做指令微调，以视觉基础模型+冻结LLM的智能体式组合推理实现手术场景理解，并构建可验证评测基准SurgEye-Bench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术场景理解需要跨器械、解剖与工作流的组合式推理，而现有手术视觉-语言模型依赖大规模问答对指令微调，数据整理繁重且重训成本高。

Method: 1) Routed Multi-Task Perception (RMP)：利用公开感知标签学习任务专属通路，将视觉基础模型路由至不同子任务，输出空间与时间证据；2) Agentic Compositional Reasoning (ACR)：以冻结LLM作为协调器，把RMP各专家与时间记忆作为可调用工具，跨任务、跨时间组合证据形成有据可依的答案；3) 构建SurgEye-Bench，覆盖八类手术，每题配对感知标签以支持可验证评测。

Result: 大量实验表明SurgEye在手术推理任务上优于现有最先进方法；基准支持基于感知标签的可验证评测。源码将公开。

Conclusion: 以感知标签驱动的路由多任务感知加冻结LLM智能体组合，可替代指令微调范式，实现免重训、可验证的手术场景推理。

Abstract: Surgical scene understanding is pivotal for computer-assisted intervention, yet demands compositional reasoning across instruments, anatomy, and workflow. Existing surgical vision-language models rely on instruction tuning with large-scale question-answer pairs, entailing laborious data curation and costly retraining. To bridge this gap, we propose SurgEye, a perception-reasoning framework that grounds reasoning in a vision foundation model without instruction tuning. Specifically, we devise Routed Multi-Task Perception (RMP) that routes the vision foundation model through task-specific pathways learned from public perception labels, producing spatial and temporal evidence. We further introduce Agentic Compositional Reasoning (ACR) that employs a frozen large language model to coordinate RMP specialists and temporal memory as callable tools, composing evidence across tasks and time into grounded answers. Moreover, we construct SurgEye-Bench spanning eight surgical procedures, pairing each question with perception labels for verifiable evaluation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SurgEye outperforms state-of-the-arts in surgical reasoning. The source code will be available.

</details>


### [13] [Compression Forcing: Hierarchical History Tokenization Enables Flexible World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04028)
*Siqiao Huang,Partha Kaushik,Michael Chen,Hengkai Pan,Kaiwen Geng,Omar Chehab,Fernando Moreno-Pino,Max Simchowitz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Compression Forcing (CF) 框架，通过面向预测目标的历史压缩（Dynamics-Aware Tokenization 与 Hierarchical History Guidance），提升世界模型在视觉干扰环境下的预测保真度与决策性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型沿用视频生成的 tokenization 与条件机制，其目标围绕感知真实感而非预测效用，导致在有视觉干扰或任务无关背景的环境下效果受限；因此需要让历史压缩与预测目标对齐。

Method: 提出通用框架 Compression Forcing (CF)，包含：(1) Dynamics-Aware Tokenization (DAT)，将历史观测压缩为按动态显著性递减排列的 token 序列；(2) 基于扩散骨干网络的 Hierarchical History Guidance (HG-H)，在推理阶段选择性地放大动态显著信息。

Result: 在多种环境中实验表明，预测保真度、物理真实感及下游决策性能均有提升。

Conclusion: Compression Forcing 是一种灵活且有效的世界建模方法，通过将历史压缩与预测目标对齐，克服了沿袭视频生成范式的局限。

Abstract: Effective world modeling requires compressing salient history information that facilitates future prediction. However, most existing approaches inherit tokenization and conditioning mechanisms from video generation, where objectives are centered around perceptual realism rather than predictive utility. This discrepancy limits the world model's effectiveness, especially in environments dominated by visual nuisances or task-irrelevant background details. To this end, we introduce Compression Forcing (CF), a general framework that aligns history compression with the predictive objective of world modeling. Compression Forcing features Dynamics-Aware Tokenization (DAT), a novel tokenization method that compresses history observations into a sequence ordered by decreasing dynamics saliency. Built on a diffusion backbone, this structure enables Hierarchical History Guidance (HG-H), allowing selective amplification of dynamics-salient information during inference. Extensive experiments across various environments demonstrate improved prediction fidelity, physical realism, and downstream decision-making performance, establishing Compression Forcing as a highly flexible and effective approach for world modeling.

</details>


### [14] [Do Pretrained Backbones Transfer to Physical Sensors? A Systematic Study of Multimodal Initialization Across the Sensor Stack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04034)
*Fabio Montello,Ronja Güldenring,Lucas Mogensen Sandby,Christoffer Koo Øhrstrøm,Lazaros Nalpantidis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了包含2.82M图像、16个公开数据集的自动驾驶多传感器数据集，在MultiMAE基础上做域内预训练并改进其损失以适配模态稀疏，系统比较ViT、MAE、DINO、MultiMAE的迁移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多传感器视觉中预训练权重迁移能力与域内预训练可行性尚不明确；现有MultiMAE依赖密集的模型生成模态，难以适配真实物理传感器下公开数据聚合带来的模态稀疏问题。

Method: 整理16个公开数据集共2.82M图像的自动驾驶多传感器集合（RGB、LWIR、NIR、Event及立体深度、分割）；基于MultiMAE进行域内预训练，修改损失公式以支持稀疏样本，并研究编码器瓶颈对模态对齐的作用以及SSIM、FFT等密集重建辅助损失的影响。

Result: MultiMAE是出乎意料的强基线，甚至能有效迁移到未见模态；针对性修改在多传感器迁移与整体鲁棒性上取得增量但一致的提升。

Conclusion: 真实传感器场景下域内多传感器预训练可行，稀疏感知的损失设计与重建辅助损失带来稳定收益；MultiMAE可作为强即用基线。

Abstract: Transfer learning from pretrained backbones has become standard practice in multi-sensor computer vision---yet two questions remain open: which pretrained weights transfer best to multi-sensor setups, and can in-domain pretraining outperform off-the-shelf solutions? Frameworks like MultiMAE demonstrate the utility of masked autoencoding in a multi-modal context, but rely entirely on dense, model-derived modalities. It is unclear how robustly these methods translate to real-world applications that use physical sensors, where the aggregation of diverse public datasets for large-scale pretraining inherently introduces modality sparsity. To investigate this systematically, we curate a multi-sensor autonomous driving collection of $2.82M$ images from 16 publicly available datasets, incorporating both physical hardware modalities (RGB, LWIR, NIR, Event) and model-derived modalities (Stereo Depth, Segmentation). We use this collection for in-domain pretraining based on the MultiMAE approach. We adapt MultiMAE's loss formulation to accommodate sparse samples, and study the effect of an enforced encoder bottleneck for modality alignment and of additional losses specific to dense reconstruction (SSIM and FFT). We then systematically compare against common pretrained backbones (ViT, MAE, DINO and MultiMAE) across downstream sensor fusion tasks. We find that MultiMAE is a surprisingly strong out-of-the-box baseline, transferring effectively even to modalities it has never seen, while our targeted modifications yield incremental but consistent improvements on multi-sensor transfer and overall robustness.

</details>


### [15] [Spatial and Temporal Super-Resolution and Denoising of Intracardiac 4D Flow MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04053)
*Yusuf A. Ozturk,Jie Zheng,Fanwei Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出4D-MRAR深度学习方法，对4D Flow MRI进行时空超分辨与去噪，可在心脏大变形条件下预测心内血流的速度场与压力场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心内血流由复杂时变心脏运动与瓣膜交互驱动，蕴含疾病诊断与治疗规划信息；4D Flow MRI虽能无创测量三维心血管速度场，但噪声大、时空分辨率有限，难以准确刻画血流特征。

Method: 方法融合残差卷积网络、循环神经网络与隐式神经场，实现3D空间与时间同步超分辨；训练中通过嵌入流体控制方程约束，联合预测高分辨率速度场与压力场。仅在左心室血流CFD合成数据上训练。

Result: 在合成全心4D Flow MRI数据上具备泛化能力，并在真实4D Flow MRI数据上表现良好；相比已有方法，超分辨血流场精度显著提升，且与控制方程一致性更强。

Conclusion: 4D-MRAR突破了以往仅在静态血管区域训练的局限，可处理大形变心脏内的血流重建，为4D Flow MRI的高保真血流场估计提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Intracardiac flow is driven by complex, and time-varying cardiac motion and interaction with the heart valves, producing complex flow features that may provide valuable information for disease diagnosis and treatment planning. 4D Flow MRI enables non-invasive, time-resolved measurement of three-dimensional cardiovascular velocity fields. However, substantial noise and limited spatial and temporal resolution limit its ability to accurately resolve flow features. We propose 4D-MRAR (4D Magnetic Resonance Augmented Reconstruction), a deep learning (DL) method to super-resolve and denoise 4D Flow MRI data. Unlike prior DL methods that train and infer on static vascular regions, our method predicts intracardiac flow within hearts undergoing large deformation. Moreover, it uniquely allows for simultaneous super-resolution in 3D space and time. The method combines a residual convolutional neural network, a recurrent neural network, and an implicit neural field to predict high-resolution velocity and pressure fields while efficiently enforcing the governing equations of fluid flow during training. Trained on a synthetic dataset obtained from computational fluid dynamics (CFD) simulations of left ventricle (LV) flow, our method generalizes to synthetic whole-heart 4D flow MRI data, and demonstrated promising performance on real 4D Flow MRI. The produced super-resolved flow fields exhibit significantly improved accuracy and greater consistency with the governing equations of blood flow compared with prior methods.

</details>


### [16] [From Visual Matching to Textual Conditioning: Cross-Domain Few-Shot Segmentation via Reference Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04084)
*Jiahao Nie,Yun Xing,Wenbin An,Qixin Zhang,Yap-Peng Tan,Alex C. Kot,Shijian Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RI-SAM3，在冻结SAM3的前提下通过双层参考反演学习类别文本token，实现免逐样本视觉匹配的跨域小样本分割，仅约12K参数且单次前向即可完成分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAM类CD-FSS方法依赖参考-目标视觉匹配，对目标外观变化高度敏感；SAM3虽支持文本条件分割，但直接用类别名作条件在严重域偏移下性能不稳定。

Method: 三项核心设计：(i) 双层参考反演，在冻结文本编码器前后分别学习互补token，前者锚定SAM3文本空间、后者更灵活地表征类别；(ii) 层自适应文本条件，随解码器各层object query演化以不同方式组合这些token；(iii) 存在性感知置信监督，联合监督query置信度与目标存在性。

Result: 在多个广泛采用的基准上取得一致更优的CD-FSS性能，并具备较强鲁棒性、效率与泛化能力；SAM3保持冻结，每类别仅优化约12K参数。

Conclusion: RI-SAM3借助大规模视觉-语言预训练反演出可复用的类别文本条件，替代逐样本参考-目标视觉匹配，在单次前向中高效完成跨域小样本分割。

Abstract: Cross-Domain Few-Shot Segmentation (CD-FSS) aims to segment objects from unseen domains using only a few annotated references. Recent Segment Anything Model (SAM)-based methods have advanced CD-FSS, yet their reference-target visual matching is highly sensitive to target appearance variations. SAM3 introduces text-conditioned segmentation, enabling natural-language descriptions to guide segmentation across target images, but directly using the category name as the condition still yields unstable performance under severe domain shifts. We propose Reference Inversion for SAM3 (RI-SAM3) that leverages SAM3's large-scale vision-language pre-training to learn suitable textual tokens for each target category. RI-SAM3 comprises three core designs: (i) Bi-level Reference Inversion that learns complementary tokens before and after the frozen text encoder: the former remain anchored in SAM3's text space while the latter represent the category more flexibly; (ii) Layer-adaptive Textual Conditioning combines these tokens differently as object queries evolve across decoder layers; and (iii) Presence-aware Confidence Supervision that jointly supervises query confidence and target presence for reliable predictions. With SAM3 frozen, RI-SAM3 optimizes around 12K parameters only per category and it efficiently segments each target in a single forward pass with reusable textual conditioning without per-sample reference-target visual matching. Extensive experiments over multiple widely adopted benchmarks show that RI-SAM3 achieves superior performance consistently in CD-FSS tasks, together with strong robustness, efficiency, and generalization.

</details>


### [17] [ScienceVision: Can AI Observe and Reason Like a Scientist?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04087)
*Tianyi Ma,Jing Gu,Haozhe Zhao,Liang Chen,Yunfan Zhang,Haijun Su,Jialong Wu,Le Xue,Arnav Das,Dustin Tran,Yinfei Yang,Andrew Dai,Kuan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出跨领域科学3D理解与推理基准 ScienceVision，含5学科250道专家级VQA题，对比12个模型在原生3D工件（含/不含MCP检查工具）与渲染2D图像三种设定下的表现，最佳平均得分仅0.53。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现依赖对3D数据的准确观察与领域推理（如蛋白接触、解剖组织），但现有多模态基准多集中于文本与2D图像，科学3D评测在表示形式与协议上跨学科不一致，难以进行跨领域可比评估，因此需要统一的3D理解与推理基准。

Method: 构建 ScienceVision 基准：从生物、化学、医学影像、机械CAD、建筑各取50题共250题，采用合成问题生成+领域专家审核标注的流水线；每题配对原生3D工件、科学问题、指定回答格式与任务特定参考答案。在相同问题上评测12个模型，设三种设定：①通过agent harness的文件与代码执行工具访问原生3D工件；②在此基础上面向MCP暴露专家式查看器检查与测量工具；③基于规定渲染的2D图像作答。全部采用任务特定的确定性最终答案打分；检查环境支持视角变换、目标隔离与切片查看。

Result: 主要对比中最佳平均答案得分为0.53（满分1）；在每个模型上，原生3D工件工作流（不加MCP工具）的平均分均高于渲染图像工作流，平均高出17.2个百分点。

Conclusion: 现有模型在科学3D理解与推理上仍有显著不足（最高仅约0.53）；原生3D工件访问明显优于仅凭渲染2D图像，说明表示形式与交互工具对3D科学推理至关重要。

Abstract: Scientific discovery builds on accurate observations interpreted through domain knowledge and reasoning. For scientific 3D data, this requires understanding structures and spatial relationships, from protein contacts to anatomical organization. Existing multimodal benchmarks largely evaluate text and 2D images, while scientific 3D evaluations vary in representations and protocols across disciplines, limiting cross-disciplinary comparison. To assess these foundational capabilities, we introduce ScienceVision, a cross-domain benchmark for scientific 3D understanding and reasoning through expert-level visual question answering (VQA). We construct 250 questions, with 50 each from Biology, Chemistry, Medical Imaging, Mechanical CAD, and Architecture, through a pipeline combining synthetic question generation with domain-expert review and annotation. Each task pairs a native 3D artifact with a scientific question, a specified response format, and a task-specific reference answer, requiring domain knowledge to be applied to geometric or topological evidence. We evaluate 12 models on the same questions in three settings: visual question answering with native 3D artifacts accessed through an agent harness's file and code-execution tools; the same workflow augmented with expert-inspired viewer inspection and measurement tools exposed through the Model Context Protocol (MCP); and visual question answering from prescribed rendered 2D images using an agent harness. All settings use the same task-specific deterministic final-answer scoring. The inspection environment supports view changes, target isolation, and slice inspection. In this main comparison, the best mean answer score is 0.53 out of 1. Mean scores in the native-artifact workflow without additional MCP tools exceed those in the rendered-image workflow for every model, by 17.2 percentage points on average.

</details>


### [18] [CHROMA-MILL: Joint Low-Light Enhancement and White Balance under Colored Illumination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04092)
*Pol Riubrogent-Comas,David Serrano-Lozano,Maria Pilligua,Michael S. Brown,Ramon Baldrich,Javier Vazquez-Corral*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CHROMA-MILL RAW数据集（50场景、双设备、4种标定光源×11档强度）并利用RAW线性域混合合成物理有效的光源混合样本，据此发现联合进行LLIE与AWB最优，并给出CTI-Retinexformer方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低光照图像增强(LLIE)与自动白平衡(AWB)共享同一弱噪声传感器信号，二者性能均受其害；现有流程为"先LLIE后AWB"（级联），但缺乏验证，因为现有低光数据集光源范围窄且无光源色度真值，无法评估AWB。

Method: 构建CHROMA-MILL：DSLR与智能手机在4种已标定光源、11个强度档位下拍摄50个场景的RAW数据；利用光的可加性，在RAW线性域中混合拍摄样本，合成具有精确色度真值的物理有效光源混合；在该基准上对比级联与联合方案，并提出CTI-Retinexformer，同时对光照图、色度预测与光照不变场景嵌入进行监督。

Result: 基准测试表明联合校正（joint correction）效果最佳；CTI-Retinexformer较最优级联方案提升0.7 dB PSNR_L与1.1 ΔE00。

Conclusion: 低光场景下LLIE与AWB应联合求解而非级联；CHROMA-MILL提供了带精确光源真值、光源覆盖更广的RAW评测基准，CTI-Retinexformer以多任务监督实现更优的增强与色彩一致性。

Abstract: Capturing an image in a low-light environment produces a weak, noisy sensor signal. Low-light image enhancement (LLIE) aims to restore it. Auto white balance (AWB) is also required for every image, and the same noisy signal degrades its ability to infer the scene illuminant. LLIE is typically performed first, followed by AWB, but this strategy remains largely unexamined because existing low-light datasets span a narrow range of illuminants and lack illuminant ground truth for evaluating AWB. We address this with CHROMA-MILL, a RAW dataset of 50 scenes captured with a DSLR and smartphone under four calibrated illuminants at eleven intensity levels. Because light is additive, we blend these captures in the linear RAW domain to synthesize physically valid illuminant mixtures with exact chromaticity ground truth. Benchmarking on this data, we find joint correction is best, and introduce CTI-Retinexformer, which supervises an illumination map, a chromaticity prediction, and an illumination-invariant scene embedding, outperforming the best cascade by 0.7 dB PSNR$_L$ and 1.1 $ΔE_{00}$.

</details>


### [19] [Is This 3D Asset Usable? Beyond the Beauty Render for Asset Curation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04102)
*Rushikesh Zawar,Vladimir Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Diagnostic Rendered Evidence (DRE)：用对齐的beauty、wireframe、UV接缝+线框三视图，经分视图冻结ViT分类器与后期融合，判定3D资产可用性，无需元数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型3D资产库便于收集资产，却难以可靠判断其能否在场景装配、编辑、渲染、游戏制作与训练数据构建中复用。仅凭RGB外观合理的资产可能隐藏断裂几何、UV组织不良或导入后材质解析失败等缺陷。

Method: DRE构造对齐的三种渲染视图（beauty、wireframe、UV-seam+wireframe），各视图由针对该视图定制的冻结ViT分类器打分，再通过late fusion融合。推理仅需导入资产及渲染器产生的证据，不使用仓库身份、创作者、热度、文本或来源元数据。

Result: 在1,234个人工标注的TexVerse资产上，嵌套分组评测达到86.36%平衡准确率与88.32%坏资产召回率；在独立的、类别平衡的764个外部参照测试上，达到88.9%平衡参照标签准确率。

Conclusion: 结果支持跨语料可迁移性，并表明面向用途设计的诊断视图能揭示仅靠beauty视图易遗漏的缺陷。

Abstract: Large 3D repositories make it easy to collect assets, but difficult to determine whether they can be reused reliably across scene assembly, editing, rendering, game production, and training-data construction. An asset can look plausible in RGB while hiding disconnected geometry, problematic UV organization, or materials that fail to resolve correctly after import. We introduce Diagnostic Rendered Evidence (DRE): aligned beauty, wireframe, and UV-seam-plus-wireframe views scored by pass-specific frozen ViT classifiers and combined through late fusion. On 1,234 human labeled TexVerse assets, nested grouped evaluation achieves 86.36% balanced accuracy and 88.32% bad-asset recall. On a separate, balanced 764 external reference test, DRE achieved 88.9% balanced reference-label accuracy. Our method requires only an imported asset and renderer-derived evidence, without repository identity, creator, popularity, text, or source metadata at inference. Our results support cross-corpus portability and show that purpose built diagnostic views expose defects that just the beauty views can miss. Webpage: https://rbz-99.github.io/is-this-3d-asset-usable/

</details>


### [20] [Co-Occurrence Priors Override Visual Spatial Evidence: Understanding False Alarms in Vision-Language Models for Construction Struck-By Hazard Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04112)
*Xiaoshan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示视觉语言模型（VLM）在"撞击型（struck-by）"危险预警中的误报并非源于目标识别或空间关系编码失败，而是源于决策阶段被学得的共现先验所主导，并提出以模型自身空间关系表征为条件的决策校正方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM日益被用于施工危险监测，但误报频繁且成因不清。作者试图区分误报的三类可能来源：目标识别失败、空间关系编码失败，还是危险决策被"工人—挖掘机"共现先验支配。

Method: 构建355组反事实"工人—挖掘机"场景对，控制空间接近度并设置移除工人等变体；评估Qwen2.5-VL-7B与InternVL3.5-8B；综合行为分析、内部表征探针、因果干预（注意力头干预、神经元级干预、激活引导）以及决策校正（以模型自身空间关系表征为条件、并对照阈值重校准）。

Result: 两模型均能识别实体并编码空间关系（out-of-fold AUROC分别为0.82与0.91），在79%与73%的场景对中给近距离场景更高的危险分数；但远处场景被误判为危险的比例仍达74%与100%。移除工人使Qwen危险率降至11%，工人存在对危险分数的影响是空间接近度的2.4倍与3.2倍。各类因果干预均无法消除共现偏差；而将决策条件化于空间关系表征，在匹配灵敏度下把Qwen的特异性从0.36提升到0.58，优于阈值重校准。

Conclusion: VLM的struck-by误报主要源于决策失败，而非识别或空间编码缺陷；未来应优先让危险决策以已编码的空间证据为条件，而非仅提升视觉识别能力。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) increasingly support construction hazard monitoring, yet their frequent false alarms remain poorly understood. This study investigates whether false struck-by hazard warnings arise from failures in object recognition, spatial relation encoding, or hazard decisions dominated by learned co-occurrence priors. We evaluated Qwen2.5-VL-7B and InternVL3.5-8B using 355 counterfactual worker-excavator scene pairs with controlled spatial proximity and worker-removal variants, combining behavioral analysis, internal representation probing, causal interventions, and decision correction. Both models recognized the entities and encoded their spatial relations (out-of-fold AUROC: 0.82 and 0.91), assigning higher hazard scores to near than far scenes in 79% and 73% of pairs. Nevertheless, 74% and 100% of far scenes were falsely classified as hazardous. Removing the worker reduced Qwen's hazard rate to 11%, while worker presence exerted 2.4- and 3.2-times greater influence on hazard scores than spatial proximity in the respective models. Attention-head interventions, neuron-level interventions, and activation steering failed to eliminate this co-occurrence bias. However, conditioning hazard decisions on the model's own spatial-relation representation improved Qwen's specificity from 0.36 to 0.58 at matched sensitivity, outperforming threshold recalibration. These findings reveal that VLM struck-by false alarms stem primarily from decision-making failures rather than deficiencies in object recognition or spatial encoding, suggesting that future efforts should prioritize conditioning hazard decisions on encoded spatial evidence rather than further improving visual recognition alone.

</details>


### [21] [Improving PI-RADS Prediction and Prostate Cancer Detection from Multiparametric MRI with Anatomical Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04155)
*Seungbin Park,Thibault Heintz,Rachit Saluja,Francesca Khani,Timothy McClure,Daniel Margolis,Mert R. Sabuncu,Heejong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究评估将前列腺分区掩膜（解剖引导）引入多种网络架构（3D ResNet-18/50、ViT），是否能提升PI-RADS评分预测与临床显著前列腺癌（csPCa）检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从mpMRI准确预测PI-RADS评分比检测csPCa更具挑战性，而病灶所在前列腺分区是PI-RADS评分的关键依据，因此有必要验证解剖分区信息能否为深度学习模型提供有效增益。

Method: 在大规模内部数据集上，将前列腺分区掩膜（2区/5区引导）与3D ResNet-18、ResNet-50、ViT三类架构结合，比较有无解剖引导下的PI-RADS预测（QWK）与csPCa检测（AUC）；并在外部数据集上微调验证泛化性；进一步评估模型预测PI-RADS与专家PI-RADS联合用于csPCa检测的效果。

Result: 解剖引导在多数架构上普遍带来性能提升：PI-RADS预测最佳为ResNet-18+2区引导（QWK=0.511），csPCa检测最佳为ResNet-50+5区引导（AUC=0.809）。外部数据集微调后同样体现解剖引导优势。模型预测PI-RADS在PI-RADS≥3定义下可用于csPCa检测；将模型预测与专家评分结合使AUC由0.711提升至0.756（p<0.05）。

Conclusion: 将前列腺分区解剖先验纳入深度学习框架可提升前列腺MRI评估性能，且AI预测的PI-RADS评分与专家评分具有互补性，可作为专家判读的补充信息。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of Prostate Imaging Reporting and Data System (PI-RADS) scores from multiparametric MRI (mpMRI) remains challenging, more so than clinically significant prostate cancer (csPCa) detection. Given the importance of lesion location in PI-RADS scoring, we evaluated whether incorporating prostate zonal masks improves PI-RADS prediction and csPCa detection across multiple architectures (3D ResNet-18, ResNet-50, and ViT) using a large-scale internal dataset. Anatomical guidance generally improved performance across architectures, with the best results achieved by ResNet-18 with 2-zone guidance for PI-RADS prediction (QWK = 0.511) and ResNet-50 with 5-zone guidance for csPCa detection (AUC = 0.809). Fine-tuned results on external datasets further demonstrated the benefits of anatomical guidance. Furthermore, models with anatomical guidance improved the utility of model-predicted PI-RADS scores for PI-RADS-based csPCa detection (positive defined as PI-RADS>=3). Combining model-predicted and expert-assigned PI-RADS scores significantly increased the AUC from 0.711 to 0.756 (p < 0.05), suggesting that AI-derived PI-RADS scores may provide complementary information to expert assessment for csPCa detection. Overall, our findings highlight the value of incorporating prostate zonal anatomy into deep learning-based prostate MRI assessment.

</details>


### [22] [What Remains in Sight? Autoregressive Video Decoding as Representation-Guided Context Rewriting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04164)
*Lixuan He,Jihyeon Je,Yang You,Leonidas Guibas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练方法 ReCR，将自回归视频解码中的“可见历史选择”显式化为基于解码器内部表征的上下文重写，在匹配可见 token 预算下稳定提升长时视频生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归长视频生成虽通过因果解码器、长上下文训练与 KV-cache 延长了可行时长，但其推理流程仍以近期窗口、压缩缓存或外部记忆隐式决定可见的过去，缺少对“给解码器看哪些历史帧/片段”这一选择的显式决策机制。

Method: ReCR 为免训练方法，把自回归视频解码建模为表征引导的上下文重写：每步利用解码器内部表征从已生成历史中构建被选中的可见上下文，偏好四类上下文单元（与全局 rollout 状态匹配、避免与近期延续冗余、保留时间边界覆盖、扩大视野内空间布局多样性），再将选中单元映射至紧凑因果轴以解码下一块。

Result: 在长时文本到视频基准上，ReCR 在匹配可见 token 预算下对多个自回归骨干模型均取得一致提升；表征空间诊断与转场次数提示切换等实证分析进一步表明，改善“保留哪些可见内容”是通向更稳定长视频解码的有效路径。

Conclusion: 可见上下文的选择可作为显式的测试时决策，且以表征引导的上下文重写优于隐式的近期窗口或缓存压缩策略，为长视频自回归解码提供了免训练的通用改进思路。

Abstract: Long-horizon video generation is increasingly moving toward autoregressive rollout, where each newly generated segment becomes part of the evidence used for future prediction. Recent causal decoders, long-context training, and KV-cache mechanisms have extended feasible duration, but most inference pipelines still decide the visible past implicitly through recent windows, compressed caches, or external memory. This raises a direct question: \textbf{can the choice of which past frames or segments to show to the decoder be treated as an explicit test-time decision?} We answer this question with \textsc{ReCR}, a training-free method that formulates autoregressive video decoding as representation-guided context rewriting. At each step, \textsc{ReCR} builds a selected visible context from the generated history by using internal decoder representations to favor context units that match the global rollout state, avoid redundancy with the recent continuation, preserve temporal boundary coverage, and widen the range of spatial layouts in view. The selected units are then mapped onto a compact causal axis before decoding the next block. Across long-horizon text-to-video benchmarks, \textsc{ReCR} consistently improves multiple autoregressive backbones under matched visible-token budgets. Empirical analyses such as representation-space diagnostics and transition-count prompt switching further show that improving what remains visible is an effective path toward more stable long-video decoding.

</details>


### [23] [ReconDreamer#: Self-Evolving Closed-Loop Synergy of Reconstruction and Generation for Dynamic Driving Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04173)
*Songling Liu,Guosheng Zhao,Xiaofeng Wang,Chaojun Ni,Guan Huang,Jiwen Lu,Xingang Wang,Zheng Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReconDreamer# 是一个将重建与生成协同的自进化闭环框架，通过生成模型与重建模型的迭代互增强，弥合合成新轨迹数据与真实传感器观测之间的差距，并引入基于几何先验的自适应地面模型提升渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态驾驶场景重建借助生成先验在新轨迹渲染质量上进展显著，但生成数据与真实世界传感器观测之间仍存在显著差距，严重限制了重建保真度的进一步提升。

Method: 提出自进化闭环框架，使生成模型与重建模型相互迭代精炼、彼此增强，最终提升重建模型的3D表征能力；并引入基于几何先验的自适应地面模型以增强渲染质量。

Result: 在 Waymo、nuScenes、PandaSet、EUVS 四个基准上进行全面评测；Waymo 上 6 米横向偏移时，相较 ReconDreamer++，NTA-IoU 提升 8.8%，NTL-IoU 提升 3.0%，FID 降低 15.1%。

Conclusion: 自进化闭环与几何先验地面模型能有效弥合合成数据与真实观测的差距，显著提升动态驾驶场景重建的保真度与渲染质量。

Abstract: Dynamic driving scene reconstruction has made remarkable strides in recent years by leveraging generative priors, particularly in the quality of novel trajectory rendering. Nevertheless, a significant gap remains between the generated data and real-world sensor observations, severely hindering further improvements in reconstruction fidelity. In this paper, we present ReconDreamer#, a self-evolving framework that synergizes reconstruction and generation for dynamic driving scenes, which substantially bridges the gap between synthesized novel trajectory data and recorded real-world observations. Within this self-evolving closed-loop framework, the generative and reconstruction models mutually reinforce each other through iterative refinement, ultimately yielding a significant enhancement in the 3D representation capability of the reconstruction model. Additionally, we introduce a geometry-prior-based adaptive ground model to further enhance the rendering quality of the reconstruction model. To validate the effectiveness of the proposed method, we conduct comprehensive evaluations on Waymo, nuScenes, PandaSet, and EUVS benchmarks. Specifically on Waymo, at a 6-m lateral shift, ReconDreamer# improves NTA-IoU and NTL-IoU by 8.8% and 3.0%, respectively, and reduces FID by 15.1% relative to ReconDreamer++.

</details>


### [24] [Statistical Machine Learning for Medicinal Plant Leaves Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04193)
*Jayani P. Gamage,Thiyanga S. Talagala*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MEDIPI：基于52个可解释特征（形状、纹理、颜色及新增scagnostics类）的三级层次分类方法识别药用植物叶片，并发布含1,099张图像、31个物种的R包数据集MedLEA；层次随机森林在该数据集上达到100%准确率，但跨数据集泛化仍是难题（准确率骤降至23–50%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药用植物识别目前多依赖从业者经验，缺乏可复现的、基于图像的方法；作者希望以可解释的统计机器学习方法补充传统经验知识，并提供公开数据集促进可复现研究。

Method: 提出MEDIPI统计机器学习流程：从每张叶片图像提取52个可解释特征，涵盖形状、纹理、颜色以及新引入的scagnostics特征类别；采用形状→边缘类型→物种的三级层次分类结构。对比层次与非层次策略，并比较8种算法（随机森林、XGBoost、梯度提升、LDA、kNN、SVM、概率神经网络、人工神经网络）。同时发布R包MedLEA（31种斯里兰卡药用植物、1,099张叶片图像）。

Result: 在该数据集上，层次随机森林取得最佳总体准确率100%；在形状特征基础上加入纹理、颜色与scagnostic特征，使形状层级准确率由90%提升至97%；LDA图可展示训练模型对叶片形状的区分能力。当训练与测试数据来自不同数据集时，准确率大幅下降（23–50%）。

Conclusion: 层次化结合多类可解释特征的随机森林在同一数据集内可实现近乎完美的药用植物叶片识别，验证了特征扩展与层次结构的有效性；但跨数据集泛化性差是主要瓶颈，是后续研究需重点解决的问题。

Abstract: Medicinal plants are usually identified using practitioners' experience, but reproducible, image based methods can complement this knowledge. We present MEDIPI (MEDIcinal Plant Identification), a statistical machine learning approach that classifies medicinal plant leaves from photographs. From each leaf image we extract 52 interpretable features covering shape, texture, color, and a new scagnostics category. We then classify leaves using a three level hierarchy: shape, then edge type, then species. We also release MedLEA, an open R package with 1,099 leaf images of 31 Sri Lankan medicinal plant species. On this dataset, a hierarchical Random Forest achieved the best overall accuracy (100%) in our comparison of hierarchical and non-hierarchical approaches and eight algorithms: Random Forest, XGBoost, Gradient Boosting, linear discriminant analysis, k-nearest neighbor, support vector machine, probabilistic neural network, and artificial neural network. Adding texture, color, and scagnostic features to shape features raised shape level accuracy from 90% to 97%. Linear discriminant analysis plots show how the trained model separates leaf shapes. Accuracy dropped sharply (23-50%) when training and test data came from different datasets, so generalization across datasets remains a challenge.

</details>


### [25] [SettleSplat: Monocular Gaussian Mapping under In-View Scene Changes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04195)
*Anh Thuan Tran,Xiyun Song,Yubin Zhou,Heather Yu,Jana Kosecka,Liang Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SettleSplat：面向"视野内场景变化"的单目在线3D高斯建图系统，通过不确定性感知的动态性场剪除瞬态物体、并用无参数渲染深度不确定性加权的几何矛盾检测持久变化，在Bonn和Co-Fusion上取得一致的3D高斯地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线3D高斯建图虽能实现快速高保真新视角合成，但现有方法多假设静态场景或将动态视为离群点滤除。本文研究的"视野内场景变化"（单个在线序列中物体在相机前被操作）不同于以往检测视野外或跨采集的变化，其难点在于瞬态动态与持久变化互相纠缠：物体移动时是干扰物，静止后却成为场景的一部分。已有工作仅通过移除干扰物获得干净静态背景，该设定仍缺乏研究。

Method: SettleSplat为单目建图系统：（1）将粗粒度逐像素不确定性提升为高斯动态性场（dynamicity field），据此剪除瞬态高斯；（2）针对持久变化，利用无参数（parameter-free）的渲染深度不确定性对几何矛盾进行加权，从而将真实场景变化与尚未充分优化的区域区分开。

Result: 在Bonn与Co-Fusion数据集上，SettleSplat生成一致的3D高斯地图，在全图与变化区域两方面均优于现有基线，同时保持相当的速度。

Conclusion: 本文提出并形式化"视野内场景变化"这一在线单目建图设定，给出基于不确定性的动态性场与深度不确定性加权几何矛盾的解决方案，实现变化场景下持续更新的3D高斯地图，验证了区分瞬态干扰与持久变化的关键作用。

Abstract: Online 3D Gaussian mapping enables fast and high-fidelity novel view synthesis, with broad applications in augmented reality, robotics, and autonomous systems. However, many current methods assume static scenes or filter out dynamics as outliers. In this paper, we study in-view scene changes where objects are manipulated in front of the camera within a single online sequence, unlike prior works that detect changes outside the camera view or between separate captures. This setting entangles transient and persistent dynamics, as objects can be distractors while moving and persistent once they settle. While recent studies start to address distractors by removing them for clean static background, in-view scene changes remain underexplored. To resolve this, we present SettleSplat, a monocular mapping system that maintains up-to-date 3D Gaussian maps under such changes. Specifically, we lift coarse per-pixel uncertainty into Gaussian dynamicity field, then prune transient ones. For persistent changes, we derive geometric contradictions weighted by parameter-free rendered depth uncertainty to separate true changes from under-optimized regions. On Bonn and Co-Fusion, SettleSplat achieves consistent 3D Gaussian maps, outperforming current baselines on both full images and change regions while maintaining comparable speed.

</details>


### [26] [Impact of Input Resolution in Mammographic Mass Detection: A Controlled Multi-Architecture Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04200)
*Kaan Buyukdemirci,Emine Ulku Saritas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究在VinDr-Mammo上受控隔离输入分辨率的影响：训练18个检测器×6种分辨率（512×416至1600×1280），共108个组合。分辨率提升使中位mAP50由0.626升至0.660，增益主要来自小肿块（+0.074），代价为训练/推理时间增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习乳腺X线肿块检测器通常对图像下采样以降低计算成本，但已有文献因网络结构、预处理和评估协议不一致，无法量化该权衡究竟牺牲了多少精度，因此需要受控实验单独考察输入分辨率的作用。

Method: 在VinDr-Mammo数据集上进行受控实验：18个检测器×6种输入分辨率（512×416至1600×1280），共108个模型-分辨率组合；全幅乳腺图像直接缩放，不裁剪、不切块；对每个模型固定预处理、初始化、数据增强与评估流程；以官方测试集219张肿块阳性图像的mAP50为指标，并分析测试mAP50≥0.60的57个组合。

Result: 从最低到最高分辨率，mAP50中位数由0.626升至0.660，最佳值由0.670升至0.709；最优结果由DINO+ConvNeXt-S取得，为目前肿块阳性VinDr-Mammo图像上的最高mAP50。增益集中于小肿块：最小三分之一病灶的mAP50中位数提升0.074（0.367→0.441），中等与较大病灶仅提升0.007与0.032。训练时间中位数由0.45增至1.40 h，单图推理时间由23.6增至113.7 ms。

Conclusion: 提高输入分辨率主要惠及最难检测的小肿块，且其计算代价仍在可接受范围内；因此不应为节省算力而盲目下采样，尤其在小病灶检测场景。

Abstract: Deep-learning-based mass detectors usually downsample mammograms, trading fine image detail for lower computational cost. However, published studies cannot reveal how much accuracy this trade-off sacrifices, since they also differ in architecture, preprocessing, and evaluation protocol. In this work, we isolated the effect of input resolution in a controlled study on the VinDr-Mammo dataset, training 18 detectors at six input resolutions ranging from 512 x 416 to 1600 x 1280 pixels, for 108 model-resolution combinations. Whole mammograms were resized without cropping or tiling. For each model, preprocessing, model initialization, augmentation, and evaluation procedures were kept fixed across resolutions. Detection accuracy was evaluated by mAP50 on the 219 mass-positive images of the official test split, and 57 combinations with test mAP50 of at least 0.60 were analyzed. From the lowest to the highest resolution, median mAP50 rose from 0.626 to 0.660 and the best mAP50 from 0.670 to 0.709. The best result, achieved by DINO with a ConvNeXt-S backbone, is the highest mAP50 reported to date on mass-positive VinDr-Mammo images. Gains were concentrated in small masses: for the smallest third of lesions, median mAP50 rose by 0.074 (from 0.367 to 0.441), compared with 0.007 and 0.032 for medium and large masses. Median training time increased from 0.45 to 1.40 h, and median inference time from 23.6 to 113.7 ms per image. Therefore, higher input resolution mainly benefits the small masses that are hardest to detect, at a computational cost that remains practical.

</details>


### [27] [LiSA: Linear Stochastic Attention for Long-Range Visual Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04216)
*Sahar Dastani,Fereshteh Shakeri,Samuel Barbeau,Kunal Mahatha,Ismail Ben Ayed,Herve Lombaert,Christian Desrosiers*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LiSA是一种线性注意力插件模块，通过基于截断随机游走的随机扩散过程实现高阶token交互，在保持线性复杂度与稳定效率的同时提升表达力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准自注意力对序列长度呈二次复杂度，构成计算瓶颈；现有基于核近似softmax的线性注意力仅做一阶聚合，限制表达力，难以捕捉高阶结构信息。

Method: 提出Linear Stochastic Attention (LiSA)线性注意力插件模块：用截断随机游走构建随机扩散过程以实现高阶token交互；引入自适应局部性，偏好语义相似的邻居以保留细粒度结构，并保持训练稳定高效。

Result: 在分类、语义分割与目标检测任务上开展大量实验，结果优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过随机扩散的高阶聚合可有效弥补线性注意力一阶聚合的表达力不足，LiSA在效率与性能之间取得更优平衡，具备作为通用线性注意力插件的适用性。

Abstract: Self-attention has become the standard for modern sequence modeling, enabling Transformers to model long-range dependencies across a wide range of modalities. Despite these advances, its quadratic cost with respect to sequence length remains a significant computational bottleneck. Recent work addresses this limitation by introducing linear variants of self-attention, namely with kernel approximations of the softmax operator. However, their first-order aggregation restricts expressivity and struggles to capture higher-order structure. We propose Linear Stochastic Attention (LiSA), a linear attention plug-in module that enables higher-order token interactions via a stochastic diffusion process based on truncated random walks. LiSA incorporates adaptive locality, favoring semantically similar neighbors to preserve fine-grained structures, while maintaining stable and efficient training. We conduct extensive experiments on classification, semantic segmentation, and object detection tasks and show improved results over state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [28] [Reliability-Role Collaborative Learning for Semi-Supervised Cardiac Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04219)
*Zhongrui Xie,Wenfeng Zhang,Wei Hu,Ruitao Xie,Wenjie Wang,Zhouyun Xie,Yi Zhang,Tao Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出R2CL框架，通过建模双网络置信关系为无标注区域分配不同角色并施加差异化监督，配合原型对比学习与高置信上下文精炼，实现半监督心脏图像分割的稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半监督分割中伪标签监督的可靠性在空间上高度不均：内部区域稳定，而边界、低置信区域及双网络分歧区易引入噪声；现有方法对所有无标注像素施加统一监督，忽视可靠性差异，削弱了困难区域的判别性学习。

Method: R2CL基于双网络置信关系将无标注区域划分为三类角色：互不确定区用于熵正则、置信差异区用于定向知识迁移、互置信区作为可靠监督锚点。具体由三模块实现：RR-MCL进行区域混合训练、定向跨网络监督与熵正则；RT-PCL以前景邻接的可靠性过渡区构造查询特征，并从互置信区学习网络专属类原型以增强边界判别；HCR利用互置信区内的可靠内部像素与可信稠密块补充稳定上下文监督。

Result: 在多个数据集上于区域重叠与边界相关指标均取得稳定提升，且在前景稀疏的MVAA-CT数据集上优势更为明显。

Conclusion: 按可靠性差异对无标注区域进行角色化、差异化监督，并结合原型对比学习与上下文精炼，可有效缓解伪标签噪声、改善边界判别，是半监督心脏分割的一种有效范式。

Abstract: Semi-supervised cardiac image segmentation aims to exploit abundant unlabeled images together with limited annotations, but the reliability of pseudo-label supervision is highly uneven across spatial regions.
  In a dual-network setting, interior regions are usually more stable, whereas boundary regions, low-confidence areas, and disagreements between networks can introduce noisy supervision.
  However, existing methods typically impose uniform supervision on all unlabeled pixels, overlooking reliability differences and limiting discriminative learning in challenging areas.
  To address this issue, we propose R2CL (Reliability-Role Collaborative Learning), a framework for semi-supervised cardiac image segmentation.
  R2CL models confidence relationships between two networks to assign roles to unlabeled regions: mutually uncertain regions are used for entropy regularization, confidence-discrepant regions for directional knowledge transfer, and mutually confident regions as reliable supervision anchors.
  Reliability-Role Mixed Cross Learning (RR-MCL) implements these roles through region-mixed training, directional cross-network supervision, and entropy regularization over mutually uncertain regions.
  Reliability-Transition Prototype Contrastive Learning (RT-PCL) constructs query features from foreground-adjacent reliability-transition regions and learns network-specific class prototypes from mutually confident regions, improving feature discrimination around ambiguous boundaries.
  High-confidence Context Refinement (HCR) exploits reliable interior pixels and trusted dense blocks within mutually confident regions to complement stable contextual supervision.
  R2CL achieves stable improvements in region-overlap and boundary-related metrics across multiple datasets, with more evident advantages on the foreground-sparse MVAA-CT dataset.

</details>


### [29] [Mamba3R: Fixed-Capacity Selective State Memory for Long-Sequence 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04221)
*Zhenxiang Xiong,Tianchen Deng,Nailin Wang,Lei Zhao,Guole Shen,Weidong Che*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Mamba3R：一种可训练的状态-记忆适配器，在不更新预训练循环模型骨干与任务头的前提下，仅用 128 万参数实现流式 3D 重建的长期几何质量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式 3D 重建需在固定 GPU 预算下处理长序列并保持几何质量。预训练循环模型虽提供有界状态，但冻结骨干也固定了其状态-观测交互方式，难以适配新场景/新条件。

Method: 设计联合的状态-记忆适配器：以选择性状态空间（Mamba 式）混合器同时变换原生状态特征与固定容量辅助记忆；设置显式读/写路径实现跨帧信息传递；并用分解式反馈路径调控辅助记忆回馈内容。仅训练 128 万适配器参数，骨干与任务头冻结。

Result: 在共享周期性重置协议下，相比循环基线在公开室内外数据集与机器人采集轨迹上降低轨迹误差、提升累积表面与深度精度，单项指标最高提升 40%。消融对比验证了混合算子选择与跨帧辅助记忆的有效性；内存占用不变，1k 至 2k 帧吞吐变化不超过 1.4%。RTX 4060 离线回放平均 11.7 FPS、占用 5.1 GiB，可覆盖 10 Hz 传感器速率。

Conclusion: 以极小参数量的适配器即可为冻结的循环重建模型提供可训练适配路径，兼顾精度、恒定内存与实时吞吐，适合长序列流式部署。

Abstract: Streaming 3D reconstruction must maintain geometric quality over long inputs within a fixed GPU budget. A pretrained recurrent model provides bounded state, but freezing its backbone also fixes its learned state-observation interactions. We introduce Mamba3R, a jointly designed state-memory adapter that provides a trainable adaptation path without updating the backbone or task heads. A selective state-space mixer jointly transforms native state features and a fixed-capacity auxiliary memory, explicit read and write paths carry information across frames, and a decomposed feedback path regulates what the added memory feeds back. Only 1.28 million adapter parameters are trained. Under a shared periodic-reset protocol, it lowers trajectory error and improves accumulated surface and depth accuracy over recurrent baselines on public indoor and outdoor datasets and robot-recorded traversals, gaining up to 40 percent on one metric. Matched comparisons support both the choice of the mixing operator and the utility of carrying the auxiliary memory across frames. Allocated memory remains unchanged and throughput moves by at most 1.4 percent from one to two thousand frames. Replaying its recorded traversals offline, the platform's RTX 4060 averages 11.7 frames per second within 5.1 GiB with the same checkpoint, a mean throughput covering the 10 Hz sensor rate.

</details>


### [30] [LINDA: Low Resource Foot Ulcer Segmentation using Local Similarity and Iterative Neural Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04233)
*Trishaani Acharjee,Sudip Chakrabarty,Rajdeep Chatterjee,Tathagata Bandyopadhyay*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LINDA：以局部自相似性（LSS）结构先验替代原始强度作为分割依据的轻量伤口分割模型，在FUSeg 2021上取得最佳Dice/IoU/边界精度且参数量仅25.70M。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足溃疡是下肢截肢的主要诱因，负担最重的是专家与算力均匮乏的低中收入地区；此类场景需要既准确又能运行于普通设备的伤口监测工具，而高精度通常依赖大模型，二者存在矛盾。

Method: 提出LINDA紧凑模型：从原图直接计算对局部光照变化稳定的Local Self-Similarity（LSS）图，作为显式结构先验融入ResNet-34编码器；再用小型Liquid Time-Constant（LTC）模块经由四步迭代细化边界。所有模型在FUSeg 2021上按统一协议训练。

Result: 在对比中获得最佳Dice 86.68%、IoU 79.67%、HD95 6.37像素，参数量仅25.70M，少于U-Net与Transformer基线；由于LSS图是输入的固定函数，模型所依赖的结构线索可被可读地呈现。

Conclusion: LINDA在资源受限的医疗场景下兼具准确性、可解释性与轻量性，是伤口评估的可行方案。

Abstract: Foot ulcers are a leading cause of lower limb amputation, and the highest burden often falls on low and middle income regions where specialists and computing hardware are both scarce. Wound monitoring tools are most useful there when they are accurate yet small enough to run on ordinary devices, but accuracy usually comes from heavy networks. We present LINDA, a compact model that grounds segmentation in an explicit structural prior instead of raw intensity. A Local Self-Similarity (LSS) map, computed directly from the image and stable under local lighting changes, is fused into a ResNet-34 encoder, and a small Liquid Time-Constant module refines the boundary over four iterative steps. With every model trained under one protocol on the FUSeg 2021 dataset, LINDA obtains the best Dice (86.68\%), IoU (79.67\%), and boundary accuracy (HD95 6.37 pixels) in our comparison while using only 25.70M parameters, fewer than the U-Net and transformer baselines. As the LSS map is a fixed function of the input, it exposes the structural cues the model follows in a readable form. Together this makes LINDA an accurate, inspectable, and lightweight option for wound assessment in resource-constrained care.

</details>


### [31] [TCINet: Tissue-Conditioned Adaptive Cross-Branch Interaction Network for Nuclei Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04235)
*Zhenghang Qian,Yang Liu,Yanzhi Yu,Peiwu Qin,Yibing Shen,Joongwon Chae,Lihui Luo,Zhongde Zhang,Jianwen Guo,Lianshun Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TCINet，一个单阶段网络，通过任务特定特征适配器（TSFA）和组织条件化任务感知跨分支交互（TC-TACBI）来同时满足细胞核分割与分型的异质特征需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粘连细胞核分离需要局部边界细节，细胞类型分类需要更广的形态学上下文，且这些需求随周围组织环境而变化；现有方法难以在一个模型中同时满足这些冲突性的特征需求。

Method: TCINet为单阶段模型，包含两个模块：(1) TSFA在跳跃连接处生成分支专属特征；(2) TC-TACBI利用预测的组织概率，在核存在（NP）、水平-垂直（HV）和核类型（NT）三个分支间控制三条非对称交互路径。

Result: PanNuke标准三折交叉验证下多类与二元全景质量（PQ）分别为0.519和0.692；MoNuSeg零样本测试检测F1为0.876；PanNuke fold 0上加入TC-TACBI使mPQ从0.505提升至0.525。

Conclusion: 结果表明任务解耦的特征适配与组织条件化的非对称分支交互能有效协调分割与分型的矛盾需求，提升全景分割性能并具备良好的零样本泛化能力。

Abstract: Separating touching nuclei requires local boundary detail, while cell type classification requires broader morphological context. These requirements also vary with the surrounding tissue environment. We propose the Tissue-Conditioned Adaptive Cross-Branch Interaction Network (TCINet), a single-stage model with Task-Specific Feature Adapters (TSFA) and Tissue-Conditioned Task-Aware Cross-Branch Interaction (TC-TACBI). TSFA produces branch-specific features at skip connections, and TC-TACBI controls three asymmetric paths among nucleus-presence (NP), horizontal-vertical (HV), and nucleus-type (NT) branches using predicted tissue probabilities. On PanNuke under standard three-fold cross-validation, TCINet achieves multiclass and binary panoptic quality scores of 0.519 and 0.692, respectively. Zero-shot tests on MoNuSeg yield a detection F1 score of 0.876. On PanNuke fold 0, adding TC-TACBI increases mPQ from 0.505 to 0.525. Code is available at https://github.com/celestial1321/TCINet.

</details>


### [32] [Improving Diffusion-Based Augmentation for Data-Scarce Image Classification via Mask-Guided Reference Attention and Repulsive Noise Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04242)
*Hyun-Jic Oh,GaYeon Koh,Won-Ki Jeong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对扩散模型数据增强在极端数据稀缺（长尾单样本）下的双重失效——合成样本缺乏判别性前景细节、类先验偏置导致多样性不足——提出采样侧框架：掩码引导参考注意力（MGR）与排斥噪声优化（RNO）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有"微调预训练文生图扩散模型 + 多样文本提示采样"流水线在长尾单样本等极端稀缺场景存在两个问题：(1) 生成样本缺少对分类器训练至关重要的判别性前景细节；(2) 适配后的类先验会偏置早期去噪过程，使不同文本提示下输出仍呈相似模式，多样性受限。

Method: 提出采样侧的两个互补组件，同时增强细节与多样性。MGR（Mask-Guided Reference Attention）：借助零样本分割，仅用参考特征更新前景 value token，将参考影响限制在目标物体上，缓解全局特征的过度注入。RNO（Repulsive Noise Optimization）：促使初始噪声轨迹偏离适配模型的主导生成模态，使目标提示能表达更广的上下文变化。

Result: 在 CUB-LT IF100 长尾单样本设置上取得最高 2.50 与 2.94 个点的提升；在 few-shot 与背景偏移评测中亦表现稳健，改进一致。

Conclusion: 在采样阶段而非模型适配阶段干预，可同时改善前景细节保真度与生成多样性，是缓解极端数据稀缺图像分类中扩散增强失效的有效途径。

Abstract: Diffusion-based data augmentation has emerged as a powerful strategy for addressing data-scarce image classification. Recent pipelines typically adapt a pretrained text-to-image diffusion model to a limited number of class examples and subsequently sample synthetic images using diverse textual prompts. However, in extreme data-scarce regimes such as long-tailed single-sample settings, this approach suffers from a dual failure mode: the generated samples often lack discriminative foreground details crucial for classifier training, and the adapted class prior can bias the early denoising process, causing output images to exhibit similar patterns despite varying text prompts. To overcome these limitations, we propose a sampling-side framework of two complementary components that simultaneously enhance fine details and diversity. First, Mask-Guided Reference (MGR) Attention updates only foreground value tokens with reference features via zero-shot segmentation, limiting reference influence to the target object and mitigating excessive global feature injection. Second, Repulsive Noise Optimization (RNO) encourages initial noise trajectories to diverge from dominant generative modes of the adapted model, enabling target prompts to express broader contextual variation. Extensive evaluations show consistent improvements, with the largest gains in long-tailed single-sample settings of up to 2.50 and 2.94 points on CUB-LT IF100, and further robustness across few-shot and background-shift evaluations.

</details>


### [33] [CounterSketch: Physics-Grounded Counterfactual Reasoning for Sketch-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04244)
*Hien Trinh,Tan-Hiep To,Dinh-Khoi Vo,Tam V. Nguyen,Minh-Triet Tran,Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CounterSketch将自然语言"What if...?"反事实问题转化为物理合理的静态素描动画，通过VLM场景接地、因果事件链推理、约束轨迹生成与验证实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态素描只能表达单一状态，难以呈现假设性干预下的演化；直接依赖VLM预测运动缺乏物理约束与可验证性。

Method: 框架整合四部分：VLM引导的场景接地；因果事件链推理；基于约束的轨迹生成（建模物理状态转移并更新场景图、计算连续物体轨迹）；以及依据物理、几何、语义约束的轨迹验证。验证后的轨迹投影为物体级运动边界框，用于引导下游素描视频合成。

Result: 在包含80个问题的CounterSketchBench上，物理接地轨迹相比VLM直接预测能提供更强的运动引导。

Conclusion: 引入物理接地与约束验证的中间轨迹表示可有效提升反事实素描动画的生成质量，为素描视频合成提供可靠的运动先验。

Abstract: Counterfactual sketch animation explores how a static sketch evolves under a hypothetical intervention. We present CounterSketch, a physics-grounded framework that transforms natural-language ``What if...?'' queries into physically plausible sketch animations. The framework integrates VLM-guided scene grounding, causal event-chain reasoning, constraint-based trajectory generation, and verification. It models physical transitions to update the scene graph, computes continuous object trajectories, and validates them against physical, geometric, and semantic constraints. The verified trajectories are then projected into object-level motion bounding boxes to guide downstream sketch-video synthesis. Experiments on CounterSketchBench (80 questions) demonstrate that physics-grounded trajectories provide stronger motion guidance than direct VLM predictions.

</details>


### [34] [P-SER: Physical Consistency-Constrained Scattering Expandable Representation for Class-Incremental HRRP Target Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04254)
*Fangyi Wang,Peng Zhao,Haipeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出P-SER框架，利用物理一致性约束的散射原型与轻量残差适配器，解决无样本类增量HRRP识别中的方位敏感性与模型膨胀问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HRRP表示紧凑但方位敏感性强：视角变化导致散射峰位置与幅度漂移，且反射体组合可能产生类似舰船的响应；在历史数据不可用的类增量场景下识别困难。现有动态扩展方法还带来大量参数与内存开销。

Method: 从复数域和通道（和通道、方位差通道）HRRP及观测几何中重构主导散射点；由重构的空间结构、目标尺寸与能量分布导出类级散射原型和样本级显著性先验，作为物理约束用于特征学习与旧类知识保持；构建基元散射体字典刻画局部强散射机理，并在推理时校准不确定的舰船—反射体预测；在共享HRRP骨干上增量引入轻量残差适配器以学习新类，限制模型增长。

Result: 在仿真与实测HRRP数据集上，P-SER在不保留旧类样本的条件下取得有竞争力的识别性能，并保持物理一致性、抗干扰性、知识保持能力与模型可扩展性。

Conclusion: 将散射物理先验与无样本类增量学习结合，可在低模型增长下实现稳健的HRRP增量识别。

Abstract: High-resolution range profiles (HRRPs) provide compact radar target representations but exhibit strong aspect sensitivity, complicating class-incremental recognition when historical data are unavailable. Changes in viewing aspect shift scattering-peak locations and amplitudes, while reflector groups may produce responses resembling ships. Existing dynamic expansion methods can also incur substantial parameter and memory overhead. To address these challenges, we propose P-SER, a Physical Consistency-Constrained Scattering Expandable Representation framework for exemplar-free class-incremental HRRP recognition. P-SER reconstructs dominant scattering points from complex-valued sum- and azimuth-difference-channel HRRPs and observation geometry. The reconstructed spatial structure, target extent, and energy distribution are used to derive class-level scattering prototypes and sample-level saliency priors, providing physically grounded constraints for feature learning and old-class knowledge retention. A primitive scatterer dictionary further characterizes local strong-scattering mechanisms and calibrates uncertain ship--reflector predictions at inference. Meanwhile, lightweight residual adapters are incrementally introduced into a shared HRRP backbone to learn new classes with limited model growth. Experiments on simulated and measured HRRP datasets show that P-SER delivers competitive recognition performance without retaining old-class exemplars, while maintaining physical consistency, interference robustness, knowledge retention, and model scalability.

</details>


### [35] [ProGeo-RC: Prototype-guided Semantic Reliability and Geometric Error Calibration for Semi-supervised Cardiac Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04257)
*Yunfei Zhu,Wenfeng Zhang,Pan Zeng,Xin Huang,Tao Jia,Weiting Shao,Wei Hu,Jianming Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ProGeo-RC框架，通过原型引导的语义可靠性估计与结构化语义-几何误差校准，为半监督医学图像分割提供更可靠的伪监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有半监督医学图像分割方法依赖预测置信度或模型一致性来评估伪标签可靠性，这类准则无法同时刻画语义可靠性与几何合理性，导致伪监督质量不足。

Method: ProGeo-RC包含两个模块：(1) PSRE（Prototype-guided Semantic Reliability Estimation），利用概率类别原型估计无标签预测的语义可靠性，并据此校准像素级教师伪标签；(2) S-SGEC（Structured Semantic-Geometric Error Calibration），引入基于符号距离场（SDF）的几何约束，用于细化模糊边界并抑制目标结构外的虚假前景响应。

Result: 在ACDC与MVAA-CT数据集上优于代表性半监督分割方法；ACDC仅用10%标注时，DSC与Jaccard分别比次优结果提升0.37%和0.49%，95HD与ASD分别降低0.61和0.21。

Conclusion: 联合语义可靠性估计与几何误差校准可为半监督医学图像分割提供更可靠的伪监督，验证了语义与几何双重约束协同的有效性。

Abstract: Semi-supervised medical image segmentation aims to train reliable segmentation models using limited labeled data and abundant unlabeled data. However, existing methods typically rely on prediction confidence or model consistency to estimate pseudo-label reliability, but these criteria are insufficient to characterize both semantic reliability and geometric plausibility. To address this issue, we propose ProGeo-RC, a prototype-guided semantic reliability and geometric error calibration framework for semi-supervised medical image segmentation. ProGeo-RC consists of two key modules: Prototype-guided Semantic Reliability Estimation (PSRE) and Structured Semantic-Geometric Error Calibration (S-SGEC). PSRE uses probabilistic class prototypes to estimate the semantic reliability of unlabeled predictions and calibrate pixel-wise teacher pseudo labels, while S-SGEC introduces signed-distance-field-based geometric constraints to refine ambiguous boundaries and suppress spurious foreground responses outside target structures. Experiments on the ACDC and MVAA-CT datasets show that ProGeo-RC achieves better performance compared with representative semi-supervised segmentation methods. Under the 10\% labeled setting on ACDC, ProGeo-RC improves DSC and Jaccard by 0.37\% and 0.49\% over the corresponding second-best results, while reducing 95HD and ASD by 0.61 and 0.21, respectively. These results indicate that joint semantic reliability estimation and geometric error calibration can provide more reliable pseudo supervision for semi-supervised medical image segmentation.

</details>


### [36] [Sketch-Omni: Instance-Level Sketch-Based Visual Prompting for Perception MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04262)
*Dongyu Wang,Dar-Yen Chen,Zhiming Hong,Arkaprabha Basu,Yi-Zhe Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Sketch-Omni，首个面向草图并可"以草图为提示"的感知多模态大模型；通过边缘检测自动构造训练数据与照片引导的关系对齐训练，在草图提示定位上达 91.4 F1（强通用 MLLM 为 81.8），六大任务 40 个评测单元中 39 项最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可提示感知模型（GroundingDINO 至 Rex-Omni）均面向照片训练，缺乏草图处理能力。核心难点有二：(1) 照片训练模型的注意力偏向颜色与纹理，而草图不具备这些线索；(2) 不存在将一幅画与其所描绘的同类中特定实例配对的现成数据集。

Method: 不采集而构造训练数据：用边缘检测器将每张有标注照片转为类草图，并继承原标签；用两种构造方式从既有数据生成草图-照片实例对。此外提出照片引导的关系对齐，将照片与其边缘草图输入同一模型，训练草图通路复现照片通路中"短语—图块"及"图块—图块"的关系结构。全流程无需人工标注。

Result: 草图提示定位取得 91.4 F1，强通用 MLLM 为 81.8；当照片中存在多个同类实例时领先幅度翻倍；六个任务共 40 个评测单元中 39 个最优。同时将发布代码、模型及含 2754 条草图—照片链接的人工标注基准。

Conclusion: Sketch-Omni 将可提示感知从照片扩展到草图，并支持以草图为提示进行细粒度实例级定位；自动数据构造与跨模态关系对齐是绕开标注瓶颈的有效途径。

Abstract: Promptable perception, from GroundingDINO to Rex-Omni, puts one model behind one prompt interface and returns coordinates for any localisation task.
