Every model in that line was built for photographs; none has been given a sketch. We present Sketch-Omni, to our knowledge the first perception MLLM built to work on sketches and from them. On a sketch, the sketch is the image, and text prompts detect, point, resolve object and spatial referring expressions, and ground affordances in it. From a sketch, the sketch is the prompt, and a drawing of one object localises that object in a photograph, even among several of the same category, a query that neither a category name nor a photo exemplar can pose. Two obstacles stand in the way. A photo-trained model's attention is tuned to colour and texture, which a sketch does not have, and no dataset pairs a drawing with the one instance it depicts among others of its kind. We build the training data instead of collecting it. An edge detector turns every labelled photograph into a sketch-like drawing that keeps the photograph's labels, and two constructions produce sketch-to-photo instance pairs from existing data. On top of this, photo-guided relation alignment passes each photograph and its edge sketch through the same model and trains the sketch pass to reproduce how the photograph pass relates phrases to patches and patches to each other. Not one training label is drawn by hand. Sketch-Omni reaches 91.4 F1 on sketch-prompted localisation against 81.8 for the strongest general-purpose MLLM, with a lead that doubles when the photograph holds several instances of the queried category, and it is best in 39 of 40 evaluation cells across six tasks. Code, model, and a hand-annotated benchmark of 2754 sketch-to-photo links will be released.

</details>


### [37] [Weight-Space Programming for Improving Image-Restoration Diffusion Models: Technical Conjectures and a Recursive Improvement Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04268)
*Zhuoran Zheng,Doubao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出可检验的猜想：LLM 能否在无梯度、无配对数据、无微调的条件下，直接编辑扩散式图像复原 UNet 的少量权重方向以提升复原质量，并构建"诊断→编辑→评估→再诊断"的最小递归自改进（RSI）回路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期证据显示前沿 LLM 可通过修改约 0.016% 参数修复结构性 bug，但无法注入新能力，即权重空间编程（WSP）目前停留在"调试"层面。图像复原是精确回归任务，扩散 UNet 兼具"低层信号处理层集中、高层语义层分散"的中间特性，是检验 WSP 能否从调试跃迁到"改进"的理想试验场。作者意在为图像复原开辟新子方向。

Method: 纯概念/猜想性分析，无实验。提出三条技术猜想：C1 在 200 次前向预算内，LLM 可定位导致质量差距（过平滑、色偏、幻构纹理）的层；C2 编辑少量已识别的权重方向（如缩放高频滤波器范数、校正输出偏置通道）可测量地提升 PSNR 与感知质量；C3 递归回路可跨轮次复利增益。另分析所需 AI 能力（定向编辑不需超级智能，通用能力生成则需要），剖析像素级精确回归的难点，给出 PyTorch 风格部署流水线与基准设计草案。

Result: 无实证结果；贡献为猜想陈述、能力层级论证、挑战清单、部署流水线与基准大纲。

Conclusion: WSP 有望在图像复原的精确回归设定中从"调试"迈向"改进"，其可行性取决于定向权重编辑是否足以弥合质量差距，以及递归诊断—编辑闭环能否形成复利增益；该手稿以猜想形式发布，旨在为图像复原开启新的研究子话题。

Abstract: Can an LLM directly edit the weights of a diffusion-based image-restoration model to \emph{improve} restoration quality---without gradients, paired data, or fine-tuning? Recent experiments show frontier LLMs can fix structural bugs by modifying as little as \(0.016\%\) of parameters, but cannot instill new capabilities. We ask whether weight-space programming (WSP) can move from \emph{debugging} to \emph{improvement} in the precise-regression setting of image restoration (deblurring, super-resolution, denoising, inpainting). Restoration diffusion UNets present a middle ground: low-level signal-processing layers are concentrated enough for targeted weight-direction edits, while high-level semantic layers remain distributed and resistant to sparse modification. We propose three technical conjectures: (C1) an LLM can localize the layer behind a quality gap (over-smoothing, color shift, hallucinated texture) within a 200 forward-pass budget; (C2) editing a small set of identified weight directions (e.g., scaling high-frequency filter norms, correcting output-bias channels) can measurably improve PSNR and perceptual quality; (C3) a recursive loop---diagnose \(\rightarrow\) edit \(\rightarrow\) evaluate \(\rightarrow\) re-diagnose---can compound gains across rounds, forming a minimal recursive self-improvement (RSI) loop for vision. We analyze the required AI capability (arguing targeted edits need not superintelligence, while general capability generation does) and detail the challenges of precise pixel-level regression. We provide a PyTorch-style deployment pipeline and outline a benchmark. This manuscript is conjectural, intended to open a new subtopic for image restoration.

</details>


### [38] [GroundSeek-CXR: Retrospective Evaluation-Guided Active Observation Framework for Multi-Label Chest Radiography Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04286)
*Yihang Fu,Xinyu Liu,Qing Xu,Xiaofeng Yang,Zhen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 GroundSeek-CXR 主动式胸部X光诊断框架，通过最终诊断回顾性评价观察与停止决策，并用 RDE 与 RGPO 学习，异常样本 F1 提升 4.48%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胸片诊断需识别跨区域的细微病灶与共现异常；现有基于偏好的视觉语言模型方法以每次观察对当前诊断的即时影响进行监督，无法刻画单次观察及停止决策对最终诊断的贡献。

Method: 构建主动式 CXR 诊断框架 GroundSeek-CXR：1）Retrospective Diagnostic Evaluation（RDE），借助离线蒙特卡洛树搜索，通过独立延续（independent continuations）对观察与停止候选打分；2）Retrospective Gap-weighted Preference Optimization（RGPO），以分数差距加权的回顾性偏好进行学习，优先优化提升诊断准确率的动作。

Result: 大量实验表明该框架优于现有最优方法；相较即时分数偏好学习，异常图像样本 F1 提升 4.48%。

Conclusion: 以最终诊断回溯评价观察与停止决策，可更有效地训练主动式胸片诊断策略，显著优于即时反馈式偏好监督。

Abstract: Chest radiography (CXR) diagnosis is critical for thoracic disease assessment, yet challenged by the need to identify subtle lesions and co-occurring abnormalities across regions. While recent vision-language models can actively observe local regions, existing preference-based methods supervise each observation by its immediate effect on the current diagnosis, failing to capture how each observation and stopping decision contributes to the final diagnosis. To address this issue, we propose GroundSeek-CXR, an active CXR diagnosis framework that learns observation and stopping decisions by retrospectively evaluating them with the final diagnosis. Specifically, we first devise a Retrospective Diagnostic Evaluation (RDE) that scores observation and stop candidates from offline Monte Carlo tree search through independent continuations. Moreover, we design a Retrospective Gap-weighted Preference Optimization (RGPO) that learns from retrospective-score preferences weighted by their score gaps, prioritizing actions that improve diagnostic accuracy. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our GroundSeek-CXR framework outperforms state-of-the-art methods, and improves abnormal-image sample-F1 by 4.48% over immediate-score preference learning.

</details>


### [39] [CLOCs-R: Radar-Assisted Camera-LiDAR Fusion for Long-Range Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04300)
*Richard Beuck,John Nguyen,Sudip Dhakal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CLOCs-R将雷达证据加入相机-激光雷达候选级重打分以改善远距离3D检测，但在50m以上增益极小，最终与纯LiDAR的SECOND统计上持平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远距离3D检测困难：LiDAR回波稀疏、相机证据随距离衰减，而车载雷达在更远距离仍能提供测量。作者希望利用雷达补充证据，提升远距离检测性能。

Method: 在CLOCs候选级融合框架上扩展：多帧累积雷达回波并做自运动补偿，通过距离相关门控与每个pre-NMS 3D候选关联；提取支持度、多普勒速度及展宽、距离残差、偏移等紧凑特征，附加到每个相机-激光雷达候选对，由轻量融合网络重打分。先在KITTI上验证复现CLOCs，再在aiMotive晴好白天子集上，使用冻结的SECOND与Cascade R-CNN检测器，按距离分箱（至200m）评估。

Result: CLOCs在50m内优于SECOND，但更远处性能下降；事后提出的残差打分变体（修正而非替换SECOND分数）挽回了大部分损失。雷达在30-50m稳定提升BEV AP（0.857→0.870），但50m以外增益很小（中心距离AP +0.003），处于种子波动范围内，CLOCs-R配残差打分与纯LiDAR SECOND统计上持平。

Conclusion: 远距离收益有限的原因包括：雷达特征对真/假检测的区分度弱、远距离训练正样本稀缺、远距离相机证据差、以及重打分无法恢复LiDAR缺失的候选。作者并排报告了设计与事后结果，给出诚实的负向结论。

Abstract: Long-range 3D object detection remains difficult for autonomous vehicles because LiDAR returns become sparse and camera evidence weakens with distance, while automotive radar keeps returning measurements far beyond both. We present CLOCs-R, an extension of the CLOCs candidate-level fusion framework that adds radar evidence to camera-LiDAR rescoring. Radar returns are accumulated over several sweeps, compensated for ego-motion, and associated with each pre-NMS 3D candidate through a range-dependent gate. Compact features summarizing support, Doppler speed and spread, range residual, and offset are then appended to every camera-LiDAR pair before a lightweight fusion network rescores the candidates. We first verify that our fusion implementation reproduces CLOCs on KITTI. We then evaluate on the daytime clear-weather subset of the aiMotive dataset, reporting distance-binned metrics out to 200 m with frozen SECOND and Cascade R-CNN detectors. Camera-LiDAR CLOCs improves on SECOND up to 50 m but degrades beyond it. A residual scoring variant, introduced post hoc, corrects SECOND's score instead of replacing it and recovers most of this loss. Radar consistently improves BEV AP at 30-50 m (0.857 to 0.870), but beyond 50 m its gain is small (+0.003 center-distance AP), within seed variation, and CLOCs-R with residual scoring is statistically tied with LiDAR-only SECOND. Our analysis attributes the limited long-range benefit to weak separation of true and false detections by radar features, scarce far-range training positives, poor camera evidence at distance, and missing LiDAR proposals that rescoring cannot recover. We report all as-designed and post hoc results side by side.

</details>


### [40] [FlowTranslator: Training-Free In-Image Machine Translation with Flow-Based Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04303)
*Zhixian Li,Xingsong Ye,Yongkun Du,Yunyan Xiao,Yanglin Huang,Pinxuan Chen,Jiyu Zhuang,Cong Xu,Zhineng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无训练的图像内机器翻译框架 FlowTranslator，借助多语言文本生成能力，通过两项轨迹控制实现译文文本的准确自然生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 IIMT 专用模型依赖大量翻译前后成对图像训练；而先进 T2I 模型已具备可用的多语言文本生成能力，作者认为该能力可迁移至 IIMT。

Method: OCR 模型加 LLM 生成翻译提示并注入基于 T2I 的扩散模型；引入两项轨迹控制：Region Coupling Release（RCR）在轨迹早期于文本区域内部分解耦目标分支噪声，Coupling Expansion（CE）在轨迹中期放大渐现的编辑位移。

Result: 在 EasyOCR 回读协议下，PRIM 上 BLEU 20.6、COMET 57.2，显著优于训练型强基线 UniTranslator；在更具挑战的整图 ScenePair 真实照片译为中文任务上达 40.1% 精确匹配准确率，超过现有编辑系统。

Conclusion: 基于流（flow-based）的编辑可作为 IIMT 强有力且完全无训练的基线。

Abstract: In-image machine translation (IIMT) replaces text in an image with its translation while preserving the surrounding visual context. Existing task-specific models achieve convincing performance but require massive paired training images before and after translation. As advanced text-to-image (T2I) models have reached a practically usable level of multilingual text generation, we argue that this capability can be transferred to IIMT and present FlowTranslator. It is a training-free IIMT framework that first employs an OCR model and an LLM to generate the translation prompt, which is injected into the T2I-based diffusion model. To optimize the accurate and natural generation of translated text, we introduce two trajectory controls: Region Coupling Release (RCR) partially decorrelates target-branch noise inside the text region early in the trajectory, while Coupling Expansion (CE) amplifies the emerging edit displacement mid-trajectory. Under the EasyOCR read-back protocol, FlowTranslator achieves average PRIM BLEU and COMET scores of 20.6 and 57.2, respectively, substantially outperforming the strong training-based baseline UniTranslator. On the more challenging and realistic whole-image ScenePair photographs translated into Chinese, it reaches 40.1\% exact-match accuracy and surpasses existing editing systems, some of which only address the text-rendering stage rather than the complete IIMT task. These results establish flow-based editing as a strong, fully training-free baseline for IIMT.

</details>


### [41] [Weather Adaptive Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04305)
*Nicholas Taylor,Alexander Picon,Sudip Dhakal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究冻结目标检测器在恶劣天气下的置信度阈值决策，复现SeqCRC作为基线，提出用单参数λ成本权衡"旋钮"选择期望代价最优决策规则（单阈值或基于校准概率的逐框规则）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机检测器在雨、雾、雪、沙等恶劣天气下召回下降，部署置信度阈值直接决定漏检与虚警数量；而现有共形方法（如SeqCRC）虽能在给定漏检预算下校准阈值，其产生的虚警数只是"输出"而非可调选择（DAWN/BDD100K上每图1.6至117个），缺乏显式的漏检-虚警权衡机制。

Method: 1) 复现SeqCRC，以共形风险控制(CRC)按天气条件分别校准阈值作为基线，YOLOv8x在DAWN各天气箱内保持留出集漏检率在20%预算内；2) 提出校准权衡旋钮：单一参数λ（愿为避免一次漏检而接受的虚警数）选取期望代价最优决策规则——或为单一分数阈值，或为逐检测规则：用梯度提升树基于检测器自身逐框输出估计"真物体"校准概率，概率≥1/(1+λ)即保留该框；3) F-score偏好可映射到同一旋钮，计划对λ本身施加CRC以恢复逐条件漏检保证；旋钮仅在BDD100K训练集拟合一次，仅在BDD100K验证集与DAWN上评估。

Result: SeqCRC基线在DAWN各天气箱中（YOLOv8x）将留出漏检比例控制在20%预算内，而单一全局阈值在雾天失效；但其虚警数不可控，跨DAWN与BDD100K条件下每图从1.6到117不等。提出的λ旋钮在BDD100K验证集与DAWN上评估（摘要未给出具体数值结果）。

Conclusion: 将部署阈值决策由被动输出改为显式的单参数成本权衡，可在恶劣天气下按用户对漏检-虚警的相对代价偏好选择最优决策规则，且F-score偏好与逐条件漏检保证（计划中）可统一到同一旋钮框架。

Abstract: Camera object detectors lose recall in rain, fog, snow and sand, and the confidence threshold at which a detector is deployed decides how many objects it misses and how many false alarms it raises. We study how a frozen detector should make this decision in adverse weather. We first reproduce SeqCRC, a conformal object detector whose first step calibrates the confidence threshold by conformal risk control (CRC), and use that step, calibrated separately in each weather condition, as our baseline. With YOLOv8x it keeps the held-out miss fraction within a 20% budget in every DAWN weather bin, where a single global threshold fails in fog, but the false alarms it needs are an output rather than a choice: from 1.6 to 117 per image across DAWN and BDD100K conditions. We then propose a calibrated trade-off dial. One setting, $λ$, the number of false alarms accepted to avoid one missed object, selects the expected-cost-optimal decision rule: either one score threshold, or a per-detection rule that keeps a box when its calibrated probability of being a real object, estimated by gradient-boosted trees from the detector's own per-box outputs, is at least $1/(1+λ)$. F-score preferences map onto the same dial, and CRC applied to $λ$ itself is planned to restore a per-condition miss guarantee. The dial is fitted once on BDD100K train and graded only on BDD100K val and DAWN.

</details>


### [42] [SteelHuman: Predicting Operator Estimates of Steel-Scrap Composition from Sparse RGB Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04317)
*Jovan Kamchev,Zoran Ivanovski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于稀疏手持视角图像与冻结DINOv2特征的废钢堆成分估计方法，在600个out-of-fold钢堆上达到13.4%的总变差距离，并通过图像相关的HMS类别分配将HMS内部TV从25.1%降至11.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 废钢成分是产量规划与供应商公平结算的关键，但多吨级料堆的成分测量极为费力，通常依赖人工操作员评估。现有方法虽面向成分级任务，却需要结构化采集与多阶段预处理，成本高、部署难。

Method: 采用更简化的流程：从料堆的稀疏手持视角中聚合特征。具体使用冻结的DINOv2 token，在多个视角上均匀聚合以回归成分；对四个按厚度定义的重熔废钢（HMS）类别，用图像相关的分配替代训练集划分先验。

Result: 600个out-of-fold料堆上，冻结DINOv2均匀聚合特征相对操作员估计达到13.4% TV距离；图像相关分配使HMS内部TV由先验下的25.1%降至11.2%。作为外部评估，同一冻结特征在公开DOES分类数据集上取得93.97%准确率，超过所对比的已发表结果，且成分模型无需适配即可与相关DOES类别对齐。

Conclusion: 无需结构化采集与多阶段预处理的简单方案即可有效估计废钢料堆成分，误差主要集中在HMS类别内部分配，而图像相关分配可显著缓解该问题；模型还具备与公开分类基准的可迁移性。

Abstract: Steel scrap composition estimation is an important part of yield planning and fair supplier compensation. Measuring the composition of a multi-ton pile is an inherently laborious process, so the assessment is often left to trained human operators. Recent works tackle composition-level tasks, but require structured acquisition and multi-stage preprocessing. We study a simpler approach to steel scrap load assessment by aggregating features from sparse, handheld views of the pile. On 600 out-of-fold piles, frozen DINOv2 tokens aggregated uniformly across views reach 13.4% total variation (TV) distance against operator estimates. Most remaining error lies in allocation among four thickness-defined heavy melting scrap (HMS) classes, yet image-dependent allocation reduces within-HMS TV from 25.1% under a training-set split prior to 11.2%. Due to the lack of public datasets and benchmarks for predicting composition, external evaluation relies on the public DOES dataset intended for classification. A classifier using the same frozen features reaches 93.97% accuracy, exceeding the published results we compare against. The composition model also aligns with related DOES categories without adaptation.

</details>


### [43] [Mitigating Spatial Blindness in Multiple Instance Learning for Whole-Slide Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04337)
*Xiangyu Li,Jincheng Li,Xing Xie,Bin Guan,Shaohua Kevin Zhou,Rui Yan,Ran Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出"空间盲区"概念，即模型虽输入坐标或图结构，但训练后并未真正利用组织邻域信息；为此提出ResTopoMIL：先冻结已学好的成分预测器，再用带坐标重分配分离损失的图残差分支学习空间校正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全切片多示例学习虽能预测诊断与预后标签，但主要依赖组织成分，对空间组织利用有限。仅提供坐标或图输入，并不能保证训练后的预测器真正使用了组织邻域——作者将这一差距定义为"空间盲区"，并假设早期成分学习会削弱空间通路的监督信号。

Method: 1) 分阶段训练：先学习并冻结成分预测器作为参照锚点，再训练图残差分支学习空间校正；2) 引入坐标重分配分离损失（coordinate-reassignment separation loss）强化空间通路监督；3) 通过置换分析与信息分析区分"架构对称性""可用空间信息"与"优化机制"三种解释；4) 设置无坐标残差对照、邻域破坏、保邻域变换及按解剖结构分层扰动等控制实验。

Result: 受控空间任务上AUC达0.987，同一架构联合训练仅0.684；仅成分任务性能仍然很高。在4个WSI分类与5个生存分析基准中，ResTopoMIL在其中8个任务上提升平均主指标，其中4项经Holm校正的患者簇检验后仍显著。无坐标残差对照可恢复部分增益，邻域破坏使性能降至接近成分锚点水平。

Conclusion: 结果支持将"固定成分参照"作为学习空间校正的实用干预手段；同时作者承认，保留额外的外观表征容量也是增益的贡献解释之一。

Abstract: Whole-slide multiple instance learning can predict diagnostic and prognostic labels from tissue composition while making limited use of spatial organization. Providing coordinates or a graph does not establish that a trained predictor uses tissue neighborhoods. We examine this gap as spatial blindness and hypothesize that early composition learning can weaken supervision of a spatial pathway. We introduce ResTopoMIL, which freezes a learned composition predictor before training a graph residual with a coordinate-reassignment separation loss. Permutation and information analyses distinguish architectural symmetry, available spatial information and the proposed optimization mechanism. On a controlled spatial task, staged training achieved an AUC of 0.987, compared with 0.684 for the same jointly trained architecture. Performance remained high on a composition-only task. Across four WSI classification and five survival benchmarks, ResTopoMIL improved mean primary performance on eight tasks; four improvements remained significant after Holm-adjusted patient-cluster tests. Coordinate-free residual controls recovered some of the gains, while neighborhood destruction reduced performance close to the composition anchor. Neighborhood-preserving transformations and anatomy-stratified perturbations further characterized this dependence. These results support fixing a composition reference as a practical intervention for learning spatial corrections, while retaining additional appearance capacity as a contributing explanation.

</details>


### [44] [ReSMap: Recasting Satellite Priors for Robust and Accurate Online HD Map Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04352)
*Kyungmin Kim,Sumin Lee,Sungoh Jeong,DoHyun Lim,Seonghyun Park,Soonmin Hwang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ReSMap——一种冗余感知的相机-卫星融合框架，将卫星影像显式作为相机传感器失效时的冗余源，用于鲁棒的在线 HD 地图构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相机在线 HD 地图构建依赖车载相机，易受传感器故障影响；卫星影像可离线缓存、不依赖实时感知条件，但已有卫星融合方法仅在正常工况下使用卫星图，未将其作为传感器退化时的显式冗余。

Method: 包含两个核心模块：(1) Predict-then-Fuse (PTF)：以各分支自身的预测置信度作为逐像素可靠性权重，融合相机与卫星 BEV 特征，无需学习路由器；(2) Object-Centric Cooperative Multi-modal Query (OCMQ) 解码器：将逐模态分离扩展到 query 层面，每个模态仅对自身 BEV 做 cross-attention，逐模态更新通过学习的逐 token 门控合并。

Result: 在 nuScenes 所有 split 与 range 设置下取得 SOTA；在相机失效场景下亦创下新的 SOTA。

Conclusion: 卫星影像不仅是强先验，更可作为实用冗余，支撑鲁棒的在线 HD 地图构建。

Abstract: Despite recent advances in camera-based online HD map construction, these systems remain susceptible to sensor failures due to their reliance on onboard cameras. In contrast, satellite images remain available independently of real-time sensing conditions as they are cached offline. Existing methods utilizing satellite images, however, primarily leverage them under nominal conditions, without explicitly addressing them as redundancy when a sensor degrades. To address this gap, we propose ReSMap, a redundancy-aware camera-satellite fusion framework for robust online HD map construction. Our framework explicitly leverages satellite images as a complementary source to improve robustness against camera failures. A Predict-then-Fuse (PTF) module fuses camera and satellite BEV features by using each branch's own prediction confidence as a per-pixel reliability weight, with no learned router. An Object-Centric Cooperative Multi-modal Query (OCMQ) decoder then extends this per-modality separation to the query level: each modality cross-attends only to its own BEV, and per-modality updates are merged through a learned per-token gate. Our model achieves state-of-the-art performance on nuScenes across all splits and range settings. More importantly, it also sets the new state-of-the-art under camera-failure scenarios, demonstrating that satellite images serve not only as a strong prior but as practical redundancy for robust online HD mapping.

</details>


### [45] [EndoS3:Endoscopic Semantic Splatting SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04358)
*Mona Sheikh Zeinoddin,Benjamin Wright,Zafer Tandogdu,Greg Shaw,Evangelos Mazomenos,Danail Stoyanov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于高斯泼溅的语义SLAM框架EndoS3，用实例掩码统一引导相机跟踪、可变形重建与语义建图，并发布CRCD++数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D手术场景理解中语义SLAM研究不足：一是需并行解决相机位姿跟踪、高保真可变形重建与语义建图三项难题；二是缺乏能在接近真实手术条件下全面评估这三项任务的真值数据集。

Method: EndoS3以实例掩码引导三项任务：(1) 实例级运动分解模块通过补偿相机运动后的残差光流区分静态/动态实例，并考虑光流噪声与相邻实例运动比较；(2) 相机针对静态高斯进行跟踪，实例感知warp场为每个动态实例配置独立控制节点与变形网络；(3) 语义节点分配模块按实例尺寸与非刚性程度重分配固定节点预算。同时构建CRCD++离体胆囊切除数据集，含RGB-D帧、相机位姿与实例标注。

Result: 在CRCD++与SemSup数据集上，EndoS3在跟踪、重建与语义建图三方面均超越现有通用及手术语义SLAM方法。

Conclusion: 语义感知的运动与形变建模对手术语义SLAM至关重要；EndoS3与CRCD++为内窥镜几何与语义理解提供了方法与基准。

Abstract: Purpose: Despite advances in 3D surgical scene understanding, semantic simultaneous localisation and mapping (SLAM) remains under-explored for two key reasons. First, it requires solving three challenging tasks in parallel: camera pose tracking, high-fidelity deformable reconstruction, and semantic mapping, making progress particularly demanding. Second, the scarcity of datasets providing the necessary ground truth to evaluate all three tasks comprehensively under conditions that closely reflect real surgical procedures environments. Method: We propose EndoS3, a Gaussian Splatting-based semantic SLAM framework that uses instance masks to guide all three tasks. An instance-level motion decomposition module separates static and dynamic instances by evaluating residual flow after compensating for camera motion, accounting for flow noise and comparing motion across neighbouring instances. The camera is tracked against the static Gaussians, while an instance-aware warp field gives each dynamic instance its own control nodes and deformation network. A semantic node allocation module redistributes a fixed node budget across instances according to their size and non-rigidity. We also introduce CRCD++, an ex-vivo Cholecystectomy dataset with RGB-D frames, camera pose and instance annotations for full semantic SLAM evaluation. Results: EndoS3 surpasses all state-of-the-art general-purpose and surgical semantic SLAM methods in tracking, reconstruction and semantic mapping on the CRCD++ and SemSup datasets. Conclusion: Semantic-aware motion and deformation modelling is vital to surgical semantic SLAM. EndoS3 and CRCD++ offer a method and benchmark for improved geometric and semantic understanding in endoscopy.

</details>


### [46] [IGSW: Multi-Weather Urban Scene Reconstruction with Intrinsic-Decoupled Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04374)
*Zhongquan Wang,Long Cao,Peijia Xie,Senlei Ma,Jie Li,Guofa Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: IGSW：两阶段框架，先用本征解耦的4D高斯场景建模，再做结构保持的天气精修，实现高保真4D场景天气编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式图像编辑与动态高斯表示结合可实现多天气场景重建，但天气编辑图像常含结构畸变；直接拟合其外观会把教师图像误差传播进4D场景，破坏原始结构与时间连续性。

Method: Stage I 将背景高斯外观解耦为天气共享的albedo与法线，以及天气特定的辐照度（irradiance）与残差；以扩散图像编辑模型生成的天气教师图像和扩散逆渲染器估计的本征图为特征级软监督。Stage II 引入结构保持门控，结合语义冲突与跨天气本征差异抑制不可靠教师区域，并用逐高斯天气传输变量与目标天气分支增强目标天气外观。

Result: 在nuScenes与Waymo上，相比对比方法取得最高的内容相似度与语义一致性、最低的时间误差，同时保留明显的天气相关光照与大气效果。

Conclusion: 解耦本征建模配合结构保持的门控精修，可在抑制教师图像结构误差传播的同时实现高保真、时序一致的4D场景多天气编辑。

Abstract: High-fidelity weather editing of 4D scenes can support end-to-end training and closed-loop simulation for autonomous driving. Combining generative image editing with dynamic Gaussian representations enables multi-weather scene reconstruction, but weather-edited images often contain structural distortions. Directly fitting their appearance may propagate errors from teacher images into the reconstructed 4D scene, compromising the original scene structure and temporal continuity. To address these challenges, we propose IGSW, a two-stage framework that combines intrinsic-decoupled 4D Gaussian scene modeling with structure-preserving weather refinement. In Stage I, background Gaussian appearance is decomposed into weather-shared albedo and normals and weather-specific irradiance and residuals. Weather teacher images generated by a diffusion-based image editing model and intrinsic maps estimated by a diffusion-based inverse renderer provide feature-level soft supervision for stable structure and illumination changes. In Stage II, a structure-preservation gate combines semantic conflicts with cross-weather intrinsic discrepancies to suppress unreliable teacher regions. Per-Gaussian weather transport variables and target-weather branches then strengthen the desired weather appearance over the intrinsic-decoupled scene. Experiments on nuScenes and Waymo show that IGSW achieves the highest content similarity and semantic consistency and the lowest temporal error among the compared methods, while retaining pronounced weather-dependent illumination and atmosphere. The project page is available at https://akaxi6.github.io/IGSW/ .

</details>


### [47] [Understanding Backdoor Attacks in Scene Graph Generation via Global Context Pathway](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04379)
*Xiaoguang Chang,Tuo Chen,Chengze Jiang,Teng Wang,Changyin Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究场景图生成（SGG）的训练期后门风险，提出针对"涉及特定物体类别的配对级关系"的选择性攻击方法及三轴评估协议，并揭示显式全局上下文通路是后门效应的主要载体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SGG的后门风险尚未被探索。与分类任务不同，SGG具有配对级攻击面，可选择性污染涉及指定类别的三元组（主语-谓词-宾语）；但SGG依赖局部证据，全局放置的触发器可能难以奏效。而近期SGG架构引入全局上下文建模，可能反而放大该攻击可行性，故需系统研究并厘清全局上下文通路在其中的作用。

Method: 首先实例化一种有效的配对级选择性后门攻击方法，并设计三轴评估协议刻画后门行为；在Visual Genome上对代表性开放词表与闭集SGG模型进行实验；随后开展机理分析：(1) 推理时用干净全局上下文表示替换被污染的表示；(2) 在OvSGTR家族中进行训练期干预以抑制该通路中的触发器信号。

Result: 攻击在开放词表与闭集SGG模型上均有效。机理分析表明，显式全局上下文通路是各模型后门效应的主要载体：推理时替换为干净全局表示可使OvSGTR家族与RelationFormer的攻击成功率分别下降100%与97%。OvSGTR家族的训练期干预显示，抑制该通路触发器信号要么降低攻击成功率，要么显著损害模型效用（utility），存在效用-安全权衡。

Conclusion: 显式全局上下文设计可能在SGG架构中引入后门风险：其既是攻击得以生效的关键通道，也使得简单防御难以在不损失性能的前提下奏效。研究提示SGG模型设计需在引入全局上下文时同步考虑安全性。

Abstract: Scene Graph Generation (SGG) produces structured scene representations, yet its training-time backdoor risk remains unexplored. Unlike classification, SGG exposes a pair-level attack surface that enables selective corruption of relations involving a designated object category. However, because SGG largely relies on localized evidence, globally placed triggers may face difficulty in manipulating such predictions. Recent SGG architectures increasingly incorporate global context to improve scene modeling, which may in turn facilitate such attacks. In this paper, we systematically study this backdoor risk and investigate the role of the global context pathway in backdoor behavior. Concretely, we first instantiate this attack with an effective method, and propose a three-axis evaluation protocol to characterize backdoor behavior. Experiments on Visual Genome with representative open-vocabulary and closed-set SGG models demonstrate the effectiveness of the attack. Mechanistic analyses reveal that the explicit global context pathway serves as a major carrier of the backdoor effect across the evaluated SGG models. In particular, replacing the poisoned global context representation with its clean counterpart during inference sharply suppresses the attack, yielding 100% and 97% reductions in attack success for the OvSGTR family and RelationFormer, respectively. Moreover, training-time interventions in the OvSGTR family show that suppressing trigger signals in this pathway either reduces attack success or substantially degrades utility. Together, these findings suggest that explicit global context designs may pose a backdoor risk in SGG architectures.

</details>


### [48] [StepX-OPD: Rethinking Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04386)
*Chenru Wang,Xuanyang Zhang,Yucheng Han,Wenbo Nie,Wei Cheng,Xianfang Zeng,Gang YU,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出StepX-OPD，将能力蒸馏与少步蒸馏合并为单阶段：学生在自身rollout状态上接受冻结专家监督，并回归低噪声段速度，四步即超越四十步Flow-OPD，训练GPU时数减少约37%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可部署的T2I扩散模型须同时具备指令跟随、文本渲染、人类偏好等能力并支持少步采样。现有做法分两阶段串行：先用多教师on-policy蒸馏整合能力，再以固定全步教师做步数蒸馏。问题在于：所有被评估的全步模型压缩后性能下降，且第二阶段必须等第一阶段结束。

Method: StepX-OPD合并两阶段：单一学生在学习少步采样的同时获取全部能力，冻结专家在学生自身rollout所访问的状态上监督，而非其从未运行的长轨迹。基于rollout的KL分解显示速度失配在干净端（低噪声）最重，故额外在rollout低噪声段回归学生速度。另发现能力整合会诱发模型在无文本要求的提示上把提示词写入图像，提出降低该现象的变体。

Result: 在SD3.5-Medium与FLUX.1-dev上，按指令跟随、文本渲染、图像质量与人类偏好评估；两种架构上StepX-OPD在四步方法中归一化平均分最高，并超过四十步Flow-OPD；SD3.5-Medium上训练GPU时数比"序列式DiffusionOPD+步蒸馏"少约37%。

Conclusion: 单阶段联合优化能力与少步采样可行且更高效，避免了串行蒸馏的压缩损失与阶段依赖，为少步可部署扩散模型提供统一训练范式。

Abstract: A deployable text-to-image diffusion model must hold several capabilities at once, such as instruction following, text rendering and human preference, and must sample in a few steps. Capabilities and few-step sampling are built in separate stages: a full-step model first consolidates capabilities through multi-teacher on-policy distillation, then serves as a fixed teacher for step distillation. Every full-step model we evaluate scores lower once compressed, and the second stage cannot begin until the first finishes. We introduce StepX-OPD, which merges the two stages: one student acquires every capability while learning to sample in a few steps, so frozen specialists supervise it on the states its own rollout visits rather than on a longer trajectory it never runs. Because a decomposition of the rollout's KL weights velocity mismatch most heavily near the clean end, we also regress the student's velocity on the low-noise part of the rollout. Separately, we find that capability integration makes models write prompt words into images on prompts that never asked for text, and we give a variant that lowers it. We evaluate on SD3.5-Medium and FLUX.1-dev across instruction following, text rendering, image quality and human preference. On both architectures, StepX-OPD attains the highest normalized average score among the four-step methods compared and exceeds the forty-step Flow-OPD model, and on SD3.5-Medium it trains with about 37% fewer GPU-hours than sequential DiffusionOPD followed by step distillation.

</details>


### [49] [Spin Spherical Fourier Neural Operators: Efficient Equivariant Learning for Vector and Spin Fields on the Sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04389)
*Kevin Mulder,Matthew A. Price,Eirini Leftaki,Jason D. McEwen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出自旋SFNO（S²FNO），用提升的球面卷积将SFNO扩展到任意自旋场，实现精确的旋转等变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球面上诸多物理场（风场、CMB偏振、弱引力透镜剪切）为自旋场，局部标架旋转时相位改变，现有SFNO仅处理标量场。

Method: 将自旋场提升为旋转群上的Mackey函数作为概念框架，通过调和表示及球面与旋转群上的采样定理实现精确高效计算，构建自旋等变球面卷积层并组装为S²FNO。

Result: 旋转等变性在机器精度下成立；在已有基准上达到最优性能；新基准上自旋感知模型优于标量模型。

Conclusion: S²FNO为任意自旋球面场提供精确旋转等变的神经算子框架，验证了自旋感知建模的必要性。

Abstract: Many fields of physical interest on the sphere, such as wind, the polarisation of the cosmic microwave background and the shear of weak gravitational lensing, are spin fields, whose values change phase when the local frame rotates. We extend spherical Fourier neural operators (SFNOs) to fields of any spin, through lifted spherical convolutions that are exactly rotationally equivariant. Lifting spin fields to Mackey functions on the rotation group provides the conceptual framework, while computation is performed exactly and efficiently through their harmonic representation and by appealing to sampling theorems on the sphere and rotation group. We construct spin SFNOs (S$^2$FNOs) from these building blocks. We demonstrate empirically that rotational equivariance holds to machine precision for these layers. We achieve state-of-the-art performance on existing benchmarks, and introduce new benchmarks on which spin-aware models outperform their scalar counterparts.

</details>


### [50] [Auditing Targetless Camera-LiDAR Extrinsic Correction before Recursive Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04397)
*Bo Ma,Weiqi Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出将无目标外参校正拆分为校正质量、置信度排序、递归风险三个独立审计阶段，并用日志不相交协议在Argoverse 2合成外参漂移下评估；结果显示各阶段均未达到可部署门槛，仅支持"可审计分离"这一方法学结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机-激光雷达融合在投影、关联与递归状态估计中反复使用外参变换，因此一个无目标（targetless）外参校正若直接进入下游消费者，误差会被递归放大。作者试图回答：校正结果是否应仅在通过校正质量、置信度排序与递归风险三重独立审计后才被下游采纳。

Method: 采用日志不相交（log-disjoint）评估协议，在Argoverse 2上注入合成外参漂移并按严重程度分级；对比单帧Canny对齐与多帧对齐，另设零注入检查（零漂移时应保持真值外参）、求解器分歧度AUROC与风险-覆盖率分析，以及15条日志的最终系统角色测试（锁定保守策略 vs. Always-correct vs. Raw），将跨数据集与里程计实验定位为有界诊断而非性能主张。

Result: 27条测试日志上：轻度漂移下单帧Canny对齐反而有害；多帧对齐在重度档将旋转误差改善2.52°但平移误差恶化0.177 m，声明式联合误差幅值改进为负（-0.158，95% CI [-0.223, -0.098]），故旋转增益不能视为六自由度校正成功；零注入检查使估计偏离真值外参；所选划分上的求解器分歧AUROC未能给出经校验的非零覆盖率风险-覆盖率工作点。最终系统角色中，锁定保守策略不接纳任何候选、等价于Raw，而Always-correct增大轨迹误差。

Conclusion: 结果支持"校正—置信度—递归污染"三者可分离审计的框架，但未建立可部署的标定门控、里程计或SLAM的正向改进、地图一致性或闭环安全性；本质上是负结果与评估方法学贡献，而非系统性能贡献。

Abstract: Camera-LiDAR fusion repeatedly uses an extrinsic transform in projection, association, and recursive state estimation. We study whether a targetless correction should be admitted to a downstream consumer only after separate audits of correction quality, confidence ranking, and recursive risk. Our log-disjoint protocol evaluates these stages under synthetic extrinsic drift on Argoverse 2 and treats cross-dataset and odometry experiments as bounded diagnostics.
  On 27 test-split logs, single-frame Canny alignment is harmful in the mild tier. Multi-frame alignment improves severe-tier rotation by 2.52 degrees but worsens translation by 0.177 m; its declared joint error-magnitude improvement is also negative (-0.158, 95% CI [-0.223, -0.098]), so the rotation gain is not a six-degree-of-freedom correction success. A zero-injection check moves away from the true extrinsic, and solver-disagreement AUROC on selected splits does not yield a validated nonzero-coverage risk-coverage operating point.
  In a 15-log final system role, the locked conservative policy admits no candidate and equals Raw, whereas Always-correct increases trajectory error. These results support an auditable separation of correction, confidence, and recursive contamination; they do not establish a deployable calibration gate, positive odometry or SLAM improvement, map consistency, or closed-loop safety.

</details>


### [51] [DR-LiteNet: A Lightweight Explainable Hybrid CNN Framework for Imbalanced Diabetic Retinopathy Grading via Adaptive SMOTE Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04405)
*Nishi Kanta Paul,Md Shihabul Islam Shovo,Audrija Chowdhury,Israt Jahan Esha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DR-LiteNet：EfficientNetB0深度特征与51维手工病灶描述子拼接成1331维混合嵌入，训练时用ADASYN合成少数类特征，两阶段训练，在APTOS 2019五折交叉验证取得88.77%准确率、QWK 0.884，仅4.69M参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化糖网分级受两大问题制约：五级严重度上的严重类别不平衡，以及大规模网络在资源受限临床环境中的高昂计算成本；本文希望同时解决二者。

Method: 双分支混合CNN：CNN分支用EfficientNetB0提取1280维深层特征；手工特征分支生成51维紧凑病灶描述子（血管密度、渗出强度、纹理熵、HSV颜色直方图）；拼接得到1331维混合嵌入，在特征空间（非图像空间）施加ADASYN以合成少数类向量；两阶段训练——先在均衡特征上预热分类头，再端到端微调；用Grad-CAM提供事后临床可解释性。

Result: APTOS 2019五折分层交叉验证：准确率88.77%±0.61，QWK 0.884±0.010，宏F1 0.792±0.008；仅4.69M参数，较ResNet50（23.5M）的ΔQWK=0.022，可在单张Colab T4 GPU上运行。

Conclusion: 轻量双分支混合特征+特征级重采样是兼顾不平衡鲁棒性、性能与部署可行性的有效方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Automated diabetic retinopathy (DR) grading from colour fundus photographs is held back by two compounding challenges: severe class imbalance across five severity grades and the heavy computational cost of large scale architectures in resource constrained clinical settings. We introduce DR-LiteNet, a dual branch hybrid convolutional neural network that tackles both problems at once. The CNN branch extracts 1,280 dimensional deep features through EfficientNetB0, while the handcrafted (HC) branch produces a compact 51 dimensional lesion descriptor capturing vessel density, exudate intensity, texture entropy, and HSV colour histograms. Concatenating both branches yields a 1,331-d hybrid embedding, on which Adaptive SMOTE (ADASYN) is applied during training to synthesise minority class feature vectors, correcting distributional skew without touching the image space. A two phase training protocol first warms up the classification head on balanced features, then fine tunes the full network end to end. Grad CAM saliency maps deliver post-hoc clinical explainability. Tested on APTOS 2019 under five fold stratified cross validation, DR-LiteNet reaches 88.77% +/- 0.61 accuracy, a quadratic weighted kappa (QWK) of 0.884 +/- 0.010, and a macro F1 of 0.792 +/- 0.008 with only 4.69 M parameters beating ResNet50 (23.5 M) by DeltaQWK = 0.022 while staying practical on a single Google Colab T4 GPU. The source code of this project is available at: https://github.com/Nishi-Kanta-Paul/DR-LiteNet.

</details>


### [52] [LCM-UNet: Scale-Selective Mamba with Reparameterizable Multi-Basis Local Modeling for Lightweight Skin Lesion Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04409)
*Md Shihabul Islam Shovo,Nishi Kanta Paul,Dip Kumar Saha,Kishor Morol*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出33,474参数的超轻量U形网络LCM-UNet，将双向Mamba限制在1/16及更粗分辨率阶段、细尺度用局部卷积，并引入可重参数化多基适配器与逐级skip增益。在ISIC 2017/2018/PH²上Dice为88.62%/89.73%/90.46%，两个ISIC测试集排名第一、PH²第二；消融显示移除1/8解码阶段Mamba仅代价0.03个百分点Dice，适配器带来+0.48个百分点且不增加部署开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原发性或移动端皮肤镜部署需要紧凑分割网络；现有超轻Mamba U-Net虽提供全局上下文，但全局建模开销随每个Mamba块的分辨率增长，且其局部分支仅依赖单一方形卷积核，建模能力受限。目标是同时压缩全局建模成本并增强局部特征表达。

Method: 1) 全部skip在原生分辨率融合；2) 双向Mamba仅用于输入1/16及更粗分辨率的编码/解码阶段，更细尺度改用局部卷积；3) 多基可重参数化适配器：训练时每个局部分支并联膨胀、水平、垂直深度卷积核，推理前折叠进宿主卷积核；4) 每个skip连接学习阶段特定增益。9个网络在ISIC 2017/2018官方划分下采用统一协议重训练，PH²用于外部验证，消融在开发划分上进行。

Result: Dice：ISIC 2017 88.62%、ISIC 2018 89.73%、PH² 90.46%，在两个ISIC测试集上排名第一、PH²第二。消融：在原生分辨率宿主中移除1/8分辨率解码阶段的Mamba，减少63%的Mamba操作（占总操作5.3%），Dice仅变化−0.03个百分点；多基适配器较单一宿主卷积核提升Dice 0.48个百分点，且不增加部署参数与运算量。

Conclusion: Mamba的全局建模在高分辨率阶段性价比极低，而局部多基可重参数化适配器是几乎零成本的增益来源。LCM-UNet在保持极低参数量的同时在异构基准上具备竞争力，可作为资源受限场景的部署候选；但延迟目前仅在GPU上测得，实际端侧（移动/边缘）评估仍待补充。

Abstract: Accurate lesion segmentation underpins automated dermoscopic analysis: masks enable measurements of asymmetry, border and size. Deployment in primary care or on mobile devices requires a compact network. Existing ultra-light Mamba U-Nets offer global context, but global-modeling cost depends on each Mamba block's resolution, while their local branches rely on a single square kernel. We introduce LCM-UNet, a 33,474-parameter U-shaped network that fuses every skip at its native resolution, restricts bidirectional Mamba to stages at one-sixteenth of the input resolution or coarser, and uses local convolutions at finer scales. A multi-basis reparameterizable adapter augments each local branch during training with dilated, horizontal and vertical depthwise kernels, then folds them into the host kernel before inference. Each skip connection also learns a stage-specific gain. Nine networks were retrained on the official ISIC 2017 and ISIC 2018 partitions under a harmonized protocol, with PH$^2$ reserved for external validation and ablations run on a development split. LCM-UNet achieved Dice scores of 88.62%, 89.73% and 90.46% on ISIC 2017, ISIC 2018 and PH$^2$, respectively, ranking first on both ISIC test sets and second on PH$^2$ among the compared models. In the native-resolution host, removing Mamba from the one-eighth-resolution decoder stage eliminated 63% of Mamba operations, corresponding to 5.3% of all operations, with a Dice change of $-0.03$ percentage points. The adapter improved Dice by 0.48 percentage points over a single host kernel without adding deployed parameters or operations. With latency so far measured only on a GPU, LCM-UNet is a candidate for resource-constrained deployment.

</details>


### [53] [SphereFlow: Missing Modality Imputation via Geometric Transport on Hypersphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04414)
*Huihan Wang,Juncheng Wang,Yuxiang Feng,Wenlong Hou,Guoqi Yu,Chao Xu,Yang Liu,Baigui Sun,Shujun Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SphereFlow：将各模态经冻结自监督编码器投影到共享超球潜空间，使跨模态补全变为球面上近邻点间的短程几何传输；通过"数据即源"与时间加权解码器损失解决两处几何冲突，在BraTS补全与下游分割上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缺失模态补全方法将每个模态视为处于各自的潜流形上，迫使生成器学习模态间长距离、无结构的跳跃，生成难度大且保真度受限。

Method: 用冻结的自监督编码器将所有模态投射到统一超球潜空间，用flow matching做跨模态传输。针对两个几何冲突做改进：(1)以观测模态潜向量替换标准高斯噪声源，使速度场只学习跨模态位移；(2)对解码器采用时间加权损失，将其定义域扩展到流轨迹经过的近球面区域。并从理论上证明数据即源使最坏情况偏离流形量由随维度增长的量降为受模态间相似度控制的、与维度无关的小上界。

Result: 理论上界由维度相关降为维度无关；BraTS多模态MRI补全与CT-MRI翻译各设置下生成保真度均达SOTA；用补全数据做脑肿瘤分割，弥补约80%与全模态oracle的差距。

Conclusion: 在共享超球潜空间中做短程几何传输是缺失模态补全的有效范式，配合几何一致的噪声源与解码器域扩展可显著提升生成 fidelity 与下游可用性。

Abstract: Missing modality imputation aims to synthesize unobserved modalities from observed ones. Existing methods treat each modality as living on its own latent manifold, forcing the generator to learn a long, unstructured jump between them. We present SphereFlow, which takes a fundamentally different view: by projecting all modalities into a shared hyperspherical latent space via a frozen self-supervised encoder, cross-modal imputation reduces to short-range geometric transport between nearby points on the sphere. Realizing this idea with flow matching, however, exposes two geometric conflicts: the standard Gaussian noise source is catastrophically mismatched with the unit-norm data manifold, and the linear interpolation path departs the sphere surface into a region where the decoder has no training signal. We resolve both with simple, geometry-aware modifications: replacing the noise source with the observed-modality latent so that the velocity field learns only the cross-modal displacement, and training the decoder with a time-weighted loss that extends its domain to the near-surface region traversed by the flow. We prove that this data-as-source formulation reduces the worst-case off-manifold deviation from a quantity that grows with the latent dimension to a small, dimension-independent bound governed by inter-modality similarity. Experiments on BraTS multi-modal MRI imputation and CT-MRI translation demonstrate state-of-the-art generation fidelity across all settings, while downstream brain tumor segmentation with our imputed data closes roughly eighty percent of the gap to the full-modality oracle. Code is available at https://github.com/WTHH031230/SphereFlow.

</details>


### [54] [Efficient and Faithful High-Resolution Image Editing with Sparse Cross-Resolution Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04416)
*Maosen Zhao,Xianfang Zeng,Pengtao Chen,Peng Ye,Gang Yu,Jiayuan Fan,Tao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FLASH：一种基于稀疏跨分辨率去噪的原生高分辨率指令式图像编辑器，在2K/4K下兼顾编辑轨迹保真与低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令式图像编辑器多停留在1K，而实际编辑需求日益要求2K/4K高质量输出。稠密高分辨率适配虽保留编辑轨迹但目标-源注意力代价高昂；先1K编辑再做外部超分虽便宜，却使细节恢复与编辑轨迹脱节。

Method: 稀疏跨分辨率去噪：冻结的1K分支提供语义与结构锚点，目标分辨率分支在同一Transformer内重建局部细节。非对称拓扑——保留完整1K注意力，用空间对齐的低分辨率噪声目标状态引导高分辨率查询，并将高分辨率目标-参考交互限制在局部Halo邻域。辅以分辨率特定的扩散状态映射与空间YaRN，使预训练编辑器适配更大网格。另提供免训练渐进路径：用1K前缀替代早期高分辨率去噪，并在同一编辑器内完成精修。

Result: 在VINS-4KEval上，直接模型在2K与4K下均取得对比方法中最强的结构保真度；R=1的4K前向基准显示总前向执行快4.2×、注意力快25.3×（相对稠密目标-参考注意力）；渐进推理使端到端4K延迟降低30.5%，质量仅小幅下降。

Conclusion: 通过稀疏跨分辨率去噪与非对称注意力拓扑，FLASH在保持编辑轨迹一致性的同时实现原生高分辨率编辑，显著提升效率，并可经免训练渐进推理进一步降低延迟。

Abstract: Instruction-based image editors still primarily operate at 1K, whereas practical editing increasingly requires faithful 2K/4K outputs. Dense high-resolution adaptation preserves the editing trajectory but incurs expensive target-source attention; editing at 1K followed by external refinement is cheaper but can separate detail restoration from the editing trajectory. We introduce FLASH, a native high-resolution editor based on sparse cross-resolution denoising. A frozen 1K branch provides a semantic and structural anchor, while a target-resolution branch reconstructs local detail within the same Transformer. An asymmetric topology retains full 1K attention, guides high-resolution queries with spatially aligned low-resolution noisy-target states, and confines high-resolution target-reference interaction to local Halo neighborhoods. Resolution-specific diffusion-state mapping and spatial YaRN adapt the pretrained editor to larger grids. An optional, training-free progressive path replaces early high-resolution denoising with a 1K prefix and completes refinement in the same editor. On VINS-4KEval, the direct model achieves the strongest structural fidelity among the compared methods at both 2K and 4K. A separate 4K forward benchmark with R = 1 records 4.2x faster total forward execution and 25.3x faster attention than dense target-reference attention. Progressive inference reduces end-to-end 4K latency by 30.5%, with a modest quality cost.

</details>


### [55] [VidUEU-Agent: A Data-Curation Agent for Multimodal Understanding, Editing, and Unified Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04418)
*Tengju Ru,Weitong Lian,Zecong Tang,Lingyi Meng,Haoran Li,Zhejun Cui,Yichen Zhu,Hangshuo Cao,Qi Kang,Yechi Liu,Kaixuan Wang,Yu-Jie Yuan,Chunwei Wang,Yu Zhang,Bo Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 VidUEU-Agent 自动流水线，从原始视频合成多模态理解、编辑与统一任务指令数据，并构建 100K 数据集 VidUEU-100K。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态学习受限于高质量指令数据的稀缺与高昂标注成本；原始视频虽丰富，但难以转化为精确指令数据。

Method: 四阶段流水线：全局感知过滤低质量镜头、挖掘关键帧与多模态信号、构建元数据并路由合成指令样本；智能体框架可依据下游训练结果自主迭代数据分布，优化质量、平衡性与训练效果。

Result: 构建 VidUEU-100K（10 万训练样本，覆盖多领域多任务）；基线模型微调后在多个代表性基准上取得显著且一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 智能体驱动的数据合成框架与数据集有效，可支撑综合多模态能力发展。

Abstract: The advancement of multimodal learning is heavily constrained by the scarcity and high annotation cost of high-quality instruction data. While raw videos offer an abundant and dynamic source of visual knowledge, transforming them into precise instruction data remains highly challenging. To bridge this gap, we present VidUEU-Agent, an automated pipeline that synthesizes instruction data from raw videos for multimodal understanding, editing, and unified tasks. VidUEU-Agent follows a four-stage pipeline: it performs global perception to filter low-quality shots, mines keyframes and multimodal signals, constructs metadata and routes the metadata to synthesize instruction samples. More importantly, our agent framework can autonomously iterate the data distribution based on downstream training results, thereby optimizing data quality, balance, and training effectiveness. Scaling this pipeline, we construct VidUEU-100K, a large-scale dataset of 100K training samples spanning multiple domains and tasks, providing a robust foundation for developing comprehensive multimodal capabilities. Extensive experiments demonstrate that fine-tuning baseline models on VidUEU-100K yields significant and consistent performance gains across diverse representative benchmarks, validating the effectiveness of our proposed agent framework and dataset.

</details>


### [56] [GVT3D: Gesture-Vision-Text Token Enrichment for 3D Embodied Reference Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04422)
*Vimarsh Shah,Dulanga Weerakoon,Ajinkya Deshpande,Saksham Agarwal,Nilesh Bhatia,Neel Kiran Naik,Parth Shah,Sravan Danda,Sanjay E. Sarma,Archan Misra,Vigneshwaran Subbaraju,Sougata Sen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GVT3D通过Embodied Fusion模块在共享嵌入空间中联合建模语言、手势与视觉点云以实现跨模态对齐，并引入foveation（仅处理指向方向粗定位区域内点云）降低计算量，精度提升约8%，执行速度提升2.95倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然人机交互需机器人理解3D环境中的语言与手势以支持取放、检索等任务。现有3D-ERU模型存在两点不足：(1) 缺乏跨模态表示对齐；(2) 处理开销随点云规模增长而显著上升。

Method: 提出GVT3D：包含Embodied Fusion模块，将语言、手势、视觉输入在同一共享嵌入空间中联合建模以促进跨模态对齐；并引入foveation策略，根据估计的指向方向确定粗略区域，仅对该区域内点云进行选择性处理。

Result: 相比先前3D-ERU模型，准确率提升约8%；foveation在保持相当精度的前提下实现2.95倍加速。

Conclusion: 跨模态对齐与基于指向方向的选择性点云处理可同时提升3D-ERU的精度与效率，为高效3D具身指代理解提供可行方案。

Abstract: Natural human-robot interaction requires robots to interpret language and gestures within a 3D environment to support tasks such as pick-and-place and object retrieval. 3D Embodied Reference Understanding (3D-ERU) supports this by identifying the 3D location of a target object from a scene (point-cloud) containing a human pointing gesture and an accompanying language description. However, current 3D-ERU models lack alignment of cross-modal representations and incur high processing costs that increase with the point-cloud's size. To address this, we propose GVT3D, which employs an Embodied Fusion module that jointly models language, gesture, and vision inputs within a shared embedding space, promoting cross-modal alignment. We additionally introduce a foveation approach that selectively processes the point cloud within a coarse region around the estimated pointing direction to reduce computations. GVT3D achieves ~8% higher accuracy and foveation enables 2.95x faster execution while maintaining comparable accuracy, relative to prior 3D-ERU models.

</details>


### [57] [SWAP-INR: Implicit Neural Representation with Switchable Attention Paths for Time-varying Multivariate Scientific Data Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04423)
*Hyunsoo Son,Jeonghyun Noh,Chaoli Wang,Won-Ki Jeong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SWAP-INR，通过可切换注意力路径与自适应SIREN改进隐式神经表示，实现多变量时变仿真数据高保真压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量科学仿真数据规模巨大，存储瓶颈严重；现有INR方法难以显式建模跨变量交互，也难以适应各物理变量差异化的频谱特性。

Method: 两大组件：(1) SWAP模块——用跨变量注意力层显式建模变量间交互，再依据确定性的、由损失驱动的判据将每个变量路由至注意力或非注意力路径，并剪枝未激活层以实现结构压缩；(2) A2-SIREN——以通道级可学习调制替代刚性频率缩放，以可忽略开销适配异质物理特征。结合训练后16比特量化。

Result: 在小型模块化反应堆（SMR）数据集上达到SOTA，在公开物理仿真数据集上保持竞争力，同时维持受限内存占用。

Conclusion: 跨变量注意力与自适应频率调制可有效提升多变量INR压缩的保真度与参数效率，代码已开源。

Abstract: Visualizing multivariate scientific simulation data is essential for understanding complex physical phenomena, yet the massive scale of such data imposes a severe storage bottleneck in high-performance computing environments. Implicit neural representations (INRs) have emerged as a promising continuous alternative to traditional lossy compressors, but existing methods struggle to explicitly capture cross-variable interactions and to adapt to the diverse spectral characteristics of physical variables. To address these limitations, we propose SWAP-INR, an architecture for high-fidelity compression of multivariate, time-varying simulation data. SWAP-INR introduces two key components: (1) The switchable attention path (SWAP) module explicitly models cross-variable interactions through a cross-variable attention layer, then routes each variable to an attention or non-attention path by a deterministic, loss-driven criterion, and prunes the inactive layers for structural compression. (2) The adaptability amplification SIREN (A2-SIREN) replaces rigid frequency scaling with channel-wise learnable modulation, adapting to heterogeneous physical features with negligible overhead. Combined with post-training 16-bit quantization, SWAP-INR achieves state-of-the-art performance on small modular reactor (SMR) datasets and remains competitive on public physics simulation datasets, while maintaining a constrained memory footprint. Our code is https://github.com/hvcl/SWAP-INR.

</details>


### [58] [A Systematic Point Cloud Edge Detection Framework for Automatic Aircraft Skin Milling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04435)
*Zijie Wu,Yaonan Wang,He Xie,Mingtao Feng,Haotian Wu,Chao Ding,Ajmal Mian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出面向飞机蒙皮自动铣边的边缘检测框架，由空间切向连续性(STC)边缘概率检测器与分层分支搜索(HBS)方法组成，在合成模型与真实蒙皮工件上优于基线并获得准确铣削边缘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有边缘检测方法多针对传统定义的边缘提取任务，忽视了铣边作业的关键系统性需求，难以直接支撑飞机蒙皮自动铣边的工程应用。

Method: 先构建基于空间切向连续性(Spatial Tangent Continuity, STC)的边缘概率检测器，为边缘提取提供必要参考；再提出分层分支搜索(Hierarchical Branch Searching, HBS)方法，从原始点云中分层剥离目标铣削边缘，包含分支主干生成、分支延伸、分支修剪三个递进步骤。

Result: 在合成模型与飞机蒙皮工件上验证，所提方法优于其他基线方法，能够给出满足铣边任务需求的准确边缘。

Conclusion: 该框架将边缘检测与铣边系统性需求结合，可为飞机蒙皮自动铣边提供可靠的点云边缘提取方案。

Abstract: The edge detection technique is an essential step for aircraft skin milling in aviation manufacturing. Most of the current detection methods focus on traditionally defined edge extraction tasks but disregard the crucial systematic requirement of edge milling. In this paper, we proposed a novel edge detection framework for automatic edge milling of aircraft skins. First, an edge probability detector is proposed by the spatial tangent continuity (STC) to provide the essential reference. Second, we propose a hierarchical branch searching (HBS) method to hierarchically strip the desired milling edges from the raw point cloud, which consists of three graded progressive steps: branch backbone generation, branch extension, and branch pruning. We demonstrate the performance of the proposed method on both synthetic models and aircraft skin workpieces. The proposed method outperforms the other baselines and shows accurate edges for the edge milling task.

</details>


### [59] [Entropy-Gated Dynamic Rectification for Object Hallucination in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04438)
*Riasad Alvi,Md. Asaduzzaman Shuvo,Md. Ahashan Habib,Md. Faiyaz Abdullah Sayeedi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出熵门控动态矫正（EGDR），用前一步已有logits的预测熵作为token级控制信号来调节潜在引导的门控阈值，在不增加前向计算的前提下进一步降低LVLM幻觉率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM常命名图片中不存在的物体；潜在引导（latent steering）能在单次前向中缓解该问题，但固定门控对解码早期与后期步骤采用同一判据。作者观察到自由生成过程中预测熵波动剧烈，而承诺短答案的状态落在狭窄的高置信区间，二者存在时序不匹配，可被利用。

Method: 将不确定性作为因果可得、token局部的控制信号接入现有引导门控：当由前一步已可得logits导出的熵较高时降低干预阈值，熵较低时保持已校准的工作点；仅替换原静态门控的常数阈值为动态阈值。

Result: LLaVA-1.5-7B上，静态门控将CHAIR CS/CI从53.00/15.30降至32.00/14.30，EGDR进一步降至29.00/13.60；在匹配58%干预率下静态对照为30.50/14.00。增益在LLaVA-NeXT-7B上成立且随生成长度预算增大而扩大，POPE及通用多模态能力基本不变，无额外前向，延迟增加约2%。

Conclusion: 以预测熵动态调节门控阈值是低成本、有效的幻觉抑制手段，优于等干预率的静态基线，并在更大生成预算下收益更明显。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) routinely name objects that are absent from the image. Latent steering can reduce this failure in one model forward pass, but a fixed gate treats early and late decoding steps by the same criterion. We observe that predictive entropy varies sharply within free-running generation, while states that commit to short answers occupy a narrow, confident band. We exploit this temporal mismatch by using uncertainty as a causally available, token-local control signal for an existing steering gate. We propose Entropy-Gated Dynamic Rectification (EGDR), which lowers the intervention threshold when entropy derived from the preceding step's already available logits is high and retains the calibrated operating point when it is low. On LLaVA-1.5-7B, the underlying static gate lowers CHAIR CS/CI from 53.00/15.30 to 32.00/14.30; replacing only its constant threshold with EGDR further lowers them to 29.00/13.60. At a matched 58% intervention rate, the corresponding static control obtains 30.50/14.00. The gain over the static gate holds on LLaVA-NeXT-7B and grows with the generation budget, while POPE scores and general multimodal capability remain effectively unchanged. EGDR requires no additional model forward pass and adds roughly 2% measured latency. Our code is available at https://github.com/alvi-uiu/EGDR.

</details>


### [60] [DynOSeg: Dynamic Object Segmentation via Motion-Geometry Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04452)
*Xu Han,Yiduo Wang,Yiran Wang,Viorela Ila*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DynOSeg，一种两阶段融合的运动目标分割方法：先用几何感知残差图与深度编码构建几何运动表示，再引入语义特征细化目标理解，并构建CARLA-MOS等训练语料，在多数据集上优于现有SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动目标分割（MOS）方法多依赖纯视觉运动线索（光流、点轨迹），在相机运动与物体运动耦合、以及阴影/反射等运动致外观变化下存在固有歧义；而简单拼接深度、语义等额外信息无法充分发挥其互补性。

Method: 两阶段融合框架：第一阶段融合几何感知残差图与深度编码，形成运动几何表示，用于区分相机运动与物体动态；第二阶段引入语义特征细化物体级理解。此外整理多样化训练语料，包含新构建的合成城区驾驶MOS数据集CARLA-MOS。

Result: 在多个多样化数据集上，DynOSeg一致优于已发表的SOTA MOS方法。

Conclusion: 分阶段、有结构的几何—语义融合比朴素多模态拼接更有效地利用互补信息，可显著提升动态场景下MOS的鲁棒性与精度。

Abstract: Moving Object Segmentation (MOS) detects and separates dynamic objects from the static background in a video, which plays a fundamental role in dynamic-scene understanding. Most MOS methods rely on pure visual motion cues alone, such as optical flow or point tracks, which is inherently ambiguous due to the compounded camera and object motions as well as motion-induced appearance changes, e.g. shadows and reflections. Naive fusion of additional information such as depth and semantics, however, cannot leverage their complementarity to the fullest extent. We therefore propose DynOSeg, a novel MOS approach that takes on a two-stage fusion structure. DynOSeg first combines a geometry-aware residual map and depth encoding into a geometric representation of motion, delineating camera motions and object dynamics. We then further incorporate semantic features to refine object understanding. We have additionally curated a diverse training corpus including CARLA-MOS, a new synthetic urban-driving dataset for MOS training. Our experiments show that DynOSeg consistently outperforms published state-of-the-art MOS methods across a diverse range of datasets. Our code and pretrained models can be found at https://github.com/ACFR-RPG/DynOSeg

</details>


### [61] [Is Forgetting Unified? Exploring Concept Unlearning in Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04467)
*Zhongkai Xue,Junkai Chen,Mingcong Lei,Rumo Zhang,Yixiao Liu,Zhimin Sun,Shu Wu,Qiang Liu,Lihua Zhang,Liang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究统一多模态模型（UMMs）的跨通路概念遗忘问题，提出诊断基准 UUBench 并提出 UniForget 方法，实现生成与理解两条通路的联合遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单通路遗忘方法难以同时在图像生成与视觉理解两条通路上抑制同一目标概念；生成侧编辑虽能抑制生成，但对理解侧遗忘有限，理解侧基线甚至在本通路内效果都不佳，二者简单组合亦不足。

Method: 构建 UUBench：覆盖 30 个目标概念与 60 个语义相关近邻概念的诊断基准，用于评估联合遗忘与近邻概念保持；在此基础上提出 UniForget，实现跨通路的联合概念遗忘，并考察联合遗忘与能力保持之间的权衡。

Result: 代表性 UMMs 上单通路方法联合遗忘有限；UniForget 在 BAGEL-7B-MoT 上达到 80.0%、在 Janus-Pro-7B 上达到 92.7% 的联合遗忘率，优于代表性基线；分析揭示联合遗忘与能力保持存在权衡。

Conclusion: 跨通路概念遗忘需要专门方法而非单通路方法的直接组合；UniForget 能有效实现生成与理解的联合遗忘，但需在遗忘强度与模型其他能力保持之间权衡，UUBench 为相关研究提供诊断工具。

Abstract: Unified multimodal models (UMMs) integrate image generation and visual understanding, but are existing single-pathway unlearning methods sufficient to suppress target concepts across both? In this work, we study cross-pathway concept unlearning. Our goal is to prevent a model from both generating images of a target concept and identifying it in input images, while preserving other capabilities. To this end, we introduce UUBench, a diagnostic benchmark covering 30 target concepts and 60 semantically related neighboring concepts. Experiments on representative UMMs show that the evaluated single-pathway methods achieve limited joint forgetting: generation-side editors strongly suppress target generation but yield limited forgetting in visual understanding, while understanding-side baselines struggle to achieve substantial forgetting even in their own pathway. Their direct composition also yields limited joint forgetting. We therefore propose UniForget, achieving joint forgetting rates of 80.0% on BAGEL-7B-MoT and 92.7% on Janus-Pro-7B, outperforming representative baselines. Further analysis reveals trade-offs between joint forgetting and capability preservation.

</details>


### [62] [Using GeoAI and machine learning tools for consistent high-resolution land cover mapping based on time-series NAIP imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04470)
*Jie Liu,Xusheng Tang,Chao Wang,Zhengxiao Yan,Yuchen Dai,Qi Zhang,Conghe Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出双轨工作流，在无历史训练样本条件下从NAIP影像生成时间一致的1米土地覆盖图：2009–2017年高质量影像用预训练U-Net/ResNet-34并结合SAM细化边界，2004–2008年低质量影像通过基于NLCD的时空桥接与标签回溯反演。六县验证总体精度为0.887（2014）、0.886（2017）、0.733（2004）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率土地覆盖图对生态监测有价值，但航空影像在年份与传感器间存在成像差异，且缺乏历史训练样本，导致一致的历史时间序列稀缺。

Method: 构建双轨制工作流：(1) 对2009–2017年较高质量NAIP影像，使用预训练U-Net/ResNet-34模型分割，并用Segment Anything Model (SAM) 优化边界；(2) 对2004–2008年较低质量影像，采用基于NLCD的时空桥接（spatiotemporal bridging）与标签回溯（label back-casting）流程重建土地覆盖。

Result: 在北卡罗来纳州和宾夕法尼亚州六个县评估，总体精度分别为0.887（2014）、0.886（2017）和0.733（2004）；Structure、Water、Wetland和Cropland类别的F1分数持续较高。

Conclusion: 该工作流融合GeoAI与多源数据，缓解历史航空影像的时间异质性，可支撑美国本土的高分辨率土地变化分析与景观规划。

Abstract: High-resolution land-cover maps support ecological monitoring, but consistent historical time series remain scarce because aerial imagery varies across years and sensors. We developed a dual-track workflow to produce temporally coherent 1-m land-cover maps from National Agricultural Imagery Program (NAIP) imagery without historical training samples. For higher-quality imagery from 2009-2017, we used a pretrained U-Net/ResNet-34 model and refined boundaries with the Segment Anything Model (SAM). For lower-quality imagery from 2004-2008, we reconstructed land cover through an NLCD-based spatiotemporal bridging and label back-casting pipeline. Evaluation across six counties in North Carolina and Pennsylvania yielded overall accuracies of 0.887 (2014), 0.886 (2017), and 0.733 (2004). Structure, Water, Wetland, and Cropland consistently achieved high F1-scores. The workflow integrates GeoAI and multiple data sources to address temporal heterogeneity in historical aerial imagery, supporting high-resolution land-change analysis and landscape planning across the conterminous United States.

</details>


### [63] [Beyond Token Representations: Explicit Visual Object Grounding for Video Reasoning Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04472)
*Jun Huang,Junyang Zhang,Zijie Yue,Bingkun Wang,Yong Luo,Miaojing Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出显式视觉目标定位框架EVOG用于视频推理分割（VRS），通过生成并精炼显式空间提示（边界框与点集）、查询多样化与对齐、以及基于提示的关键帧选择来实现更精准的掩码预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VRS方法通常先生成少量分割token再送入掩码解码器，但这些token在语义上单一、空间上模糊，难以充分表示目标以指导掩码预测。

Method: 1）空间感知提示生成与精炼方案：查询多模态大语言模型生成显式空间提示（边界框和点集），并通过多步对话进行精炼；2）查询多样化与对齐模块：生成从不同视角描述同一目标的辅助查询，并在其预测的空间提示间施加跨查询一致性约束以缓解查询局限性带来的训练偏差；3）基于提示的关键帧选择策略：筛选可靠帧以支持掩码解码与传播。

Result: 在八个数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法显著优于当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: 以显式空间提示替代隐式分割token进行目标定位，可有效提升视频推理分割的性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Video Reasoning Segmentation (VRS) aims to infer and segment target objects specified by reasoning queries in videos. Existing methods typically first infer the target and generate a few segmentation tokens for it, which are then fed into a mask decoder for mask prediction. However, such segmentation tokens tend to be both semantically monotonous and spatially ambiguous, making them largely insufficient to represent the target to guide mask prediction. In this paper, we propose an explicit visual object grounding framework for VRS. Specifically, we first propose a spatial-aware prompt generation and refinement scheme, in which we query a multimodal large language model to generate explicit spatial prompts (i.e., bounding box and point set) for the target and then refine them through multi-step dialogue. Furthermore, we introduce a query diversification and alignment module to generate auxiliary queries that describe the same target from different perspectives. We then enforce cross-query consistency between their predicted spatial prompts to alleviate the training bias caused by query limitations. Finally, we select several reliable frames using a prompt-based keyframe selection strategy to support mask decoding and propagation. Extensive experiments on eight datasets demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods. The code is available at https://github.com/viscom-tongji/EVOG.

</details>


### [64] [KeyWise: Efficient Video-Only Piano Transcription with a Lightweight Per-Key Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04476)
*Anastasios Chatzigiannis,Natalia Panagou,Maria Koziri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出KeyWise：利用键盘已知几何结构的逐键轻量架构，将校正后的键盘划分为88个ROI，用共享循环网络逐键跟踪，以1.3M参数达到甚至超越SOTA视觉转录性能并快4.7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频钢琴转录受延音踏板及噪声/缺失音轨困扰，而顶视视频可直接观测按键的按下与释放。但现有视觉转录网络用单一全局特征描述整个键盘，必须自行学习每个键的位置，参数冗余且效率低。

Method: KeyWise不学习键盘几何而是直接利用它：将校正（rectified）后的键盘图像划分为88个等分瓦片作为区域兴趣（ROI），每个键对应一个ROI；一个在所有权上共享的小型循环网络沿时间维度跟随该键，实现逐键（per-key）特征提取与转录。

Result: PianoVAM上：50 ms起音F1 95.0%（V2N 94.7%），100 ms 97.8%（97.0%）；参数1.3M vs 28.2M，同GPU上快4.7倍；100 ms下释键F1 97.0% vs 95.5%、力度F1 89.4% vs 84.5%。PianoYT上：经PianoVAM预训练，100 ms起音F1 69.4%，与最佳公开结果相当，但新录音条件需微调。

Conclusion: 显式利用键盘几何的逐键共享架构可在显著降低参数与计算量的同时保持或超越全局特征网络的转录精度，并改善释键与力度估计；跨数据集泛化尚需微调。

Abstract: Audio-based piano transcription is hampered by the sustain pedal and by noisy or missing soundtracks, problems that video-only transcription avoids, since a top-view video shows directly when each key is pressed and released. Most visual transcription networks, however, describe the whole keyboard with one global feature vector and must learn where each key is. In this paper, we propose KeyWise, a lightweight per-key architecture that exploits the known geometry of the keyboard instead of learning it. The rectified keyboard is divided into 88 equal tiles that serve as regions of interest (ROIs), and one small recurrent network, shared by all keys, follows each key over time. On PianoVAM, KeyWise reaches the onset accuracy of the state-of-the-art V2N (95.0% vs. 94.7% onset F1 at 50 ms and 97.8% vs. 97.0% at 100 ms) with 1.3 M instead of 28.2 M parameters, and it runs 4.7 times faster on the same GPU. At 100 ms, it is also significantly more accurate when the key releases (97.0% vs. 95.5% F1) or the velocities (89.4% vs. 84.5% F1) must be correct as well. On PianoYT, KeyWise pre-trained on PianoVAM reaches 69.4% onset F1 at 100 ms, comparable to the best published result, although it needs fine-tuning for new recording conditions.

</details>


### [65] [Bridging CLIP with DINO: Cross-Modal Information Maximization for Online Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04489)
*Taehoon Kim,Wonjun Hwang,Hyunsouk Cho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CDIM，一种在线测试时自适应方法，从表示、优化、预测三个层面打破CLIP在测试时损坏下的模态偏置与确认偏置循环，在ImageNet-C等基准上优于CLIP-based在线TTA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP等视觉语言模型虽支持任意类名的零样本分类，但在测试时损坏下视觉编码器会把畸变特征以高置信度投影到错误的文本锚点上，作者称此为"模态偏置"。标准熵最小化会强化这些过度自信的错误，形成确认偏置循环并导致预测标签分布坍缩。

Method: 三层次设计：(1) 表示层——将冻结的自监督骨干（如DINO v2）与CLIP结合，通过交叉协方差logit集成，把CLIP文本原型投影到无语义文本的自监督特征空间中，使自监督分支对原生图像特征分类，受CLIP文本偏置影响更小；(2) 优化层——门控测试时信息最大化目标，将逐样本锐化与批量级多样性配对，并用自适应门控把不可靠样本排除出梯度；(3) 预测层——在线logit调整以校正残余类别分布偏斜。

Result: 在ImageNet-C、CIFAR10-C、CIFAR100-C上优于对比的CLIP-based在线TTA基线，并在持续TTA与混合域TTA中保持竞争力；大量分析与消融验证了各组件贡献。

Conclusion: 通过表示、优化、预测三级协同，可有效打破CLIP在损坏测试下的模态偏置—确认偏置循环，实现稳健的在线测试时自适应。

Abstract: Vision-language models such as CLIP enable zero-shot classification from arbitrary class names, but under test-time corruptions, their visual encoder projects distorted features onto incorrect textual anchors with high confidence: a phenomenon we term modality bias. Standard entropy minimization reinforces these overconfident errors, which creates a confirmation-bias loop and collapses the predicted label distribution. We propose an online test-time adaptation (TTA) method that breaks this loop at three levels. At the representation level, a frozen self-supervised backbone (e.g., DINO v2) is combined with CLIP through a cross-covariance logit ensemble. CLIP text prototypes are projected into the feature space of this text-free backbone, so the self-supervised branch classifies native image features and is less affected by the text bias of CLIP. At the optimization level, a gated test-time information-maximization objective pairs per-sample sharpening with batch-level diversity, while an adaptive gate excludes unreliable samples from the gradient. At the prediction level, an online logit adjustment corrects the residual class-distribution skew. CDIM outperforms the compared CLIP-based online TTA baselines on ImageNet-C, CIFAR10-C, and CIFAR100-C, and remains competitive in continual and mixed-domain TTA. Extensive analyses and ablation studies verify the contribution of each of its components. Our code is available at https://github.com/B1ueC1oud/CDIM

</details>


### [66] [Polarimetric Scattering-Structure Contrastive Learning for Few-Shot HRRP Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04490)
*Fangyi Wang,Peng Zhao,Haipeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出极化成散射结构对比学习(PSS-CL)，用结构化物理视角辅助小样本HRRP识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小样本HRRP识别受两方面困扰：强方位敏感性导致特征变化大，新类支撑样本稀疏，难以学习稳定表征。

Method: 将主导局部响应表征为其对规范极化成散射机制的软亲和度、归一化距离位置与能量贡献，构建结构化物理训练视图；采用对称跨视图对比目标把该散射结构迁移到HRRP表征；推理阶段仅用情景式原型学习，无需物理分支。

Result: 在六类实测数据集的随机方位与跨方位实验中，跨方位1/3/5-shot的MCA分别为81.73%、86.92%、90.98%，较最优对比方法提升5.62%、3.09%、3.56%。

Conclusion: 物理散射结构先验可有效增强小样本HRRP表征的方位鲁棒性，且推理时无需引入额外物理分支，保持轻量实用。

Abstract: Few-shot high-resolution range profile (HRRP) recognition is challenged by strong aspect-dependent variation and sparse novel-class support. We propose polarimetric scattering-structure contrastive learning (PSS-CL), which supplies an episodic HRRP encoder with a structured physical training view. Dominant local responses are represented by their soft affinities to canonical polarimetric mechanisms, normalized range locations, and energy contributions. A symmetric cross-view objective transfers this scattering structure to the learned HRRP representation, while episodic prototype learning supports novel-class inference without the physical branch. We conduct Random-aspect and Cross-aspect experiments on a six-class measured dataset. In Cross-aspect 1-, 3-, and 5-shot recognition, PSS-CL achieves 81.73%, 86.92%, and 90.98% mean class accuracy (MCA), respectively, outperforming the best competing results by 5.62%, 3.09%, and 3.56%.

</details>


### [67] [SPA-Q: Structure-Preserving Adaptive Post-Training Quantization for Monocular Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04491)
*Jaemin Choi,Jincheol Yang,Nahyun Lim,Yun-Seong Jeong,Matti Zinke,Hyunwoo Yu,Suk-Ju Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SPA-Q，一种结构保持的自适应训练后量化（PTQ）框架，用于单目深度估计（MDE）模型Depth Anything的低比特量化，通过保持注意力分布与对齐通道特征分布解决4-bit量化难题，在NYUv2与KITTI上AbsRel平均降低25.9%、δ1提升17.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Transformer的基础模型（如Depth Anything）在MDE中泛化性强，但计算与内存开销大，难以高效部署；PTQ是实用压缩手段，但低比特PTQ对MDE仍具挑战。作者指出两个关键问题：(i) 独立最小化query和key投影的量化误差无法保持二者交互产生的注意力图；(ii) 量化误差逐层累积，导致中间特征分布偏移。

Method: SPA-Q包含两部分：1) Attention-Preserving Calibration (APC)——通过匹配全精度注意力分布来确定query与key的量化参数；2) Channel-Wise Distribution Alignment (CWDA)——学习逐通道仿射变换以缓解量化引起的分布偏移，训练后将学习到的参数吸收进权重（推理无额外开销）。

Result: 在4-bit量化设定下，SPA-Q在NYUv2和KITTI数据集上一致优于现有PTQ方法，AbsRel平均降低25.9%，δ1平均提升17.3%。

Conclusion: 面向MDE基础模型的低比特PTQ需从结构层面（注意力保持）与分布层面（通道对齐）协同设计；SPA-Q以无推理额外开销的方式显著提升4-bit量化性能，为高效部署提供可行方案。

Abstract: Monocular depth estimation (MDE) has advanced rapidly with the emergence of foundation models such as Depth Anything. Their transformer-based architectures provide strong generalization across diverse scenes and domains, but also incur high computational and memory cost, making efficient deployment challenging. Post-Training Quantization (PTQ) provides an efficient and practical solution for model compression, yet low-bit PTQ remains challenging for MDE models. When applying PTQ to Depth Anything, we identify two key challenges: (i) independently minimizing the quantization error of the query and key projections fails to preserve the attention maps induced by their interaction, and (ii) quantization errors accumulate across layers, resulting in intermediate feature distribution shifts. To address these issues, we propose SPA-Q, a Structure-Preserving Adaptive PTQ framework for MDE, consisting of two main components. First, Attention-Preserving Calibration (APC) determines query and key quantization parameters by matching the full-precision attention distribution. Second, Channel-Wise Distribution Alignment (CWDA) learns channel-wise affine transformations to mitigate quantization-induced distribution shifts, and the learned parameters are absorbed into the weights after training. Experimental results show that SPA-Q consistently outperforms existing PTQ methods under 4-bit quantization, achieving an average 25.9% reduction in AbsRel and a 17.3% improvement in $δ_1$ across NYUv2 and KITTI datasets.

</details>


### [68] [3DGS-VBench+ and 3DGSV-Assessor: A Multi-Dimensional Subjective Benchmark and a Reference-Flexible Objective Model for 3D Gaussian Splatting Video Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04498)
*Yuke Xing,William Gordon,Jiarui Wang,Yujie Zhang,Guangtao Zhai,Yiling Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出3DGS视频质量评估基准3DGS-VBench+（3,036个重建视频、9,108个MOS，覆盖整体/几何/颜色三维度），并提出可灵活使用参考的框架3DGSV-Assessor，在单次生成中同时输出三维度分数，支持RR与NR评估，达到SOTA相关性并具零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS存储开销大，压缩与训练都会引入表征特定失真；现有生成式压缩方法对几何与颜色属性采用不同参数，易产生维度特定失真，因此需要多维度评估。同时真实拍摄场景通常缺乏与合成连续相机轨迹对齐的GT视频，仅有稀疏参考视图，导致带对齐参考的多维质量评估困难。

Method: 构建3DGS-VBench+：涵盖11个真实场景与22个合成场景，由6种代表性压缩算法在系统设计的压缩等级下生成3,036个3DGS重建及对应渲染视频，标注9,108个MOS（整体、几何、颜色质量）。提出3DGSV-Assessor：参考灵活的大规模多模态框架，单次生成同时打分三个维度，并通过有界门控（bounded gate）将基于排序的参考线索融合进冻结回归器，从而同时支持RR与NR评估。

Result: 在3DGS-VBench+上与人类感知的相关性达到SOTA，并在其他NVS基准上表现出较强的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 面向3DGS压缩视频的稀缺对齐参考问题，本文以多维主观基准与参考灵活的多模态评估器形成完整方案，为3DGS及NVS质量评估提供了数据与方法基础，数据集与代码将开源。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables real-time novel view synthesis (NVS), but its heavy storage makes compression essential, and both training and compression introduce representation-specific distortions. Since generative compression in many methods treats geometric and color attributes via different parameters, it can induce dimension-specific distortions, motivating multi-dimensional assessment. Moreover, real-world captures generally lack an aligned ground-truth video for a synthesized, continuous camera trajectory, leaving only sparse captured views as references. To enable multi-dimensional quality assessment of 3DGS videos with scarce aligned references, we first present 3DGS-VBench+, a large-scale subjective video quality assessment (VQA) benchmark comprising 3{,}036 3DGS reconstructions, each paired with a rendered video, across 11 real-world and 22 synthetic scenes, generated by 6 representative compression algorithms at systematically designed levels and annotated with 9{,}108 MOSs covering overall, geometry, and color quality. Based on 3DGS-VBench+, we propose 3DGSV-Assessor, a reference-flexible, large multimodal framework that scores all three dimensions in one generation pass and fuses a ranking-based reference cue into the frozen regressor through a bounded gate, supporting both reduced-reference (RR) and no-reference (NR) assessment. Extensive experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art correlation with human perception on 3DGS-VBench+ and strong zero-shot generalization to other NVS benchmarks. The dataset and code will be released at https://github.com/YukeXing/3DGSV-Assessor.

</details>


### [69] [E2I-Flow: EEG-to-Image Reconstruction via Wavelet-Temporal Cross-Attention and Conditional Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04524)
*Haowei Pan,Hwa Hui Tew,Junn Yong Loo,Fuad Noman,Hernando Ombao,Chee-Ming Ting*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出E2I-Flow框架，通过时频表征学习的EEG编码器与条件流匹配实现EEG到图像的解码与重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG信号时频特性复杂，且离散去噪框架难以将神经活动映射为有意义的视觉表征，非侵入式视觉解码重建存在挑战。

Method: EEG编码器含平稳小波频率分支与时序混合分支，经双向交叉注意力和自适应融合整合；表征以对比损失和MSE损失对齐真实图像嵌入；对齐后表征作为Transformer流匹配模型的条件预测视觉嵌入并合成图像。

Result: 实验显示较强的零样本EEG到图像检索能力和改进的视觉重建性能，优于多项先进基线。

Conclusion: 时频表征学习编码器与条件流匹配的结合可有效提升EEG到图像的检索与重建效果。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG)-based visual decoding and reconstruction aims to recover visual information from non-invasive brain signals. But there are challenges due to the complex temporal-frequency characteristics of EEG signals and the difficulty of discrete denoising framework in mapping neural activity to meaningful visual representations. Here, we propose E2I-Flow, an EEG-to-image decoding and reconstruction framework that combines a novel EEG encoder for time-frequency representation learning from EEG features, and conditional flow matching for visual decoding. A stationary-wavelet frequency branch and a temporal-mixing branch are integrated through bidirectional cross-attention and adaptive fusion, with the resulting EEG representations aligned to ground-truth image embeddings using contrastive and mean-squared-error loss supervisions. The aligned EEG representation is then used as a condition for a Transformer-based flow-matching model to predict the corresponding visual embedding for image synthesis. Experimental results show strong zero-shot EEG-to-image retrieval and improved visual reconstruction performance, outperforming several state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [70] [Learning to Falsify: Hierarchical Multiple Instance Learning for High Resolution AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04529)
*Haoran Chen,Daiqi Gao,Xinyuan Li,Yongwei Wang,Lei Zhu,Rui Cai,Jianfeng Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将AI生成图像检测建模为"证伪驱动的多示例学习"问题：单个伪造patch即可证伪整图，而真实图像需要所有patch为真；据此提出分层多示例学习框架LTF、Cross-Shuffle训练策略与可插拔测试时聚合模块FATAS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型使合成图像日益逼真，检测的可靠性与泛化性面临挑战。现有方法多依赖整图级分类，易忽略局部伪影，在高分辨率图像上尤为明显。

Method: 1) 将检测形式化为证伪驱动的多示例学习（MIL）：图像视为多层级（不同空间尺度）的实例包，按"一假则假、全真才真"的证伪原则聚合预测，形成分层MIL框架LTF；2) 训练中引入Cross-Shuffle，打乱/重组样本以促使模型学习局部伪影证据而非图像级捷径；3) 提出FATAS（Falsification-driven Test-time Aggregation Strategy），将同一证伪原则用于聚合patch级预测，作为免重训的即插即用模块提升现有检测器。

Result: 大量实验表明LTF在多个基准上具有较强泛化性与鲁棒性；FATAS在无需重新训练的情况下显著提升代表性检测器在高分辨率场景下的性能。

Conclusion: 以局部可证伪性替代整图判别是提升AI生成图像检测泛化性与高分辨率适应性的有效范式，且该原则可作为通用测试时聚合策略复用到现有检测器。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative models have made synthetic images increasingly photorealistic, posing growing challenges to reliable and generalizable detection of AI-generated images. Existing methods typically rely on holistic image-level classification, which may overlook local artifacts, especially in high-resolution images. To address this limitation, we formulate this task as a falsification-driven multiple instance learning problem, where a single fake patch can falsify the whole image, while a real image requires all patches to be authentic. Based on this formulation, we propose \textit{Learning to Falsify (LTF)}, a hierarchical multiple instance learning framework that models an image as multiple bags of instances across different spatial levels and aggregates their predictions under the falsification-inspired principle. We further introduce Cross-Shuffle during training to encourage the model to learn local artifact evidence rather than image-level shortcuts. Furthermore, we propose the \textit{Falsification-driven Test-time Aggregation Strategy (FATAS)}, a plug-and-play module that extends the same falsification-inspired principle to existing detectors by aggregating patch-level predictions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LTF achieves strong generalization and robustness across multiple benchmarks, while FATAS substantially improves representative detectors in high-resolution scenarios without retraining. Code is available at https://github.com/HuiGuanLab/LTF.git

</details>


### [71] [A Universal Raw-IQ Radar Encoder for Cross-Sensor Human Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04534)
*Jonas Leo Mueller,Dario Zanca,Sebastian Hoefler,Jan Petermann,Thomas Altstidl,Naga Venkata Sai Jitin Jami,Emmanuelle Salin,Bjoern M. Eskofier*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无标签、跨平台的雷达通用编码器（不含微多普勒频谱图，改用去杂波原始测量的卷积β-VAE预训练+冻结编码器），在活动/手势/手语/步态10个设定中多数最优，跨用户泛化提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有雷达人体感知依赖端到端任务专用模型并基于人工选择的信号投影，跨用户、任务与硬件泛化差。作者认为主流微多普勒频谱图不适合迁移：保留的Doppler轴会随载频缩放，而载频不变的角度轴却被丢弃。

Method: 在去杂波后的原始雷达测量上以卷积β-VAE自监督预训练；数据覆盖4个平台（60/77 GHz、4–12虚拟天线），通过统一的频谱插值网格对齐。下游任务冻结编码器，仅训练小型有监督时序分类器。对比端到端有监督模型与一个预训练雷达基础模型，共10个设定（活动、手势、手语、步态）。

Result: 用户与雷达在训练中出现时，5个基准全部最优；未见用户条件下4个基准中3个最优（+4.8至+23.2点）；主体不重叠步态识别中4个留出受试者均最优、平均高出最强基线8.3点（预训练未含该雷达任何记录）；预训练排除测试雷达最多损失5.3点。

Conclusion: 以原始测量替代微多普勒频谱图并进行无标签跨平台预训练，可获得更强且更可迁移的雷达人体感知表示，冻结编码器即可在多种任务上超越任务专用端到端模型。

Abstract: Radar-based human sensing typically relies on end-to-end task-specific models trained on hand-chosen signal projections, which generalize poorly across unseen users, tasks, and hardware.
