We argue that the dominant signal processing choice, the micro-Doppler spectrogram, is a poor starting point for transfer because it keeps the Doppler axis, which carrier frequency rescales, and discards the angular axis, which carrier frequency leaves unchanged. We instead pretrain a convolutional beta-VAE without labels on clutter-removed raw radar measurements from four platforms spanning 60 and 77 GHz and 4 to 12 virtual antennas, aligned by a unified spectral interpolation grid. For every downstream benchmark the encoder stays frozen, and only a small supervised temporal classifier is trained on its embeddings. We compare against supervised models trained end to end on each benchmark and a pretrained radar foundation model across ten settings covering activity, gesture, sign-language, and gait recognition. When the test users and the radar are seen during training, our encoder is best on all five benchmarks. For users never seen in training, it leads on three of four benchmarks, by 4.8 to 23.2 points. On subject-disjoint gait recognition, it is best for each of the four held-out subjects and 8.3 points above the strongest baseline on average, even though its pretraining excluded every recording of this radar. Excluding the test radar from pretraining costs at most 5.3 points on any benchmark. Code is available at https://github.com/jonasmueler/UniversalRadarEncoder_WACV.

</details>


### [72] [BtsPruner: Role-Aware Visual Token Pruning for Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04537)
*Zihao Qi,Zhibo Zhang,Miaoxin Zhang,Jincheng Cai,Zengqiang Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 BtsPruner，一种免训练的角色感知视觉 token 剪枝框架，利用视觉 token 在编码过程中由局部细节载体向全局引用代表渐进转变的规律，在固定预算下保留互补角色 token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLMs 处理数百至数千个视觉 token 带来显著推理计算与显存开销；现有免训练剪枝方法多用语言模型注意力、视觉注意力或特征相似度估计重要性，对视觉 token 采用统一准则，忽视了 token 在视觉编码中承担不同角色的问题。

Method: 基于对 token 角色演化的观察，通过注意力集中度的演化确定 token 角色转变点；从后期编码阶段识别全局引用代表 token，并递归地从早期阶段检索局部细节 token，以恢复互补的细粒度证据，在固定预算下保留互补角色。

Result: 在 LLaVA-1.5、LLaVA-NeXT、Qwen-VL、InternVL、CogVLM 上验证，激进 token 缩减下性能保持优异；LLaVA-1.5-7B 上仅用 576 个视觉 token 中的 32 个（5.6%）即保留稠密模型 91.6% 的性能；高分辨率 LLaVA-NeXT-7B 上实现最高 5.95 倍 CUDA 延迟加速。

Conclusion: 视觉 token 具有可被利用的角色演化结构，角色感知的互补剪枝是免训练视觉 token 压缩的有效方向，可在大幅缩减 token 的同时维持较高性能与推理加速。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) often process hundreds or thousands of visual tokens, resulting in substantial computational and memory overhead during inference. Existing training-free pruning methods typically estimate token importance using language-model attention, visual attention, or feature similarity. However, these methods treat visual tokens with uniform criteria and overlook that tokens may serve different roles during visual encoding. In this work, we investigate the evolution of visual token roles in LVLMs and observe that tokens progressively transition from local detail carriers to globally referenced representatives as encoding proceeds. Based on this observation, we propose BtsPruner, a training-free role-aware visual token pruning framework that preserves complementary token roles under a fixed budget. Specifically, BtsPruner first identifies the token-role transition point based on the evolution of attention concentration. It then discovers globally referenced representatives from later encoding stages and recursively retrieves local detail tokens from earlier stages to recover complementary fine-grained evidence. Extensive experiments across LLaVA-1.5, LLaVA-NeXT, Qwen-VL, InternVL, and CogVLM demonstrate that BtsPruner achieves superior performance retention under aggressive token reduction. On LLaVA-1.5-7B, BtsPruner preserves 91.6% of the dense model's performance using only 32 of 576 visual tokens (5.6%). In the high-resolution LLaVA-NeXT-7B setting, it achieves up to a 5.95x CUDA-latency speedup.

</details>


### [73] [Predicting Void Points in Self-Occluding Forest Volumes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04548)
*Benjamin Bachmayr,Mohamed Youssef,Oliver Bimber*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对合成孔径成像(SAI)中遮挡被抑制而非消除导致生物量系统性高估的问题，通过改进训练标签（显式将空域标为负类）而非成像流程，训练2D/3D U-Net等模型进行冠层占据预测；2D U-Net取得IoU 0.6290，RGB输入的体素架构最高达0.6514，显著优于COLMAP(0.3446)和DepthAnything3(0.2959)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAI通过对同一深度的多视角图像进行warp与平均来观测林冠，但遮挡只是被抑制而非消除，观测不良区域被误判为占据，导致生物量系统性高估。Deep Forest项目因训练数据仅依赖实测反射点、排除空域，网络无法学习显式占据边界，从而难以区分重建伪影与真实植被。

Method: 不改变成像流程而修改训练标签：每个仿真场景渲染两次，一次生成9x9俯视网格用于焦栈合成，一次由场景几何生成550个深度裁剪切片作为占据标签，显式将空域作为负类。基于Gazebo构建含3个物种、80–200棵树的程序化林分；用预训练Pix2Pix将渲染RGB转换为合成近红外波段；用光场渲染生成三通道550平面的对齐焦栈。评估了四种架构，并在留出场景上测试。

Result: 2D U-Net IoU 0.6290，优于3D U-Net的0.5707；传统重建方法表现明显更差，COLMAP为0.3446、DepthAnything3为0.2959（均为最优膨胀扫描后）。体素架构的RGB输入变体在无膨胀、无逐场景标定条件下取得最高IoU 0.6514。

Conclusion: 结果凸显传统重建方法的局限：它们能恢复可见表面，却难以推断封闭冠层内部的占据情况；而通过标签设计显式引入空域负类可有效提升占据预测精度。

Abstract: Synthetic Aperture Imaging (SAI) enables drones to observe forest canopies by warping and averaging multiple displaced views at a common depth. However, occlusion is suppressed rather than eliminated, causing poorly observed regions to appear occupied and leading to systematic biomass overestimation. The Deep Forest project cannot distinguish reconstruction artifacts from real vegetation because its training data relies on measured reflectance points, excluding empty space and preventing the network from learning explicit occupancy boundaries.
  This thesis addresses the problem by modifying training labels rather than the imaging process. Each simulated scene is rendered twice: as a 9x9 grid of top-down captures for focal-stack generation and as 550 depth-clipped slabs derived from scene geometry to provide occupancy labels, explicitly representing void space as a negative class. A Gazebo pipeline generates procedural forest stands containing 80-200 trees across three species. A pretrained Pix2Pix model translates rendered RGB images into a synthetic near-infrared band, and Light Field Rendering produces aligned focal stacks of 550 planes across three channels.
  Four architectures were evaluated on a held-out scene. The plain 2D U-Net achieved an IoU of 0.6290, outperforming the volumetric 3D U-Net (0.5707). Established reconstruction methods performed substantially worse: COLMAP achieved 0.3446 and DepthAnything3 0.2959, both after their respective optimal dilation sweeps. An RGB-input variant of the volumetric architecture achieved the highest IoU of 0.6514 without dilation or per-scene calibration. These results highlight the limitations of conventional reconstruction methods, which recover visible surfaces but struggle to infer occupancy within closed canopies.

</details>


### [74] [SEEK-VAU: Towards Evidence-Faithful Video Anomaly Understanding via Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04554)
*Minghe Wang,Weijun Zeng,Dapeng Luo,Xinyue Wang,Nong Sang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SEEK-VAU，将视频异常理解(VAU)重构为有界预算下的智能体"搜索—验证"交互协议，配合证据忠实的反事实验证奖励(EFCV)与SEEK-Bench基准，实现事件链完整且可主动核验的异常理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VAU系统均在预定义规则下以馈送式(feed-forward)方式从预采集证据(采样帧或由其聚类的事件)推断异常，一旦开始推理就无法再寻找缺失证据；当规则不完备、证据不完整时即失效。

Method: 1) 把VAU重定义为智能体式搜索—验证交互协议，在有限搜索预算下，策略主动沿异常事件链三阶段(前兆precursor、触发trigger、余波aftermath)采集证据，并在定论前核验证据充分性；2) 引入证据忠实反事实验证(EFCV)，对所选证据在反事实检验下仍具支持性、紧凑性与必要性给予奖励，使"何处查、何时停"的策略行为可学习；3) 构建SEEK-Bench，含视频级episode、时序区间标注、语义QA与事件链阶段标签。

Result: 预算上界控制了搜索成本，但本身不足以教会策略定位与停止；EFCV使该行为可学习，将事件链完整性由规则的副产物转为显式设计目标；SEEK-Bench提供区间级、阶段级与语义QA的细粒度评测支撑。

Conclusion: SEEK-VAU与SEEK-Bench共同为证据忠实、可主动验证的视频异常理解奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: Most video anomaly understanding (VAU) systems still infer anomaly judgments from evidence collected in a feed-forward way under predefined rules, typically sampled frames, or events clustered from such frames. This protocol fails when the collected evidence is incomplete under imperfect predefined rules, because once reasoning begins the model can no longer search for the missing evidence. We address this limitation with SEEK-VAU, which reframes VAU as an agentic search-and-verify interaction protocol under a bounded search budget: instead of passively reasoning over pre-collected evidence, the policy actively gathers evidence across the three stages of an anomaly event chain (precursor, trigger, and aftermath) and verifies its sufficiency before finalization, turning event-chain completeness into an explicit design rather than an outcome of predefined rules. A bounded budget caps the cost of this search, but does not by itself teach the policy where to look or when to stop. To make this behavior learnable, we introduce evidence-faithful counterfactual verification (EFCV), which rewards selected evidence that remains supportive, compact, and necessary under counterfactual verification. We further introduce SEEK-Bench, featuring video-level episodes with temporal interval annotations, semantic QA and event-chain stage labels. Together, SEEK-VAU and SEEK-Bench establish a strong foundation for evidence-faithful and actively verifiable VAU.

</details>


### [75] [Multi-Path Mamba Diffusion for 3D Point Cloud Semantic Scene Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04583)
*Li Liang,Naveed Akhtar,Jordan Vice,Anwaar Ulhaq,Ajmal Saeed Mian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 MPath Mamba 扩散网络，将状态空间建模与扩散生成模型结合，用于3D点云语义场景补全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D语义场景补全需同时估计缺失的几何结构与语义信息，对自动驾驶等下游任务至关重要；现有方法在长序列点云处理与表征/重建质量上存在不足。

Method: 在变分自编码器的条件潜空间中运行扩散过程，并以状态空间机制增强点云表征；网络采用 skip-triple Mamba 结构、维度分解残差（dimensional decomposition residuals）与多路径数据中的多方向膨胀（multi-directional dilations），以高效处理长序列特征提取。

Result: 在 SemanticKITTI 基准上的大量实验表明，该方法在3D点云语义场景补全任务上取得极具竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 将 Mamba 状态空间建模与扩散生成相结合（于VAE潜空间）是提升3D点云语义场景补全的有效方案。

Abstract: 3D semantic scene completion plays a critical role in enabling various downstream tasks within autonomous systems by estimating both missing geometric structures and semantic information in scene data. In this work, we introduce a unique neural framework that integrates state space modeling with diffusion-based generative approaches to enhance 3D point cloud semantic scene completion. We propose \textit{MPath Mamba} diffusion network, leveraging a skip-triple Mamba structure, dimensional decomposition residuals, and multi-directional dilations within multi-path data to efficiently process long-sequence data for feature extraction. The proposed method operates in the conditioned latent space of a variational autoencoder, enhanced by a state-space mechanism to improve 3D point cloud representation and reconstruction. Extensive experiments conducted on the SemanticKITTI benchmark demonstrate that our approach achieves highly competitive performance for 3D point cloud semantic scene completion.

</details>


### [76] [Gradient-Guided Orientation Learning for Weakly Supervised Oriented Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04616)
*Junyong Jang,Juheon Park,Minyoung Jeon,Jaehyup Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种梯度引导的弱监督旋转目标检测方向学习策略：从目标区域提取主导梯度方向作为伪角度标签进行辅助监督，并引入角度敏感的评价指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱监督旋转目标检测（WS-OOD）仅使用水平框或点等廉价标注以降低旋转框标注成本，但这些弱标注不含显式方向信息，现有方法需通过间接几何一致性或等变约束推断角度，在光照变化、色彩偏移、阴影和背景杂乱等真实外观变化下监督信号模糊且不稳定。

Method: 观察到输入图像本身蕴含方向先验，故从目标区域提取基于梯度的主导方向线索，转化为伪角度标签用于辅助监督，使检测器无需旋转框标注和额外可学习参数即可学习方向感知表征；同时提出将角度差异纳入标准AP协议的方向感知评价指标。

Result: 在弱监督旋转检测基准上的大量实验表明，伪角度监督同时提升了检测性能与方向估计质量；所提指标相比单独使用传统AP能更真实地评估角度预测。

Conclusion: 以图像梯度作为方向先验的伪角度辅助监督是一种简单有效的WS-OOD方案，且AP在空间IoU较高时会掩盖显著角度误差，需引入角度敏感指标进行更可靠评估。

Abstract: Weakly supervised oriented object detection (WS-OOD) aims to reduce the annotation cost of rotated bounding boxes by leveraging cheaper annotations such as horizontal boxes or points. However, these weak annotations provide no explicit orientation information, forcing existing methods to infer object angles through indirect geometric consistency or equivariant constraints. Such indirect supervision often becomes ambiguous and unstable under real-world appearance variations, including illumination changes, color shifts, shadows, and background clutter. In this paper, we present a simple yet effective gradient-guided orientation learning strategy for WS-OOD. Motivated by the observation that the input image itself contains useful orientation priors, our method extracts dominant gradient-based orientation cues from object regions and converts them into pseudo-angle labels for auxiliary supervision, enabling the detector to learn orientation-aware representations without requiring rotated bounding box annotations or extra learnable parameters. Furthermore, since AP can mask substantial angular errors when spatial IoU remains high, we introduce an orientation-aware evaluation metric that incorporates angular discrepancies into the standard AP protocol. Extensive experiments on weakly supervised oriented detection benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed pseudo-angle supervision improves both detection performance and orientation estimation quality, while the proposed metric provides a more faithful assessment of angle prediction than conventional AP alone.

</details>


### [77] [Polyp Matching Using Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04624)
*Kristian Dalsbø Hindberg,Puneet Sharma,Benedicte Schelde-Olesen,Ulrik Deding,Jakob Frederik Frøkjær Justsen,Jan-Matthias Braun,Esmaeil S. Nadimi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于孪生网络与对比学习的结肠胶囊内镜息肉匹配方法，用于判断不同图像是否对应同一息肉，最高分类准确率约92%，AUC接近0.98。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结肠胶囊内镜（CCE）分析中的关键难题是判断多张图像是否描绘同一息肉，以避免重复计数和由此引发的过度临床干预，同时减少内镜医师在随访结肠镜检查中无精确位置信息地寻找重复息肉所带来的耗时问题。

Method: 构建基于孪生神经网络的对比学习方法。数据来自丹麦CareForColon2015试验，包含由经验丰富临床医师标注的5,103对确诊同一息肉图像与5,103对不同息肉图像。骨干网络采用ResNet18/34/50、DenseNet121及EfficientNet B0/B3，并结合对比学习与余弦向量距离度量进行分类。

Result: 实验最高分类准确率达92%，AUC接近0.98。

Conclusion: 自动化息肉匹配有望减少息肉重复计数、改善患者风险分层，并减少不必要的随访结肠镜检查。

Abstract: A key challenge in colon capsule endoscopy (CCE) analysis is accurately identifying whether multiple images depict the same polyp, which is critical to avoid over-counting and unnecessary clinical interventions. Additionally, this prevents prolonged follow-up colonoscopies where endoscopists search for duplicate polyps without precise localization. This study presents a novel contrastive learning-based approach to polyp matching using a Siamese neural network architecture. The model was trained on a dataset from the Danish CareForColon2015 trial. The dataset comprises 5,103 image pairs of confirmed same polyp and 5,103 pairs of different polyps, annotated by experienced clinicians. The proposed model employs ResNet variants (ResNet18, ResNet34, ResNet50), DenseNet121 and EfficientNet B0/B3 as backbones and utilizes contrastive learning with cosine vector distance metric for classification. Experimental results demonstrate high classification accuracies of up to 92% and AUC values close to 0.98. These results suggest that automated polyp matching has the potential to reduce polyp over-counting, improve patient risk stratification, and reduce unnecessary follow-up colonoscopies.

</details>


### [78] [MambaHand: Local Identity and Behavioral Sequence Learning with Mamba for Dynamic Hand Gesture Authentication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04626)
*Zenan Lin,Junduan Huang,Xilai Wang,Wenxiong Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出双流身份表征学习框架MambaHand Net，将CNN与Mamba结合用于动态手势认证（DHGA），在SCUT-DHGA上取得SOTA平均性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态手势认证同时包含生理与行为身份特征，但现有方法多依赖卷积网络或人工设计运动表征，难以同时捕获手势执行过程中的细粒度局部身份差异与长程行为依赖。

Method: 框架含局部身份流（LIS）与行为序列流（BSS）。LIS用卷积网络提取逐帧局部身份线索；BSS保留中间视觉特征的空间token，构建一阶与二阶时间差分序列以建模多阶行为变化，并引入双向Mamba在不同空间位置上建模语义运动，实现手势执行习惯的高效长程建模。最后通过注意力引导的空间token聚合与双流融合策略，将局部身份线索与多阶行为模式整合为判别性身份表征。

Result: 在SCUT-DHGA基准数据集上取得SOTA平均性能。

Conclusion: CNN与Mamba的结合是动态手势认证的一种有效新方案，可同时刻画局部身份细节与长程行为动态。

Abstract: Dynamic hand gesture authentication (DHGA) is an emerging biometric technique that contains both physiological and behavioral identity characteristics. However, existing methods primarily rely on convolutional networks or manually designed motion representations, making it difficult to jointly capture fine-grained local identity differences and long-range behavioral dependencies during hand gesture execution. To address these challenges, we propose a two-stream identity representation learning framework, MambaHand Net, to combine CNNs with Mamba for DHGA. The proposed framework consists of a Local Identity Stream (LIS) and a Behavioral Sequential Stream (BSS). The LIS extracts frame-wise local identity cues with a convolutional network, while the BSS preserves spatial tokens from intermediate visual features and constructs first-order and second-order temporal difference sequences to model multi-order behavioral variations. Moreover, the bidirectional Mamba is incorporated into the BSS to model semantic motion across different spatial locations, enabling efficient long-range modeling of hand gesture execution habits. Finally, attention-guided spatial token aggregation and a two-stream fusion strategy are used to integrate local identity cues and multi-order behavioral patterns into a discriminative identity representation. Our method achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) average performance on the SCUT-DHGA benchmark dataset, demonstrating that combining CNNs with Mamba is an effective new solution for DHGA.

</details>


### [79] [ReXMo: End-to-End Video Character Replacement with Expression and Motion Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04633)
*Hualiang Wei,Wenhui Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReXMo 是一个端到端视频角色替换框架，支持单人、多人及选择性替换，通过成对三元组监督与上下文扩散 Transformer 实现身份替换，同时更好保留表情、动作与视觉质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频角色替换方法多依赖掩码生成加姿态引导，在复杂场景中难以精细保留面部表情与动作，因此需要一种不依赖掩码、能端到端学习身份变换的方案。

Method: 提出成对三元组监督：合成驱动视频与原始视频共享相同表演但身份不同，实现可扩展的合成数据训练；采用 In-Context Diffusion Transformer，同时以驱动视频、参考身份和文本提示为条件联合学习身份变换；并引入 Expression Enhancement Adapter 增强多人场景下的面部表情保持。

Result: 大量实验表明，ReXMo 在身份保真度、动作一致性、表情保持和视觉质量方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: ReXMo 为单/多/选择性角色替换提供了统一的端到端范式，验证了合成三元组监督与上下文扩散建模在视频角色替换中的有效性，数据集与代码将公开。

Abstract: Video character replacement aims to replace actors while preserving facial expressions, motions, and scene dynamics. Existing methods typically rely on masked generation with pose guidance, limiting fine-grained expression and motion preservation in complex scenes. We propose ReXMo, an end-to-end framework that supports single-, multi-, and selective character replacement scenarios. ReXMo introduces paired triplet supervision, where synthesized driving videos and original videos share identical performances but different identities, enabling scalable training through synthetic data generation. An In-Context Diffusion Transformer learns identity transformation by jointly conditioning on driving videos, reference identities, and text prompts, while an Expression Enhancement Adapter improves facial expression preservation in multi-person scenes. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ReXMo outperforms existing methods in identity fidelity, motion consistency, expression preservation, and visual quality. Our dataset and code will be publicly released.

</details>


### [80] [Therapy-FIS: A Dataset for Assessing Facilitative Interpersonal Skills in Psychotherapeutic Setting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04645)
*Qijian Zheng,Wanqing Lin,Yuanfang Wang,Yijun Ge,Jordan Bate,Katie Aafjes-van Doorn,Feng Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个公开的心理治疗标准化任务自动化FIS评估数据集Therapy-FIS，并建立双任务基准及基线模型DyadicFIS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FIS（促进性人际技能）评估依赖人工观察者评分，成本高、耗时长、难以规模化；同时心理治疗场景缺乏公开可用的专业数据，制约了自动化FIS评估的发展。

Method: 构建Therapy-FIS数据集：基于890段治疗师视频与8段模拟来访者视频，提取面部、声学与文本信号，标注8个FIS子量表及总体FIS得分；据此设立治疗师表现评分与治疗师—模拟来访者联合表现评分两个下游任务，并提出基线DyadicFIS，采用门控融合（gated fusion）与路由读出（routed readout），支持仅治疗师与双人（dyadic）两种设置。

Result: DyadicFIS在两个任务上均取得最佳宏观一致性相关系数（macro CCC），分别为0.2807与0.3299。

Conclusion: Therapy-FIS为心理健康领域治疗性人际技能的自动化、数据驱动评估提供了可复现的资源与基准，推动了FIS评估的规模化。

Abstract: Facilitative Interpersonal Skills (FIS) is a performance-based assessment method that provides a structured framework for evaluating the interpersonal competencies of mental health professionals. However, existing FIS assessment relies heavily on human observer ratings, making the evaluation process labor-intensive, costly, and difficult to scale. Progress toward automated FIS assessment has also been constrained by the lack of publicly available professional data in psychotherapy settings. To address this limitation, we introduce Therapy-FIS, a dataset for automated FIS assessment in standardized psychotherapy tasks. To our knowledge, Therapy-FIS is the first publicly available dataset in this domain. It contains facial, acoustic, and textual signals derived from standardized therapist responses to standardized mock patient stimulus clips, based on 890 therapist video recordings and 8 patient videos, and is rated with eight FIS subscale ratings and an overall FIS score. Built on Therapy-FIS, we further establish a benchmark with two downstream tasks: Therapist Performance Rating and Joint Therapist--mock-Patient Performance Rating. We also introduce a competitive baseline, DyadicFIS, with gated fusion and routed readout under therapist-only and dyadic settings. DyadicFIS obtains the best macro CCC(Concordance Correlation Coefficient) scores on both tasks (0.2807 and 0.3299). Therapy-FIS offers a reproducible resource for data-driven automated assessment of therapeutic interpersonal skills in mental health.

</details>


### [81] [BhopalMLS: A LiDAR Dataset for Semantic Scene Understanding in Heterogeneous Urban Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04654)
*Pyare Lal Chauhan,Vaibhav Kumar,Bharat Lohani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文发布BhopalMLS——一个来自印度Bhopal高密度、非规划城市环境的大规模移动激光扫描（MLS）数据集，包含约10 km道路走廊、12亿+点、62个细粒度静态类别（含12类与21类基准体系）及6个可选运动子类，并揭示其相对于既有数据集的非对称迁移优势源于子类型多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D点云数据集多采集于规划良好的城市，结构同质性高、子类型多样性不足，无法覆盖非规划城市中混合交通、非正式结构与狭窄街道等复杂场景，缺乏检验3D感知泛化能力的测试平台。

Method: 构建BhopalMLS数据集（约10 km、12亿点、19,518个标注实例、62个细粒度静态类别，聚合为12类与21类基准分类体系，另设6个运动类子类）；在两种粒度上开展语义与实例分割评测，比较微调基础模型（Utonia(L)、Concerto(L)）与监督基线（如PTv3）；与Ke-MLS、Toronto-3D、Paris-Lille-3D、SemanticKITTI进行双向跨区域迁移实验；并在固定标签预算下做因子控制重训练，将每类坍缩为单一子类型以隔离多样性贡献。

Result: 微调基础模型优于监督基线：Utonia(L)在12类基准上达71.28% mIoU（PTv3从头训练为68.04%）与44.07% mAP50。跨区域实验显示一致的非对称迁移性：在全部数据对中，BhopalMLS训练的模型迁移效果均优于反向，Utonia(L)与Concerto(L)平均提升21.5、最高36 mIoU，且目标域更密集时依然成立。因子控制实验将迁移优势归因于子类型覆盖度而非数据规模或走廊几何：将每类坍缩为单一子类型后，Utonia(L)与Concerto(L)分别损失13.0与13.3 mIoU。

Conclusion: BhopalMLS为超越规划城市的3D感知提供了测试平台；其价值核心在于细粒度子类型多样性带来的更强泛化与迁移能力，而非规模或几何因素。

Abstract: We introduce BhopalMLS, a large-scale Mobile Laser Scanning (MLS) dataset from a dense, heterogeneous urban environment in Bhopal, India, with mixed traffic, informal structures, and narrow unplanned streets. It spans approximately 10 km of road corridors with over 1.2 billion points and 19,518 annotated instances across 62 fine-grained static categories, grouped into 12-class and 21-class benchmark taxonomies; 6 optional moving-class subclasses support dynamic-object analysis. Existing 3D datasets, collected in well-planned cities, are structurally more uniform than such unplanned settings, and lack their subtype diversity. On semantic and instance segmentation at both granularities, fine-tuned foundation models surpass supervised baselines: Utonia(L) reaches 71.28% mIoU (vs. PTv3's 68.04% from scratch) and 44.07% mAP50 on the 12-class benchmark. Cross-region experiments against four established datasets (Ke-MLS, Toronto-3D, Paris-Lille-3D, SemanticKITTI) reveal a consistent asymmetric transferability: models trained on BhopalMLS transfer better than the reverse on every pair, for both Utonia(L) and Concerto(L) (mean 21.5, up to 36 mIoU points), holding even when the target is denser. Factor-controlled retraining at a fixed label budget, repeated on both models, attributes this to the coverage of BhopalMLS's subtype inventory rather than to dataset size or corridor geometry: collapsing each class to a single subtype costs 13.0 and 13.3 mIoU points for Utonia(L) and Concerto(L). BhopalMLS therefore offers a testbed for 3D perception beyond well-planned cities. Data and an interactive viewer are at https://geoai4cities.github.io/bhopalmls.

</details>


### [82] [Phase-DGS: Phase-guided Dynamic Gaussian Splatting from Unsynchronized Multi-view Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04666)
*Hosung Jeon,Jun Young Jeong,Sangwoon Kwak,Joonsoo Kim,Sangmin Kim,Jaesik Park,Won-Sik Cheong,Hyon-Gon Choo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出Phase-DGS，用运动周期内的"语义相位"替代时间戳，实现非同步多相机下的动态高斯泼溅场景重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态高斯泼溅（DGS）重建默认所有相机严格时间同步，但该假设在实际中难以满足；工业级同步依赖昂贵专用硬件，数据驱动的替代方案（音频对齐、几何匹配、位姿追踪）受限于环境或场景特定假设。

Method: 以表示每帧在其 underlying 运动周期中所处固有状态的"语义相位"取代传统时间戳：处于同一运动状态的帧无论由哪台相机、何时拍摄都共享相同相位，从而在非同步相机间自然建立时空对应关系；该相位表示可即插即用集成到RealTime-4DGS、FreeTimeGS等DGS骨干中。

Result: 在时间偏移、丢帧和相机冻结三类退化条件下验证，相比基线最高提升+8.5 dB PSNR，并可与现有DGS骨干无缝集成。

Conclusion: 通过将时间同步问题转化为语义相位对齐问题，Phase-DGS摆脱了对硬件同步与场景特定假设的依赖，为野外非同步多视角动态重建提供了通用且可组合的解决方案。

Abstract: Spatiotemporal scene reconstruction with Dynamic Gaussian Splatting (DGS) fundamentally depends on perfect temporal synchronization across all cameras, an assumption rarely achievable in practice. Industry-standard synchronization requires expensive specialized hardware and complex setup, while data-driven alternatives including audio-based alignment, geometry matching, and pose tracking remain limited by restrictive environmental or scene-specific assumptions. To overcome these limitations, we propose Phase-DGS, a framework that replaces conventional timestamps with a semantic phase representing each frame's inherent state within the underlying motion cycle. Frames capturing the same motion state share the same phase regardless of which camera records them or when, naturally establishing spatiotemporal correspondence across unsynchronized cameras. We validate Phase-DGS under temporal offsets, frame drops, and camera freezes, achieving up to +8.5 dB PSNR improvement over baselines while enabling seamless integration with existing DGS backbones including RealTime-4DGS and FreeTimeGS.

</details>


### [83] [Learning from Unlabeled Photographs for General Oral Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04709)
*Zhong-Shi Zhang,Zi-Li Yu,Xiao Xiao,Huan Liu,Gui-Song Xia,Cui Huang,Meng-Xun Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出DOVE（Dental Optical Visual Encoder），通过对约53万张无标注口腔照片进行领域自适应自监督预训练，获得可迁移的口腔视觉表征。在5个分类、4个分割数据集上与3个基线对比，DOVE在少样本与标签效率上表现最佳：20-shot冻结骨干下分类macro-F1较次优DINOv3高4.37个百分点、分割macro-Dice高4.49个百分点；端到端微调时仅用10%标注数据即可达到最强全数据基线80%以上性能，定性检索与特征分析亦显示更优的表示组织性，支持其作为通用、标签高效的骨干模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 口腔照片蕴含丰富临床信息、AI应用潜力大，但现有模型多为单任务定制且依赖昂贵专家标注，缺乏可跨异构视觉任务迁移的通用表征，作者由此假设：在无标注口腔照片上自监督预训练可得到可迁移的视觉表征。

Method: 构建DOVE，在公开来源采集的约530,000张无标注口腔照片上进行领域自适应自监督预训练；在5个分类数据集和4个分割数据集上，与3个既有基线对比，覆盖冻结骨干评估与端到端微调两种范式，并重点考察少样本迁移与标签效率。

Result: 总体性能最强，在多数基准的少样本迁移与标签效率上排名第一。20-shot冻结骨干协议下，分类任务平均macro-F1较次优DINOv3高4.37个百分点，分割任务平均macro-Dice高4.49个百分点；端到端微调时，多数数据集上仅用10%标注训练数据即可达到最强全数据基线性能的至少80%。定性检索与特征分析表明其图像级与空间表征组织更佳。

Conclusion: DOVE可作为口腔视觉研究中通用性强、标签效率高的骨干网络，验证了无标注口腔图像自监督预训练可产出可迁移表征的假设。

Abstract: Oral photographs contain rich clinical information and hold significant potential for artificial intelligence applications; however, most models are trained for individual tasks and rely on costly expert annotations. We hypothesized that self-supervised training on unlabeled oral photographs could produce transferable visual representations for heterogeneous vision tasks. To test this hypothesis, we developed DOVE (Dental Optical Visual Encoder) by domain-adaptive self-supervised pretraining on approximately 530,000 unlabeled oral photographs collected from publicly accessible sources. DOVE was benchmarked against 3 established baselines across 5 classification datasets and 4 segmentation datasets, covering both frozen backbone evaluation and end-to-end finetuning. DOVE achieved the strongest overall performance, ranking best in few-shot transfer and label efficiency on most benchmarks. Under the 20-shot frozen-backbone protocol, DOVE outperformed the second best DINOv3 by 4.37 percentage points in mean macro-F1 across classification datasets and by 4.49 percentage points in mean macro-Dice across segmentation tasks. Under end-to-end finetuning, DOVE achieved at least 80% of the strongest full-data baseline's performance across most evaluation datasets using only 10% of the labeled training data. Qualitative retrieval and feature analyses further indicated better organization of image-level and spatial representations. These findings support DOVE as a generalizable and label-efficient backbone for oral vision research.

</details>


### [84] [DenseTwins: Dense Modality Decoupling for Multimodal Self-Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04710)
*Carlo Sgaravatti,Daniele Kopyshevskiy,Davide Secco,Giacomo Boracchi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DenseTwins是一个多模态自监督框架，通过将稠密特征空间显式解耦为共享与模态特有分量，学习保留空间结构的稠密表征，在多个多模态基准上取得SOTA或具竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态感知在机器人与自动驾驶等应用中至关重要，各模态提供互补线索；自监督学习可利用大量无标注多模态数据。但现有方法要么强制模态间特征空间对齐而忽略模态特有信息，要么仅学习全局表征而丢失空间结构。

Method: 提出DenseTwins：将稠密特征空间解耦为共享分量与模态特有分量，结合稠密跨模态对应关系与冗余消除目标，构建结构化的稠密嵌入空间；联合优化两个目标——跨增强视图的模态内目标，以及在对齐共享特征的同时对模态特有信息去相关的跨模态目标。

Result: 在多个多模态基准与多种传感配置下评估，性能优于或媲美现有多模态自监督基线，达到SOTA或具竞争力的水平。

Conclusion: 显式的稠密特征解耦结合跨模态对应与冗余消除，能够在保留空间结构的同时兼顾共享语义与模态特有线索，为多模态自监督稠密表征学习提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Multimodal perception is central to many computer vision applications, including robotics and autonomous driving, where different sensing modalities provide complementary cues. Self-supervised learning offers a scalable solution for exploiting large amounts of unlabeled multimodal data. However, existing methods either neglect modality-specific information by enforcing feature-space alignment between modalities or learn only global representations without preserving spatial structure. We propose DenseTwins, a multimodal self-supervised framework that learns dense representations by explicitly decoupling the dense feature space into shared and modality-specific components. The proposed approach combines dense cross-modal correspondences with redundancy-reduction objectives, enabling the model to capture both shared semantics and modality-specific cues within a structured dense embedding space. DenseTwins jointly optimizes an intra-modal objective across augmented views and a cross-modal objective that aligns shared features while decorrelating modality-specific information. We evaluate DenseTwins across multiple multimodal benchmarks and sensing setups, achieving state-of-the-art or competitive performance across multimodal self-supervised baselines. Code available at: https://github.com/CarloSgaravatti/DenseTwins.

</details>


### [85] [Cycle WAM: Coupling Action and Video through Cycle Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04711)
*Qinglin Yang,Jiepeng Wang,Yuxing Huang,Haibin Huang,Kai Xu,Chi Zhang,Ruizhen Hu,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Cycle WAM 统一训练框架，将视频与动作视为由因果关系统一的一对模态，通过双向转换与可微循环重建损失强化跨模态一致性，在 LIBERO-Pro 取得同规模方法 SOTA，并在 DROID 上大幅提升 DreamZero。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAM）虽利用预训练视频先验，或从隐表示解码动作、或从生成视频推断动作，但其训练目标本身并不保证"动作"与"其所预测的视觉后果"相互一致，从而可能限制预训练能力的发挥。论文主张视频与动作是由因果链条关联的配对模态（动作导致场景变化，视觉观测为该动作提供证据），应显式学习二者的对应关系。

Method: 1) 在视频-动作联合训练中加入双向转换（video→action 与 action→video），同时恢复正向动力学与逆向动力学并强化跨模态对应；2) 引入可微的循环重建（cycle-reconstruction）目标，将前向与逆向两个映射进一步耦合，形成闭环约束；3) 最终模型同时支持前向动力学预测与逆向动力学推断。

Result: 在 LIBERO-Pro 上于同类可比方法中达到 SOTA；在 DROID 上相对 DreamZero 有显著提升；与 UWM 的对比刻画了其逆向动力学性能，且其展示的前向动力学预测具有更锐利的视觉细节。

Conclusion: 以因果一致性为核心，将视频与动作作为配对模态进行双向建模并用循环重建目标耦合，是一种更有效利用预训练视频先验的训练范式；该框架统一了前向与逆向动力学，并在多个基准上验证了其性能与视觉质量优势。

Abstract: Recent advances in video modeling have driven substantial progress in world action models (WAMs). Existing approaches exploit pretrained video priors by decoding actions from latent representations or inferring them from generated videos. The underlying link is causal: robot actions produce changes in the scene, and video observations of these changes provide evidence for inferring the actions that produced them. However, existing training formulations do not by themselves ensure that actions and their predicted visual consequences are consistent, potentially limiting the use of pretrained capabilities. We treat video and action as paired modalities linked by this causal relationship and learn their correspondence through a unified training framework designed to better exploit pretrained video priors. Cycle WAM first augments video-action co-training with bidirectional conversion to reinforce cross-modal correspondence and recover forward and inverse dynamics. It then introduces a differentiable cycle-reconstruction objective to further couple the two mappings. The resulting model supports both forward and inverse dynamics, with the potential to improve performance through more effective use of pretrained video priors. Cycle WAM achieves state-of-the-art performance among comparable methods on LIBERO-Pro and substantially improves DreamZero on DROID. Comparisons with UWM characterize its inverse-dynamics performance and show sharper visual detail in the displayed forward-dynamics predictions.

</details>


### [86] [VISAGE: Vertex-based Intrinsic Surface Appearance GEneration via Dual-Stream Diffusion for Arbitrary 3D Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04712)
*Jeremias Krauß,Pragati Jaiswal,René Schuster,Didier Stricker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VISAGE：基于顶点的条件扩散框架，直接在任意网格拓扑上生成逐顶点albedo与roughness，无需UV参数化，FID较最强基线在NPHM上降低42.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏图像恢复3D人头身份一致、物理真实的表面外观仍具挑战：UV管线引入接缝伪影，图像空间方法缺乏表面一致性；且需要一致UV参数化。

Method: 表面条件的扩散框架，逐顶点合成albedo与roughness，适用于任意拓扑且训练无需一致UV。结合全局谱处理与局部测地KNN细化，保持结构连贯并恢复截断谱基之外的细节；多视角RGB通过可见性感知交叉注意力条件化，实现遮挡鲁棒的全头表面合成；区域专用模块处理眼、口、皮肤、毛发；切线空间输出投影恢复虹膜细节；并提出可扩展的程序化增强管线为原始扫描生成物理合理的albedo、roughness、normal用于训练；预测属性直接渲染为单一RGBA顶点颜色通道。

Result: 相对最强基线，FID在NPHM降低42.7%，零样本FaceVerse与i3DMM分别降低22.5%与4.4%；核心网络仅7.2M参数。

Conclusion: 无需UV、拓扑无关的顶点级扩散生成可实现物理一致的头部表面外观，参数量小且泛化性强（零样本迁移有效）。

Abstract: Recovering identity-faithful, physically based surface appearance for 3D human heads from sparse images remains challenging. Existing methods often rely on UV-based pipelines, introducing seam artefacts, or operate in image space, limiting surface consistency. We present VISAGE, Vertex-based Intrinsic Surface Appearance GEneration, a surface-based conditional diffusion framework that synthesises per-vertex albedo and roughness directly on arbitrary mesh topologies, without requiring consistent UV parameterisation at training time. VISAGE combines global spectral processing with local geodesic K-Nearest-Neighbour refinement, enforcing structural coherence while restoring detail beyond the truncated spectral basis. Multi-view RGB images condition the network via visibility-aware cross-attention, enabling occlusion-robust synthesis across the full head surface. Region-specific modules specialise in semantically demanding and distinct areas (eyes, mouth, skin and hair), while a tangent-space output projection recovers fine iris detail. We further introduce a scalable procedural augmentation pipeline that enriches raw geometry scans with physically plausible albedo, roughness and normal maps for training. The predicted attributes render directly as a single RGBA vertex-colour channel. VISAGE reduces FID by 42.7% on NPHM and 22.5% and 4.4% on zero-shot FaceVerse and i3DMM, respectively, relative to the strongest baseline, with a 7.2M-parameter core network.

</details>


### [87] [SPOD: Recovering Suppressed Temporal Evidence in Video-LLMs via Single-Pass Orthogonal Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04728)
*Yangguang Lin,Quan Fang,Xiaoyan Liu,Zefan Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架 SPOD，通过单次前向传播中对隐藏状态做正交分解来恢复被抑制的时间证据，缓解 Video-LLM 的时间幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Video-LLM 在场景转换理解、事件排序等时间敏感任务上存在时间幻觉；现有方案要么需昂贵重训练，要么依赖低效的多遍推理与测试时外部监督。作者观察到时间证据其实已编码在隐藏表示中，只是标准推理未充分利用。

Method: SPOD 为训练无关框架，包含两部分：Frame Distinctiveness Analysis (FDA)，从中间层隐藏状态几何结构识别显著帧；Temporal Delta Subspace (TDS)，基于加权帧间隐藏状态差异构建正交的时间子空间与先验子空间。随后将最终隐藏状态分解为时间、先验、残差三分量并独立缩放，以增强动态信息、抑制静态外观偏置并保留互补信息。

Result: 在 VidHalluc、EventHallusion、VideoMMMU 上，跨多种时间推理任务与模型架构持续优于强推理时基线，同时保持接近原始推理的效率。

Conclusion: 提升 Video-LLM 时间推理不仅依赖额外监督，也可通过恢复内部表示中已存在的时间证据实现；代码已开源。

Abstract: Video Large Language Models (Video-LLMs) have achieved strong performance on many video understanding tasks, yet remain vulnerable to temporal hallucination in time-sensitive reasoning scenarios such as scene transition understanding and event ordering. Existing solutions either require costly retraining or introduce inefficient multi-pass inference and external supervision at test time. In this work, we show that temporal evidence is already encoded in Video-LLM hidden representations but is not fully exploited during standard inference. Motivated by this observation, we propose Single-Pass Orthogonal Decomposition (SPOD), a training-free framework that recovers suppressed temporal evidence through analytical hidden-state manipulation in a single forward pass. SPOD consists of two components: Frame Distinctiveness Analysis (FDA), which identifies salient frames from middle-layer hidden-state geometry, and Temporal Delta Subspace (TDS), which constructs orthogonal temporal and prior subspaces from weighted inter-frame hidden-state differences. By decomposing final hidden states into temporal, prior, and residual components and rescaling them independently, SPOD enhances dynamic information, suppresses static appearance bias, and preserves complementary information. Experiments on VidHalluc, EventHallusion, and VideoMMMU show that SPOD consistently outperforms strong inference-time baselines across temporal reasoning tasks and model architectures while retaining near-vanilla inference efficiency. These results suggest that improving temporal reasoning in Video-LLMs may depend not only on additional supervision, but also on recovering temporal evidence already present in their internal representations. Our code is available at https://github.com/VioAgnes/SPOD.

</details>


### [88] [TEXMET: A Deep Learning Benchmark for Archaeological Textile Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04734)
*Muhammad Hamza Zafar,Davit Gigilashvili,Jon Yngve Hardeberg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TEXMET（18,644张历史纺织图像数据集），为拼图求解提供首个纺织专用基准；系统评估JPDVT、FCViT、DiffAssemble后，经领域微调的DiffAssemble在纺织数据上达85.89%，应用于45件Oseberg碎片使准确率从26.67%提升至64.44%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 考古纺织品（如834 CE Oseberg船葬出土的数百件退化碎片）的拼接重建是计算考古的难题，长期缺乏系统计算分析。深度学习虽革新了图像拼图求解，但考古纺织领域受限于缺少领域专用数据集与对现代架构缺乏系统评估，故需填补此空白。

Method: 构建TEXMET数据集（来自大都会艺术博物馆开放获取馆藏的18,644张历史纺织图像），作为首个纺织专用拼图基准；在该数据集上系统评估三种先进拼图模型JPDVT、FCViT、DiffAssemble，并进行领域特定微调，最终将微调后的模型应用于45件高度退化的Oseberg碎片。

Result: 在标准数据集上预训练的Transformer类模型无法迁移到纺织数据；领域微调效果显著。图结构的DiffAssemble经TEXMET微调后在纺织数据上达到85.89%拼图准确率；应用于Oseberg碎片时，准确率由26.67%提升至64.44%。

Conclusion: TEXMET确立了纺织重建的新基准，并证明领域自适应是将拼图求解方法应用于真实考古材料图像的必要条件。

Abstract: The reconstruction of fragmented historical textiles presents a significant challenge in computational archaeology. The Oseberg ship burial, dating to 834 CE, yielded hundreds of degraded textile fragments that have resisted systematic computational analysis. While deep learning has revolutionized image-based puzzle solving, progress on archaeological textiles has been limited by the lack of domain-specific datasets and systematic evaluation of modern architectures. To address this gap, we present TEXMET, a curated dataset of 18,644 historical textile images from the Metropolitan Museum of Art's Open Access collection, representing the first textile-specific benchmark for puzzle-solving research. Using TEXMET, we conduct a systematic evaluation of three state-of-the-art puzzle-solving models: JPDVT, FCViT, and DiffAssemble. Our experiments reveal that the transformer-based models pre-trained on standard datasets fail to generalize to textiles, but domain-specific fine-tuning proves highly effective. The graph-based DiffAssemble model, when fine-tuned on TEXMET, achieves 85.89% puzzle accuracy on textile data. Most significantly, applying this fine-tuned model to 45 highly degraded Oseberg fragments yields a performance increase from 26.67% to 64.44% puzzle accuracy. These results establish TEXMET as a new benchmark for textile reconstruction and demonstrate that domain adaptation is essential for applying puzzle-solving methods to imagery of real archaeological materials.

</details>


### [89] [Bounded AUKS: A Novel Evaluation Metric for Anomaly Detection via Bounded Area Under the Kolmogorov--Smirnov Curve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04754)
*Yanshuo Zhang,Juan Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文指出AUROC/AUPRO在MVTec AD等数据集上接近饱和，且忽略了阈值选择与FPR-FNR权衡，提出基于KS积分的数据驱动部分积分指标Bounded AUKS，用于量化正常与异常分数分布的可分性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全局阈值无关指标（AUROC、AUPRO）已接近100%，误导性地暗示像素级异常检测任务已被解决；AUROC忽略实际应用中不可避免的阈值选择过程，AUPRO虽限制FPR范围但极低FPR会导致FNR升高；缺乏能刻画正常与异常分数分布分离程度的指标，阻碍了领域进步。

Method: 从KS（Kolmogorov-Smirnov）积分出发引入AUKS，作为异常分数分布可分性的直接度量；提出Bounded AUKS：在数据驱动的有界区间内对TPR与FPR之差进行部分积分。区间下界排除无信息的高FPR区域，上界避免在极端FPR趋零、召回极低的区域评估，边界仅由正常测试样本的分数分布确定。

Result: 在MVTec AD与VisA数据集上对9个模型的实验表明，Bounded AUKS能弥补现有指标的局限，在评估无监督异常检测模型时表现更优。

Conclusion: Bounded AUKS通过限定数据驱动的积分区间，同时规避高FPR无信息区与极低FPR导致低召回的极端区，可有效量化分数分布可分性，为无监督异常检测提供更合理的评价手段。

Abstract: Recent literature widely used global metrics such as AUROC and AUPRO to quantify the detection performance. AUROC and AUPRO scores are approaching 100% in some public datasets such as MVTec AD dataset and VisA dataset, which misleadingly suggests that the pixel-level anomaly detection task has already been solved. Threshold-independent metrics represented by AUROC inherently ignore the threshold selection process, which is inevitable in practical applications. Although AUPRO restricts the False Positive rates (FPRs) to an acceptable range, extremely low FPRs can lead to increased false negative rates (FNRs). We argue that there is a lack of metrics capable of quantifying the separation between the anomaly score distributions of normal and anomalous samples, which hinders advancements in anomaly detection. In response, we propose the Bounded Area Under the Kolmogorov--Smirnov curve (Bounded AUKS), a partial integral metric that integrates the difference between TPR and FPR within a data-driven bounded range. Specifically, we introduce AUKS from KS integral as a direct measure of separability between the anomaly score distributions. The bounded range is determined only by score distributions of normal test samples. Lower bound excludes the uninformative high-FPR region. Upper bound avoids evaluation in extreme regions that aggressively push FPR towards zero, which result in a very low recall. Our experiments on 9 models across MVTec AD and VisA datasets show that Bounded AUKS can address the limitations of existing metrics and has a better performance in evaluating unsupervised anomaly detection models.

</details>


### [90] [iMED: A Multi-Endoscope Dataset for Surgical 3D Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04755)
*Sierra Bonilla,Fengyi Jiang,Chinedu Nwoye,Jingpei Lu,Kailey Reardon,Humphrey W. Chow,Francisco Vasconcelos,Sophia Bano,Adam Schmidt,Omid Mohareri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出iMED，首个面向机器人辅助微创手术（RAMIS）的同步多内窥镜数据集，填补了现有手术视觉基准缺乏独立留出视点的空白，并据此重新定义了评价几何泛化能力的基准协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术视觉数据集多来自单一内窥镜的单条轨迹序列，无法区分模型是真正实现了跨视点几何泛化，还是仅在狭窄前向路径上做光度插值。缺少独立留出视点使数据集成为评测的根本瓶颈。

Method: 构建iMED数据集：由两台独立双目内窥镜同步采集，含340条序列、约170K同步时间点（每点4个视角），覆盖离体、尸检与活体三类场景、14个标本及多种解剖区域与运动模式；提供标定后的相机内参、基于ArUco的逐帧位姿估计及不确定性量化、器械分割掩膜与临床元数据。采用“一镜训练、另一镜测试”的留出内窥镜评估协议，对刚性/非刚性新视角合成、位姿估计、特征匹配与单目深度估计四类任务上的23种SOTA方法进行基准测试。

Result: 实验表明，主要依赖光度监督的方法在留出内窥镜设定下性能显著退化；带有显式几何正则的方法跨视角更为鲁棒。此外，大规模基础模型向手术图像的迁移效果出乎意料地好，常优于领域专用模型，提示现有微调流程可能存在缺陷。

Conclusion: iMED为手术视觉中的几何泛化建立了新的评估协议，揭示了光度监督范式的局限与基础模型迁移的潜力。

Abstract: We introduce iMED, the first synchronized multi-endoscope dataset for robot-assisted minimally invasive surgery (RAMIS), designed to address a fundamental limitation in surgical vision benchmarking: the absence of independent held-out viewpoints. Existing surgical datasets capture single-trajectory sequences from a single endoscope, making it impossible to distinguish geometric generalization from photometric interpolation along a narrow forward-facing path. iMED provides 340 sequences ~170K synchronized timepoints with 4 views per timepoint) recorded simultaneously from two independent stereo endoscopes across ex vivo, postmortem, and live surgical settings, spanning 14 specimens with diverse anatomical regions and motion regimes. The dataset includes calibrated camera intrinsics, ArUco-based frame-wise pose estimates with uncertainty quantification, instrument segmentation masks, and rich clinical metadata. Using a train-on-one-endoscope, test-on-another evaluation protocol, we benchmark 23 state-of-the-art methods across rigid and deformable novel view synthesis, pose estimation, feature matching, and monocular depth estimation. Our experiments show that methods relying primarily on photometric supervision degrade substantially under this held-out-endoscope setting, while methods with explicit geometric regularization are more robust across views in our benchmark. Notably, large-scale foundation models transfer surprisingly well to surgical imagery, often outperforming domain-specific models, suggesting pitfalls in current fine-tuning protocols. iMED establishes a new evaluation protocol for geometric generalization in surgical vision.

</details>


### [91] [Beyond Local Refinement: Wide-Area Precise Cross-View Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04759)
*Qingguo Hu,Xinyu Ma,Yujie Lin,Yaoxiang Wang,Qi Shi,Zonghao Guo,Chi Chen,Jinsong Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RingMatch：面向广域（最大400 m²级航片区）地面相机-航空影像细粒度跨视角定位的“搜索-验证”框架，用预测不确定性引导渐进缩放生成候选，并用“深度环”几何表征配合LVLM验证器消解歧义；在VIGOR-Pro基准上随航区扩大仍保持强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有细粒度跨视角定位方法普遍假设较窄的地理先验（搜索范围小），当上游粗定位不够精确时适用性大幅受限。将任务扩展到更大航区并非简单放大：地-空视角空间范围差异增大，直接跨视角匹配难以可靠建立；同时大区域内相似地点增多带来严重视觉歧义。

Method: 提出RingMatch搜索-验证框架：(1) 利用预测不确定性发现可能位置，并对其周边逐步放大（progressive zoom-in）以获取由粗到细的证据，得到精简的高潜力候选集；(2) 引入depth ring——一种新的几何表征，刻画某位置周边空间布局的细微变化，作为视角共享的空间签名，供LVLM验证器消解残余歧义。同时构建VIGOR-Pro基准，将VIGOR航片拼合为连续区域，空间范围可达400 m。

Result: 随着航区范围扩大，RingMatch仍保持较强的定位性能，并持续优于代表性的端到端方法与检索式方法。

Conclusion: 通过不确定性驱动的渐进搜索与基于depth ring的LVLM验证相结合，可在广域条件下实现可扩展的细粒度跨视角定位；VIGOR-Pro为更大搜索范围下的评测提供了基准。

Abstract: Fine-grained cross-view localization aims to precisely localize a ground camera against geo-referenced aerial imagery, but current methods typically assume a narrow geographic prior, substantially limiting their applicability when upstream localization remains coarse. To bridge this gap, we study this task over much larger aerial regions. However, this seemingly simple extension fundamentally changes the localization problem: the growing disparity in spatial extent between ground and aerial views makes reliable cross-view matching difficult to establish directly, while the increasing number of look-alike locations in larger regions introduces substantial visual ambiguity. To address these challenges, we propose RingMatch, a search-and-verification framework for wide-area cross-view localization. RingMatch first leverages predictive uncertainty to uncover plausible locations and progressively zooms into their surroundings to obtain increasingly fine-grained evidence, yielding a compact set of promising candidates. We then introduce the depth ring, a novel geometric representation that reveals subtle variations in the spatial layout around a location and serves as a view-shared spatial signature for an LVLM verifier to resolve residual ambiguity. To benchmark this setting, we construct VIGOR-Pro by composing VIGOR aerial tiles into contiguous regions with spatial extents of up to 400 m. Experiments show that RingMatch maintains strong localization performance as the aerial region expands, consistently outperforming representative end-to-end and retrieval-based methods.

</details>


### [92] [Pixel-space video generation with U-JiT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04764)
*Théophane Vallaeys,Jakob Verbeek,Matthieu Cord*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探索直接在像素空间做生成式视频建模，提出UNet-like的Transformer架构U-JiT及改进的训练与采样策略，在Kinetics-600上像素空间模型优于潜在空间模型，且优势随分辨率与压缩率增大而扩大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SOTA视频生成依赖预训练自编码器将数据压缩到潜空间，但存在两大缺陷：训练分两阶段（自编码器预训练+潜空间生成模型训练），无法端到端；自编码器带来固有信号损失，重构质量与生成性能之间存在权衡。

Method: 分析近期基于基础Transformer设计的像素空间图像生成方法，指出其在高维像素空间视频生成中的不足；提出UNet-like的Transformer架构（U-JiT），并改进训练配方与采样策略；在Kinetics-600上从零训练潜空间与像素空间模型进行受控对比实验。

Result: 128分辨率帧到视频任务上，U-JiT的FVD较先前SOTA提升27%，较JiT像素基线提升14%；扩展到256分辨率时，随时空压缩率增至64×64×8，相对像素与潜空间模型的优势进一步扩大；类别到视频任务中，逐帧FID较潜空间模型提升27%、较像素空间模型提升61%，FVD分别降低9%和56%。

Conclusion: 像素空间视频生成可与甚至超越潜空间方案，在去除了自编码器依赖的同时实现更强性能，高分辨率下优势更为显著。

Abstract: Latent diffusion and flow matching models are ubiquitous in state-of-the-art generative video models. While highly effective, relying on pretrained autoencoders to compress the data into a lower-dimensional latent space also comes with drawbacks. First, training proceeds in two stages: autoencoder pretraining, with compression and reconstruction objectives, followed by latent generative model training, precluding fully end-to-end training. Second, autoencoders also lead to an inherent loss of signal, and the trade-off between reconstruction quality and generative performance in the latent space is still an active area of research. In this paper we explore the viability of generative video models directly in pixel space. We analyze recent approaches in pixel-space generative modeling of images that rely on basic transformer designs, and identify several shortcomings that limit their effectiveness for the high-dimensional pixel-space video generation. To overcome these limitations, we propose a UNet-like transformer architecture along with improvements to the training recipe and sampling strategy. We conduct controlled experiments, training latent and pixel-space models from scratch on Kinetics-600. For the standard frames-to-video task at 128 resolution, our U-JiT model improves generative FVD by 27% over prior state-of-the-art results, and by 14% against the JiT pixel-space baseline. Scaling to higher 256 resolution, we find the improvement over pixel-space and latent-based models grows as we increase the spatiotemporal compression rate up to $64 \times 64 \times 8$. Finally, we find that U-JiT improves per-frame FID by 27% over latent models and 61% over pixel-space models on class-to-video generation, with respectively 9% and 56% reduction in FVD.

</details>


### [93] [NightIQA: A Comprehensive Dataset and Benchmark for MLLM-Based Nighttime Image Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04811)
*Yiding Tian,Jiaojiao Yi,Jiayuan Lu,Pufan Xu,Jinlin Wu,Changyuan Wen,Hongyuan Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出NightIQA数据集与基准套件（真实数据、多选题基准、场景感知合成管线），用于MLLM夜间图像质量评估，微调后Qwen3-VL-8B超越开源方法并优于主流闭源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 夜间图像存在复杂复合退化，评分式IQA只输出单一分数，缺乏多维度、语言化推理能力；MLLM虽有潜力，但现有真实IQA数据集以白天为主且采集陈旧，合成数据又缺乏夜间专属退化建模，存在关键数据缺口。

Method: 构建NightIQA三部分：(1) NightIQA-Real，自采1,769张高分辨率真实图像，覆盖六类典型失真并带结构化多维标注；(2) NightIQA-Bench，1,078道视觉接地多选题，按感知、技术评估、定位、推理四级评估层级组织；(3) NightIQA-Syn，场景感知合成管线，联合建模全局级与实例级退化，初始发布8万张高保真图像及完整生成代码。采用SFT与GRPO对Qwen3-VL-8B进行微调。

Result: 微调后的Qwen3-VL-8B优于开源IQA方法，整体准确率超过领先闭源模型。

Conclusion: 领域专属数据与结构化监督对夜间图像质量评估具有显著价值，NightIQA为MLLM-based夜间IQA提供了数据与评测基础。

Abstract: Nighttime images are inherently susceptible to complex, compound degradations, making accurate quality assessment exceptionally challenging. Score-based IQA methods condense quality into a single number, lacking the multi-dimensional, language-grounded reasoning required for nighttime evaluation. Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) offer a more comprehensive alternative, yet remain constrained by a critical data gap: existing real-world IQA datasets are overwhelmingly daytime-centric and often built from outdated captures, while synthetic alternatives lack nighttime-specific degradation modeling, yielding artifacts far removed from authentic nighttime conditions. To bridge this gap, we present NightIQA, a dataset and benchmark suite purpose-built for MLLM-based nighttime IQA. It comprises three core components: NightIQA-Real, a real-world dataset of 1,769 high-resolution images that we captured ourselves with modern equipment, encompassing six canonical distortion types and structured multi-dimensional annotations; NightIQA-Bench, a benchmark of 1,078 visually grounded multiple-choice questions tailored to MLLM-based nighttime IQA, organized around a four-level evaluation hierarchy spanning Perception, Technical Assessment, Localization, and Reasoning; and NightIQA-Syn, an inherently scalable, scene-aware synthesis pipeline that jointly models global-level and instance-level degradations, with an initial release of 80k high-fidelity images alongside the full generation code. Fine-tuned on NightIQA with Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), Qwen3-VL-8B outperforms open-source IQA methods and achieves higher overall accuracy than leading closed-source models, highlighting the value of domain-specific data and structured supervision.

</details>


### [94] [Radial distortion homography estimation from affine-covariant or orientation-covariant features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04822)
*Marcus Valtonen Örnhag,Stefan Adalbjörnsson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出利用仿射/方向协变特征联合估计单应矩阵与径向畸变的最小解算器，通过引入基于局部特征几何的新约束，减少所需对应点数量并提升鲁棒估计效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点基方法联合估计单应与径向畸变通常需要较多对应点，且在存在外点时计算开销大。作者希望借助仿射描述子固有的局部几何信息构造更高效的约束，从而以更少的匹配实现稳健估计。

Method: 作者设计了一组新约束，利用仿射描述子的局部特征几何（仿射形状与方向信息），构建单应矩阵与径向畸变参数联合求解的最小解算器，并将其嵌入现代鲁棒估计框架中进行假设生成与验证。

Result: 在合成图像与含挑战性鱼眼畸变的真实图像上，所提解算器在外点存在时相比当前最先进方法显著提速，同时保持相当或更优的估计精度，并且所需对应点少于点基方法。

Conclusion: 利用仿射特征局部几何构造的约束可有效降低联合估计的最小样本量，使单应与径向畸变联合估计在鲁棒框架下更高效、更实用。

Abstract: We present minimal solvers for joint homography and radial distortion estimation using affine- or orientation-covariant features. The core contribution is a set of novel constraints that exploit local feature geometry inherent to affine descriptors, enabling solvers that require fewer correspondences than point-based methods. Using modern robust estimation frameworks, we demonstrate that the proposed solvers are advantageous to the current state-of-the-art with a significant speed-up in the presence of outliers while maintaining comparable or superior estimation accuracy. The proposed solvers are tested on both synthetic and real images containing challenging fisheye distortions.

</details>


### [95] [OCR in the Foundation Model Era: From Specialist Models to Unified Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04874)
*Zhiheng Li,Zongyang Ma,Jiaxian Chen,Cheng Cui,Yi Liu,Ziqi Zhang,Chunfeng Yuan,Bing Li,Xiang Bai,Weiming Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文是基础模型时代的OCR综述，以"忠实转录"定义OCR，梳理从专用模型到统一模型的演进，将当代OCR系统归纳为五类，比较其架构/数据/训练，并讨论幻觉、空间定位不足、推理成本及评测体系缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OCR是最古老、部署最广的AI应用之一，但在基础模型时代其定义模糊、系统形态分化、生成式方法引入幻觉与成本问题，且评测缺乏一致的输出定义与对真实采集条件的覆盖。因此需要一篇综述厘清概念、统一分类框架并指出研究缺口。

Method: 综述性研究：以"忠实转录"为准则给出OCR定义（在指定视觉范围内完整重建文本、符号与版面结构，且输出中每一元素均可在图像中找到视觉证据）；沿"专用OCR模型→统一模型"的脉络考察检测与识别联合、结构化序列生成、预训练视觉语言模型（VLM）的使用；将现有系统分为五个并存的家族：模块化工具包与任务专用模型、专用文档VLM、混合流程、通用VLM、商业产品与托管服务；对这些家族在架构、数据与训练上进行对比分析；并系统梳理评测从字符级/场景文本识别到结构元素解析与整页转录的演进。

Result: 形成五大家族分类并完成架构、数据、训练的横向比较；识别出生成式OCR的关键问题包括幻觉与漏识、空间定位（grounding）不足和推理成本高；评测方面指出现有基准在输出定义一致性、性能归因于单个组件、以及对真实拍摄条件和可见内容/结构的覆盖上存在缺口。

Conclusion: 基础模型时代的OCR已从专用流水线走向统一与生成式范式，但忠实性、可验证性与评测规范仍是核心挑战；论文总结当前挑战并给出未来研究方向，同时维护开源论文列表以持续跟踪进展。

Abstract: Optical Character Recognition (OCR) is among the oldest and most widely deployed applications of artificial intelligence. This survey reviews its development, focusing on the Foundation Model Era. We define OCR through faithful transcription: within a specified visual scope, OCR should completely reconstruct the text, notation, and document structure observable in the source image, and every textual, notational, or structural element in the output must be verifiable from visual evidence in that image. We trace the development from specialist OCR models toward unified models, examining joint text detection and recognition, structured sequence generation, and the use of pretrained vision-language models (VLMs). We organize contemporary OCR systems into five coexisting families: modular toolkits and task-specific models, specialized document VLMs, hybrid workflows, general-purpose VLMs, and commercial products and managed services. We compare their architectures, data, and training, examining hallucination and omission, insufficient spatial grounding, and inference cost in generative OCR. We review evaluation from character and scene text recognition to parsing individual structural elements and full-page transcription, highlighting gaps in consistent output definitions, attribution of performance to individual components, and coverage of real capture conditions and visible content and structure. Finally, we summarize the challenges facing OCR and discuss directions for future research. We continuously track related work at https://github.com/zhihengli-casia/Awesome-OCR-in-the-Foundation-Model-Era.

</details>


### [96] [VisSearch-Bench: Evaluating Long Horizon Visual Search in Multimodal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04894)
*ZhengXian Wu,Qianhao Nie,Zhuohong Chen,Junjie Gao,Yuyao Zhang,Yunyao Yu,Hangrui Xu,Cheng Cheng,Guiyu Ma,Jin Zhang,Xingzhong Xu,Kai Yang,Haoqian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出VisSearch-Bench基准，聚焦多模态智能体的"视觉上下文管理"能力，包含204个人工编写任务（7大领域、25个子领域），覆盖5类搜索范式；16个多模态模型中最优系统仅完整解决26.47%的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图文搜索任务中，图像多仅用于识别初始实体，随后依赖文本检索，难以揭示智能体如何在图像、物体与搜索步骤之间建立信息联系，因此需要专门评测视觉上下文管理能力，即组织并利用视觉信息、并将其与检索发现相链接的能力。

Method: 构建VisSearch-Bench，含204个人工编写任务，覆盖7个主领域、25个子领域，包含五类任务：集合式视觉搜索、序列式视觉搜索、混合宽深搜索、竞争性视觉消歧、视觉结构引导搜索。每题提供参考答案、可追溯证据与评分规则；采用Accuracy衡量任务整体成功，Process Score衡量单项结果与子问题答案的正确性与完整性。

Result: 在16个多模态模型上评测，即使配备搜索工具，最佳系统也仅完整解决26.47%的任务。

Conclusion: 结果凸显了将视觉证据与检索结果相链接、进而产出完整答案的难度，视觉上下文管理是多模态搜索智能体的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Multimodal agents use web and image tools to answer questions beyond their internal knowledge. Some search tasks use images mainly to identify an initial entity, then rely on text retrieval. These tasks offer limited insight into how agents connect information across images, objects, and search steps. We therefore focus on visual context management. This involves organizing and using visual information and linking it to other findings during search. Visually intensive tasks require agents to combine object appearance, local details, or spatial relations with external information. We introduce VisSearch-Bench, with 204 human-written tasks across 7 main domains and 25 subdomains. It covers Set-wise Visual Search, Sequential Visual Search, Hybrid Wide-Deep Search, Competitive Visual Disambiguation, and Visual-Structure-Guided Search. Each task provides a reference answer, traceable evidence, and scoring rules. Accuracy measures complete task success, while Process Score measures the correctness and completeness of individual results and subquestion answers. Across 16 multimodal models, the best system fully solves only 26.47% of tasks, even with search tools. These results highlight the challenge of linking visual evidence with search results to produce complete answers.

</details>


### [97] [Heads That Write, Not Just Point: Image Retrieval Heads in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04903)
*Junsung Park,Sangwon Yu,Donghun Kang,Yeongtak Oh,Jaewon Jeong,Gyeongtae Yoo,Han Cheol Moon,Jungbeom Lee,Sungroh Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将在上下文多图问答中为问题定位相关图像的过程建模为“in-context image retrieval”，提出 ICIR-MCQ 探针，并区分指向头（pointer head）与写出头（writer head）两种机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM 能在单一上下文中处理交错文本与多张图像，但其内部如何识别与问题相关的图像（grounding 步骤）尚不清楚，现有研究缺乏对头级机制的可控分析手段。

Method: 构建 ICIR-MCQ 受控探针；以各注意力头在图像 token 片段上的注意力质量识别稀疏 pointer heads；并引入输出侧分析：对每个头的 value-weighted output 训练轻量图像检索器以识别 writer heads；再通过针对性的集合划分消融（set-partition ablation）评估各类头对多图推理的因果贡献，并与随机头对照。

Result: pointer heads 可迁移至自然多图检索基准，单个 pointer head 与外部视觉-语言检索器性能相当；pointer 与 writer 判据选出的头集合差异显著，top-100 中仅 35–47 个头重叠；消融显示对标准多图推理最重要的头集中于 pointer–writer 交集，仅满足单一判据的头相对随机头对照分离不明显。

Conclusion: LVLM 内部存在强图像检索信号，但“从何处读取”（attention）与“向后续层传递什么”（output）需互补刻画；最一致的因果效应来自同时满足注意力指针与输出写出的头，注意力分析单独使用不足以定位关键头。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) can process interleaved text and multiple images in a single context, but how they internally identify the image relevant to a question remains poorly understood. We study this grounding step as in-context image retrieval and introduce ICIR-MCQ, a controlled probe for head-level analysis of image retrieval. Using each head's attention mass over image-token spans, we first identify sparse pointer heads that reliably point to the target image. These heads transfer beyond the probe: a single pointer head is competitive with external vision-language retrievers on a naturalistic multi-image retrieval benchmark, showing that LVLMs contain a strong internal image retrieval signal. Attention alone, however, shows where a head reads from, not what its output passes on to later layers, so we introduce a complementary output-side analysis. For each head, we train a lightweight image retriever on its value-weighted output and use it to identify writer heads, whose outputs contain enough information to identify the target image. Surprisingly, the pointer and writer criteria select substantially different head sets, with only 35 to 47 heads overlapping among the top-100 heads under each criterion. Targeted set-partition ablations show that the heads most important for standard multi-image inference are concentrated in this pointer-writer intersection, while heads selected by only one criterion show less consistent separation from random-head controls. The most consistent causal effects arise from heads selected by both the attention-based pointer and output-side writer criteria. Code and data: https://github.com/parkquasar/Image-Retrieval-Heads.

</details>


### [98] [MiCaDe: Online LiDAR-Camera Miscalibration Change Detection for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04912)
*Carlo Sgaravatti,Riccardo Pieroni,Matteo Corno,Sergio M. Savaresi,Luca Magri,Giacomo Boracchi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MiCaDe 提出一种在线外参失准变化检测方法，利用跨模态检测框的几何一致性指标与变化点模型（CPM），在无标定靶标、不改动感知栈的前提下以低误报率检测相机-LiDAR 外参漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期部署中振动、热效应与轻微机械冲击会导致相机-LiDAR 相对位姿变化，产生不可忽视的标定漂移，进而给下游融合任务带来系统性误差；而现有方案多依赖标定靶标或修改感知流程，成本高、部署不便。

Method: 核心洞察是标定正确时标准目标检测器输出的 3D 与 2D 检测框应保持几何一致。方法通过跨模态检测匹配导出几何一致性度量，作为外参漂移的敏感指标；将度量聚合为定长批次并与参考分布比对打分，生成似然流，再由 Change Point Model（CPM）监控，借助时间累积统计证据检测细微漂移。

Result: 在多个长序列数据集以及一个物理施加失准的真实数据集上的实验表明，该方法在多种条件下均能实现可靠检测，误报率低、计算开销可忽略，且无需标定靶标或修改感知栈。

Conclusion: MiCaDe 将跨模态几何一致性与序贯变化点检测结合，为自动驾驶长期运行提供了一种实用、轻量且即插即用的外参失准在线监测手段。

Abstract: Reliable multi-sensor perception in autonomous vehicles requires accurate calibration between cameras and LiDAR. However, calibration drift is often unavoidable in long-term deployments: vibrations, thermal effects, and minor mechanical shocks may alter the relative pose between sensors, leading to systematic errors in downstream fusion tasks. We propose MiCaDe (Miscalibration Change Detection), an online solution detecting when the extrinsic LiDAR-camera calibration parameters are no longer accurate by means of sequential change detection. MiCaDe requires no calibration targets, or modification of the perception stack, providing low false-alarm rates with negligible computational overhead. Our key insight is that 3D and 2D bounding boxes produced by standard object detectors should remain geometrically consistent under correct calibration. By matching cross-modal detections, we derive geometric consistency metrics that serve as sensitive indicators of extrinsic drift. These metrics are aggregated into fixed-size batches and scored against a reference distribution, producing a likelihood stream that is monitored by a Change Point Model (CPM). Crucially, the CPM accumulates statistical evidence over time, enabling detection of subtle drifts that would otherwise go unnoticed. Experiments on multiple long-sequence datasets and on a real-world dataset with physically induced miscalibration confirm reliable detection under diverse conditions. Code available at: https://github.com/CarloSgaravatti/MiCaDe.

</details>


### [99] [EP-LVMQA: Evidence-Preserving Coarse-to-Fine Localization for Low-Quality Long Video Moment Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04913)
*Guoning Chen,Zhexun Hu,Yifan Wei,Yixuan Weng,Bin Li,Yufei Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 EP-LVMQA，一个证据保留的由粗到细框架，用于长视频时刻问答（LVMQA），返回带时间戳的答案支撑证据而非标签或自由文本，并在 MedVidQA 上显著超越现有方法且抗低质量扰动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长教学/培训视频的证据常不完整或不可靠：字幕缺失、ASR噪声、OCR稀疏、关键动作短暂且无旁白。现有 VideoQA 系统只输出标签或自由文本，难以检验答案支撑，因此需要可返回带时间戳证据的 LVMQA 设定。

Method: EP-LVMQA 采用由粗到细架构，包含三部分：不确定性感知多模态证据时间线（Uncertainty-Aware Multimodal Evidence Timeline）、粗粒度证据规划器（Coarse Evidence Planner）、证据条件细粒度定位器（Evidence-Conditioned Fine Localizer）。后两个模块间通过"证据卡"传递规划器分数、匹配线索、源置信度代理、模态特定证据、邻域上下文和时间先验。

Result: 在 MedVidQA 上取得 26.1 R1@0.5 与 31.8 mIoU，较最强非本方法分别提升 6.3 与 5.1 点；Suff.@2 与 Ver.@2 达 25.4 与 22.6，分别提升 9.5 与 8.8 点；在匹配的低质量扰动下平均相对退化从 14.6% 降至 11.1%。在 Ego4D-NLQ 与 QVHighlights 上与既有任务专用定位方法总体相当。

Conclusion: EP-LVMQA 通过显式保留多模态证据与不确定性，实现可检验的带时间戳证据输出，在医学教学视频上显著优于基线并具备更强鲁棒性，且在其他定位基准上保持可比的泛化表现。

Abstract: Long instructional videos are increasingly used in medical education, industrial training, and procedural knowledge transfer, yet their evidence is often incomplete or unreliable: subtitles may be missing, ASR noisy, OCR sparse, and decisive actions briefly shown without narration. Existing VideoQA systems typically return labels or free-form text, making answer support difficult to inspect. We study Long Video Moment Question Answering (LVMQA), where a system returns timestamped answer-supporting evidence. We propose EP-LVMQA, an evidence-preserving coarse-to-fine framework comprising an Uncertainty-Aware Multimodal Evidence Timeline, a Coarse Evidence Planner, and an Evidence-Conditioned Fine Localizer. An evidence card transfers planner scores, matched cues, source-confidence proxies, modality-specific evidence, neighboring context, and a temporal prior between the latter two modules. On MedVidQA, EP-LVMQA achieves 26.1 R1@0.5 and 31.8 mIoU, improving the strongest non-ours results by 6.3 and 5.1 points. It raises Suff.@2 and Ver.@2 to 25.4 and 22.6, gains of 9.5 and 8.8 points. Under matched low-quality perturbations, average relative degradation is reduced from the strongest non-ours value of 14.6% to 11.1%. On Ego4D-NLQ and QVHighlights, EP-LVMQA remains broadly comparable to established task-specific localization methods.

</details>


### [100] [Behind Video Generation: Observations and Insights on Multimodal Understanding from Kling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04916)
*Kling Team,Dong Chen,Jialu Chen,Xinlong Chen,Yifan Dai,Haotian Dong,Binzong Geng,Yushuo Guan,Minghui Guo,Guanlin He,Guanxiao He,Jingyun Hua,Yanwei Huang,Yiyan Ji,Xueping Jia,Yizhuo Jia,Bozhou Li,Jiaze Li,Min Li,Shihao Li,Shujie Li,Shengyi Liao,Weihong Lin,Shiyuan Liu,Xinyu Liu,Siyang Mao,Xiaoru Qu,Dianmo Sheng,Yang Shi,Jiafu Tang,Longxiang Tang,Yuqi Tang,Chengzhuo Tong,Kuo Wang,Qixun Wang,Rui Wang,Tianduo Wang,Yuran Wang,Zekun Wang,Zihao Wang,Boyang Xia,Yifan Xu,Liu Yang,Zhenlong Yuan,Bohan Zeng,Mujin Zhang,Yuanxing Zhang,Zhicheng Zhang,Zhuoran Zhang,Yuanhong Zheng,Kun Gai,Chengru Song,Pengfei Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该报告总结Kling多代模型在“多模态理解”上的经验，提出生成质量是理解能力沿“数据—条件—对齐—评价”全链路的投影，并以Kling-MLLM统一底座逐模块优化该链路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成已从孤立片段走向连贯的短时音视频序列，瓶颈由渲染能力转向语义控制，因此需要系统梳理支撑可控生成的理解栈（understanding stack）。

Method: 以Kling-MLLM（统一图像、视频、音频、同步音视频与交错输入的多模态基础模型）为共享底座，按模块专门化：1）将captioning视为“语义契约”，最大化描述密度，并扩展为显式绑定参考对象的reference-grounded captioning；2）Prompt enhancement将用户意图映射到该caption分布，基础PE源自优质视频caption并经文本级与generator-in-the-loop强化学习精炼，agentic PE负责精炼故事并检索优质范例；3）论证可靠text encoder来自感知训练而非更强语言能力，并支持仅凭文本在端到端训练前筛选候选；4）奖励建模采用通用奖励模型（可解释、局部化批评）与决策奖励模型（快速多维打分）配对，并将感知带宽作为一等设计参数；5）任务特定的自动评测，聚焦可暴露评分捷径、可回溯上游失败源头的协议。

Result: 形成一条覆盖数据、条件、对齐与评测的完整理解链路方法论，并给出若干可操作结论：caption密度与参考绑定直接影响DiT学习效果；PE可通过强化学习对齐caption分布；text encoder质量取决于感知训练；候选可在文本层面低成本预筛；感知带宽应显式设计于奖励模型中。

Conclusion: 生成质量是多模态理解能力在全链路投影的结果；构建可控视频生成需同步建设理解栈，包括高密度语义契约式标注、面向caption分布的意图翻译、感知驱动的文本编码器、分层的奖励建模以及可诊断失败来源的自动评测。

Abstract: Video generation has progressed from isolated clips to coherent short-form audiovisual sequences, and the bottleneck is shifting from rendering to semantic control. This report summarizes the observations and insights on multimodal understanding gained from successive generations of Kling. We argue that generation quality is the projection of multimodal understanding through the entire data--conditioning--alignment--evaluation chain, and we examine this chain module by module. Kling-MLLM, a shared multimodal foundation model that unifies image, video, audio, synchronized audio--video, and interleaved inputs, is specialized for each downstream module. Because a DiT is trained on far fewer training samples than a language model, we treat captioning as a semantic contract that maximizes description density, and extend it to reference-grounded captioning that makes reference bindings explicit. Prompt enhancement translates user intent into this caption distribution: basic PE is derived from captions of compelling videos and refined with text-level and generator-in-the-loop reinforcement learning, while agentic PE refines the story and retrieves compelling examples. We show that a reliable text encoder comes from perceptual training rather than from stronger language ability, and that candidates can be screened from text alone before end-to-end training. Reward modeling pairs a general reward model for explainable, localized critiques with a decision reward model for fast multidimensional scoring, treating perceptual bandwidth as a first-class design parameter. Finally, we review task-specific automatic evaluation, focusing on protocols that expose scoring shortcuts and help trace failures to their upstream source. We hope these lessons offer practical guidance for building the understanding stack behind controllable video generation.

</details>


### [101] [Robustness Verification against Patch Attacks via Group-Lasso Semidefinite Relaxation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04939)
*Yukina Nakagawa,Takami Sato,Ken Kobayashi,Kazuhide Nakata*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种针对补丁攻击（patch attack）的神经网络鲁棒性验证方法：将补丁攻击建模为组稀疏约束，把验证问题表述为混合整数半定规划（MISDP），并提出三种消除整数变量的松弛（含Group-Lasso松弛），在保证验证质量的同时大幅降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒性验证方法主要针对范数球（norm-ball）攻击，无法充分刻画具有物理扰动的真实威胁即补丁攻击；而直观的补丁位置枚举方法计算代价过高，难以实用。

Method: 1) 用组稀疏性对补丁攻击建模；2) 对神经网络施加SDP松弛，将验证问题表述为混合整数半定规划（MISDP）；3) 提出三种松弛方法（包括Group-Lasso松弛），在去除整数变量的同时保留原攻击的稀疏组结构，从而实现高效验证。

Result: 所提松弛方法的计算时间不到原MISDP的3%；Group-Lasso松弛使目标值平均仅增加0.34%；Group-Lasso达到91%的验证率，而先前方法不足70%。

Conclusion: 将补丁攻击建模为组稀疏约束并结合MISDP与松弛技术，可在保证验证紧致性的前提下显著提升补丁攻击鲁棒性验证的效率与验证率，优于现有方法。

Abstract: Robustness verification is essential for ensuring the safety and security of neural networks, but current methods focus heavily on
  norm-ball attacks, which do not adequately model typical real-world threats that present physical perturbations, known as patch attacks. A naive approach requires computationally prohibitive enumeration of all possible patch positions. To address the challenge, we propose a novel neural network verification method specialized to the patch-attack threat. Instead of enumerating patch locations, we first model the patch attack with group-wise sparsity and formulate its verification methods as a mixed-integer semidefinite program (MISDP) by applying SDP relaxation to the neural network. We then propose three relaxations, including Group-Lasso relaxation, that remove the integer variables while preserving the sparse, group-wise structure of the original attack, enabling substantially efficient verification. Our empirical analysis demonstrates that the proposed relaxations require less than 3% of the computation time of the original MISDP, while the Group-Lasso relaxation incurs only a 0.34\% average increase in the objective value over the original formulation. Furthermore, Group-Lasso achieves a verified rate of 91%, compared with under 70% for prior methods.

</details>


### [102] [HTNN-MSC: Multi-view Subspace Clustering with a Hyperbolic Tangent Tensor Low-Rank Surrogate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04940)
*Yiwen Dou,Yiqiang Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HTNN-MSC，用有界双曲正切张量秩代理替代t-SVD框架中的张量核范数，以缓解对大奇异值的过度收缩，并给出ADMM求解器与KL收敛性保证；在23个数据集上平均聚类精度0.8404、平均排名2.78，对凸t-SVD-MSC基线为19胜1平3负。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视图子空间聚类中，t-SVD可刻画视图间高阶依赖，但常用的张量核范数对不同幅值奇异值施加相同的渐近边际惩罚，导致编码簇结构的主成分被过度收缩，损害聚类性能。

Method: 以正半轴严格凹、导数Lipschitz连续的有界双曲正切函数作为张量秩代理，其边际惩罚随奇异值增大而递减；设计ADMM求解器，使张量子问题分解为逐标量的频域收缩问题。采用带安全保护的多起点牛顿迭代加候选目标值比较来实现双曲正切阈值算子，形状参数按输入谱尺度缩放。理论上证明增广目标的o-minimal可定义性，并在有界性、下降性、相对误差与精确块条件成立时，借助Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz框架证明全序列收敛到KKT点。

Result: 在23个数据集上，平均聚类精度0.8404，相对九个代表性方法的平均精度排名为2.78；Friedman检验与Holm校正的Wilcoxon比较显示总体差异显著，但若干成对差异不显著。对凸t-SVD-MSC骨架为19胜1平3负；同骨架消融表明该代理并非一致优于其他非凸惩罚。

Conclusion: 有界双曲正切代理可有效缓解核范数的过度收缩并提供收敛性理论支撑，但优势具条件性：相对其他非凸惩罚并不普遍占优，部分成对比较不显著，需结合数据集特性选择惩罚形式。

Abstract: High-order dependencies among views can be modeled by tensor singular value decomposition (t-SVD) in multi-view subspace clustering. However, the tensor nuclear norm often used with this framework assigns the same asymptotic marginal penalty to singular values of different magnitudes, which may excessively shrink dominant components that encode cluster structure. We propose HTNN-MSC, which replaces this norm with a bounded hyperbolic tangent tensor rank surrogate. The surrogate is strictly concave on the positive half-axis and has a Lipschitz continuous derivative. Its marginal penalty decreases as singular values grow. We develop an alternating direction method of multipliers solver in which the tensor subproblem decomposes into scalar frequency-domain shrinkage problems. Safeguarded multi-start Newton iterations and candidate-objective comparison implement the resulting hyperbolic tangent thresholding operator. The shape parameter is scaled by the input spectrum. We also establish o-minimal definability of the augmented objective. Under explicit boundedness, descent, relative-error, and exact-block conditions, the Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz framework guarantees convergence of the full sequence to a KKT point. On 23 datasets, HTNN-MSC achieves a mean clustering accuracy of 0.8404 and an average accuracy rank of 2.78 against nine representative methods. Friedman tests and Holm-adjusted Wilcoxon comparisons reveal significant overall differences, although several pairwise differences remain nonsignificant. HTNN-MSC records 19 wins, one tie, and three losses against the convex t-SVD-MSC skeleton. A same-skeleton ablation also shows that the proposed surrogate does not uniformly outperform alternative nonconvex penalties.

</details>


### [103] [Learning Where to Look: Observation Policy Optimization for Thinking with Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04943)
*Junfeng Wang,Jiawei Liu,Yongchao Xu,Tao Jiang,Jiangbo Ai,Jin Zhang,Chong Wang,Qixing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Observation Policy Optimization (OPO)，针对"thinking with images"中观察动作（裁剪/放大）优化不足的问题，用不确定性引导分支与证据局部化优势归因，将信用精确分配到观察动作token上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂VQA需模型主动放大高分辨率图像的信息区域以获取局部视觉证据。错误分析表明失败多源于错误的观察动作（定位不准或裁剪证据不足）；且bounding-box token生成熵显著高于普通语言token，说明"看哪里"的决策不确定性高。而现有基于最终答案奖励的RL只提供轨迹级反馈，难以对观察动作做精确信用分配。

Method: OPO包含两个模块：(1) Uncertainty-Guided Observation Branching (UOB)：将每次图像放大工具调用视为观察事件，在rollout过程中于高不确定性、低冗余的事件处选择性分支，高效探索不同裁剪决策及其视觉证据；(2) Evidence-Localized Advantage Attribution (ELAA)：利用局部证据充分性奖励比较兄弟分支，仅将信用分配给对应的观察动作token，从而把观察动作优化与轨迹级结果监督解耦。

Result: OPO在不同规模的Qwen3-VL模型上均取得一致提升，并在与更大规模模型及工具增强基线的对比中取得有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 通过解耦观察动作优化与轨迹级结果监督，OPO改善了视觉证据获取能力并进一步提升视觉推理性能，代码已公开。

Abstract: Complex visual question answering often requires vision-language models to think with images, i.e., actively acquire local visual evidence by zooming into informative regions of high-resolution images. Our error analysis shows that many failures in this setting originate from incorrect observation actions, where the model fails to localize key regions or obtain crops that contain sufficient evidence for answering. We further observe that bounding-box tokens exhibit substantially higher generation entropy than ordinary language tokens, indicating high uncertainty when the model decides where to look. However, existing reinforcement learning methods based on final-answer rewards provide only trajectory-level feedback, making it difficult to assign precise credit to the observation actions. We propose Observation Policy Optimization (OPO), an RL algorithm for optimizing observation actions in tool-augmented thinking with images. OPO consists of two core modules. First, Uncertainty-Guided Observation Branching (UOB) treats each image zoom-in tool call as an observation event and selectively branches at high-uncertainty, low-redundancy events during rollout, enabling efficient exploration of alternative cropping decisions and their resulting visual evidence. Second, Evidence-Localized Advantage Attribution (ELAA) compares sibling branches using local evidence-sufficiency rewards and assigns credit only to the corresponding observation-action tokens. By decoupling observation-action optimization from trajectory-level outcome supervision, OPO improves visual evidence acquisition and further enhances visual reasoning performance. Experiments show that OPO consistently improves Qwen3-VL models across scales and achieves competitive performance against larger models and tool-augmented baselines. Code is publicly available.

</details>


### [104] [How Well Does Webcam Eye Tracking Work "in the Wild"? A Large-Scale Validation of Chiasm and a Comparison with WebGazer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04946)
*Artem V. Belopolsky,Florian Perdreau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过N=505的大规模线上研究评估首代Chiasm网络摄像头眼动追踪器，其空间准确度约2.28°、精度约1.00°(SD)，并在同协议对比中优于WebGazer的准确度与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络摄像头眼动追踪可在线触达大规模多样样本，极大加速基础与应用研究的数据采集，但其空间性能被认为远低于硬件系统，限制了应用。作者旨在系统评估首代Chiasm追踪器在真实多样条件下的实际表现，并检验其是否缩小了与硬件设备的差距。

Method: 开展N=505的大规模线上研究，覆盖多样参与者、设备与录制条件；测量空间准确度、空间精度(SD)、样本间精度(RMS-S2S)，并采用3 cm校准标准筛选(91%参与者达标)；设置诱发头部运动的三分钟任务以考察漂移；分析性别、年龄、地区、面部亮度、Michelson对比度、眼纵横比、戴眼镜等因素与准确度的关系；并以相同协议与开源WebGazer进行随机子样本对比(N=101 vs N=101)。

Result: 平均空间准确度2.39 cm(60 cm处≈2.28°)，空间精度(SD)1.05 cm(≈1.00°)，RMS-S2S 1.29 cm(≈1.23°)；达校准标准者准确度2.15 cm(≈2.05°)。头动任务后准确度下降0.59 cm，但注视中心漂移仅0.37 cm。男性验证块偏移比女性大0.30 cm；年龄、地区、面部亮度、Michelson对比度无稳定关联；眼纵横比越高(眼睛越开)偏移越小；戴眼镜在每个验证块均降低准确度。与WebGazer对比：Chiasm准确度更优(2.18 vs 4.51 cm)、精度更优(1.01 vs 1.55 cm)，但RMS-S2S更差(1.26 vs 0.79 cm)，作者认为后者反映的是保守过度平滑的估计而非更优稳定性。

Conclusion: Chiasm在真实多样线上条件下达到接近硬件的可用空间性能，正缩小网络摄像头与硬件眼动追踪之间的差距，使"野外"大规模注视研究成为现实；样本间精度指标的解释需谨慎，不应仅凭RMS-S2S判定稳定性优劣。

Abstract: Webcam eye tracking can greatly accelerate data collection in basic and applied research by reaching large, diverse samples of participants online. However, its spatial performance is considered well below that of hardware-based systems, limiting its use. We evaluated the first-generation Chiasm webcam eye tracker in a large online study ($N = 505$) spanning diverse participants, devices, and recording conditions.
